        Take the train from Dorgesh-Kaan to Keldagrim for the diary; the wiki lists the ride as a hard Lumbridge & Draynor task, and it is free once Another Slice of H.A.M. is done. Walk to Dondakan at Dondakan's Rock and use a gold bar (or gold ore) on him to progress Between a Rock.... Leave Keldagrim with the ferry or a house tablet.

        Done whenDondakan has the gold bar.

        Coins: about 320,000 gp · Slots: about 16/28 · Requirements: Death to the Dorgeshuun, The Giant Dwarf, The Dig Site, 15 Attack and 25 Prayer for Another Slice of H.A.M.; 43 quest points, 49 Firemaking, 20 Crafting and 20 Mining for Tears of Guthix

        Wiki referencesBullseye lantern (empty)Sapphire lanternChest (Dorgesh-Kaan Average)Chest (Dorgesh-Kaan Rich)LockpickAnother Slice of H.A.M.Tears of Guthix (quest)

        Varrock, Temple of the Eye, the first Tears of Guthix and the Al Kharid furnace

        A short loop between Al Kharid, Varrock, the Abyss and the Wizards' Tower. You do Temple of the Eye from its start, play the first Tears of Guthix and make the furnace items the rest of 2.1 needs, so that the desert block starts with nothing left to make.

        Gate inEnter the Abyss, Rune Mysteries and 10 Runecraft for Temple of the Eye; 16 Crafting and 49 Magic for the topaz rings; 18 Crafting for the silver sickle; the jade, red topaz, silver and gold bars and the ammo mould.
        Carry inthe Barcrawl card, a bucket, runes for Lvl-2 and Lvl-3 Enchant and a pickaxe for the silver: about 19/28.
        ExitTemple of the Eye and The Birds and the Wasps done; the first Tears of Guthix played; the golden cannonball, the necklace of passage, two Efaritay's aid and the silver sickle made; Varrock easy and medium lamps on Herblore; a Shantay pass; about 315,000 gp.
        Carries forwardVarrock armour 2 (sandstone), the steel platelegs (Ivan Strom), the golden cannonball (Between a Rock...), the necklace of passage (Spirits of the Elid, Making History), the second Efaritay's aid (Sins of the Father), the silver sickle (the Nature Grotto), two silver bars (the silver dust and the silvthrill rod).

        Varrock diary steps and Temple of the Eye

        Start in Al Kharid: take a ring of dueling to Emir's Arena, walk into town and talk to Wizard Persten, west of the Emir's Arena, to start Temple of the Eye. She gives you the eye amulet; Chapter 1 no longer starts the quest, so the amulet never rode in your bag.

        1. Teleport to Varrock. Ask the Apothecary to make you a strength potion for the Varrock diary (a limpwurt root, red spiders' eggs and 5 gp), then collect the Varrock easy and medium rewards and put both lamps on Herblore.
        2. Keep Varrock armour 2 in a slot: you wear it instead of the initiate hauberk while mining sandstone in the next block.

        Before the temple, walk north-east out of Varrock to the Jolly Boar Inn. Buy its drink for Alfred Grimhand's Barcrawl: it is the card's last stamp, and you hand the card in at the Barbarian Outpost before you start Scorpion Catcher, a few blocks on. Johnathon, the third Family Crest brother, is upstairs, but leave him for the Family Crest block, after Caleb and Avan.

        For Temple of the Eye, buy a strong cup of tea from the Tea Seller at the eastern entrance of Varrock and fill a bucket with water. Use the eye amulet on the Mage of Zamorak in the Zamorakian chapel in the south-east of the city and he asks for the bucket and the tea. Bring both back and use the tea on him (the wiki gives the mage's real name as Herbert); his one-time teleport takes you to the Dark Mage in the Abyss: wash him with the water, use the amulet on him and click each rune energy until it turns white to work out the order. The order is different for each player, so write each energy down as you go. You get the abyssal incantation. Leaving through the fire rift puts you next to Persten north of Al Kharid, so you need no teleport for that leg.

        1. Report to Persten, who offers a one-time teleport to the Wizards' Tower, then use the incantation on Archmage Sedridor in the basement. He points you to Wizard Traiborn on the first floor.
        2. Talk to Wizard Traiborn's three apprentices (Cordelia, Felix and Tamara) for the puzzle clues; the wiki gives the answer as 11, which you give to Traiborn.
        3. Then enter the portal with Sedridor for the Guardians of the Rift tutorial. The wiki warns that anything you drop during the tutorial is lost when you go to the altars, because the instance refreshes. For a UIM that means drop nothing in there.
        4. Report to Sedridor to finish.
        Rewards5,000 Runecraft XP on top of about 4,200 earned during the quest, access to Guardians of the Rift and a medium pouch if you have none.
        Done whenthe Varrock easy and medium lamps are on Herblore and Temple of the Eye is complete.

        The Birds and the Wasps

        From Varrock, walk to Farmer Bea on the ridge just east of the south-east Varrock mine, inside the Digsite, and do The Birds and the Wasps, a Quest Month quest with no skill or quest requirements. Every item it uses (insect repellent, a beekeeper's or bee hunter's hat, an insect net and bird snares) comes during the quest, and one leg sends you to the Bee dealer just inside the eastern entrance of the Grand Exchange. Nothing has to be killed, but carrying the box of bees back to Bea you must shake it at the right moments: a wrong or missed shake damages you, and that damage can kill you, so keep your Hitpoints high and food in the inventory for that walk, because a death here leaves a deathpile in the open. Drop the quest items afterwards.

        Rewards1 quest point, 1,000 Hunter XP and 250 Fletching XP, and it unlocks wasp and honey buzzard hunting and wasp darts.
        Done whenThe Birds and the Wasps is complete.

        The first Tears of Guthix

        Teleport with a games necklace to Tears of Guthix and play the minigame for the first time. From now on, visit it once a week whenever you are near Lumbridge. The XP goes to your lowest-XP skill. Check which skill that is before you play: Runecraft would be useful (The Eyes of Glouphrie in Chapter 1 added 6,000 Runecraft XP and Temple of the Eye adds about 9,200 more, so you should be on about 31 already and clear The Slug Menace's 30; What Lies Below needs 35, so check the real level). At some point you need to collect at least 100 tears in one visit for the diary.

        Done whenthe first Tears of Guthix game is played.

        The Al Kharid furnace

        Take a ring of dueling to Emir's Arena, walk into Al Kharid and do everything here in one visit:

        • buy one pair of steel platelegs at Louie's Armoured Legs. In Aid of the Myreque, later in this section, gives a steel med helm, chainbody and platelegs to Ivan Strom. Buy only one pair, because the wiki's item list needs only one;
        • at the furnace, make the golden cannonball for Between a Rock... from a gold bar and the ammo mould from Nulodion;
        • make a jade necklace (a silver bar, the jade and a necklace mould) and enchant it into a necklace of passage with Lvl-2 Enchant (27 Magic, a cosmic rune and 3 air runes), then make two topaz rings (a silver bar, a red topaz and a ring mould each, from the silver ore you mined next door; Dommik's Crafting Store sells moulds if you have none) and enchant those into Efaritay's aid with Lvl-3 Enchant (49 Magic, plus a cosmic rune and 5 fire runes each). The wiki gives each ring 200 charges, used only on hits against vampyres, and the second is a spare for Sins of the Father. Sell the moulds back afterwards;
        • cast a silver sickle from a fourth silver bar with a sickle mould (Dommik sells one for 10 gp; 18 Crafting). Chapter 1 dropped its sickle after Nature Spirit, and In Search of the Myreque needs a blessed one, which you bless at the Nature Grotto on the way in. Keep the last two silver bars for the silver dust and the silvthrill rod.
        Done whenyou hold the steel platelegs, the golden cannonball, the necklace of passage, two Efaritay's aid, the silver sickle and two silver bars.

        Coins: about 315,000 gp · Slots: about 19/28 arriving, about 22/28 at the furnace · Requirements: Enter the Abyss, Rune Mysteries and 10 Runecraft for Temple of the Eye; 16 Crafting for topaz rings; 18 Crafting for the sickle; 49 Magic for Lvl-3 Enchant

        Wiki referencesTemple of the EyeTears of GuthixEfaritay's aidVarrock DiaryTemple of the Eye/Quick guideDommik's Crafting Store

        Sandstone to the edge of 60 Mining, Enakhra's Lament and the ancient staff

        The big Mining block of 2.1. Take Ferryman Sathwood's free ferry from just south of the Al Kharid bank to the Ruins of Unkah (go through the eastern Shantay Pass at the ruins, as the wiki's route to the quarry does, with the Shantay pass you carry), walk north to the Desert Quarry and mine sandstone there until you are about 10,000 XP short of level 60 Mining, where Enakhra's Lament follows if your Crafting is 50. Buy the ancient staff from Eblis on the way out. Getting past 50 Mining here is also what opens the Ice Queen's rock slide, so the ice gloves follow this block.

        Gate in35 Mining; a Shantay pass; 50 Crafting for Enakhra's Lament (otherwise it waits for the glassblowing); Desert Treasure I and 80,000 gp for the ancient staff, with 50 Attack and 50 Magic to wield it.
        Carry ina pickaxe, Varrock armour 2, waterskins or desert robes and some wines; for the quest, its item list (about 22/28).
        ExitMining about 10,000 XP short of 60; Enakhra's Lament done or deferred; the ancient staff bought; about 230,000 gp; about 18/28.
        Carries forwardthe camulet if you did the quest.

        Mine and drop sandstone at the Desert Quarry

        Mine sandstone in the quarry south of the Bandit Camp (35 Mining; the wiki notes 3-tick mining, 3t4s, as a faster but intensive option; the wiki lists 36 sandstone rocks there, each worth 30 to 60 XP); the wiki's Ruins of Unkah mine page lists no sandstone. You need to buy no sand for this. For a UIM it is even simpler: drop every sandstone as you go, so the method costs no storage and your inventory size doesn't matter. Wear Varrock armour 2 instead of the initiate hauberk, carry waterskins or wear desert robes against the heat, and keep some wines for the stretch. The heat hurts you while you walk the desert or stay in the quarry unless you carry waterskins or wear heat-proof gear, and finishing Enakhra's Lament unlocks a fountain in the quarry to refill them. Don't buy a kebab in Al Kharid before this ferry: no item list uses one, and the wiki gives the kebab no quest or diary use (Forgettable Tale..., its only quest, is done in Chapter 1).

        From about 43 Mining, stopping 10,000 XP short of 60 is roughly 210,000 Mining XP. Feel free to take the level instead if you prefer; Enakhra's Lament gives another 7,000 Mining XP either way.

        Done whenyou are about 10,000 XP short of 60 Mining.

        Enakhra's Lament

        If you are still below 50 Crafting, skip this quest for now and do it right after the glassblowing at the end of 2.1; you mine the granite then. Otherwise walk to Lazim, north-east of the Desert Quarry, and do Enakhra's Lament. The wiki lists:

        • a chisel, a pickaxe, a tinderbox and a lit candle (not a black candle);
        • one bread, pie, cake, chocolate cake, pizza or topped baked potato (not partial or burnt);
        • normal, oak, willow and maple logs, one each, for the braziers;
        • coal, which you can mine in the quarry at 30 Mining;
        • soft clay, or mine clay in the quarry and wet it with a bucket of water from the Bandit Camp general store to the north;
        • 52 kg of sandstone (32 kg for the base, then 20 kg for the body), which you mine in the quarry next to Lazim;
        • two 5 kg pieces of granite (45 Mining). King's Ransom in 2.2 needs a third piece of any size, as the wiki's Ironman note says, but don't carry it that long: the King's Ransom step mines it fresh with a camulet teleport;
        • runes for Fire Bolt or better and Wind Bolt or better, plus several Crumble Undead casts in case you splash, all on the standard spellbook;
        • enough Prayer points to keep Protect from Melee up for about five seconds.

        An elemental staff saves slots, so wield an air staff and carry fire runes. Light the candle before you enter the shadow room, or the darkness damages you. Use your food on Pentyn with the use option, not the eat one, and don't offer food that is half eaten. Turn on Protect from Melee before you talk to the second Boneguard, and bury the five big bones it leaves. If you teleport away mid-quest, re-enter by the south-western secret entrance. The second piece of granite is for a later head, and picking the wrong head for the statue costs granite, though Lazim lets you change it.

        Rewards7,000 XP each in Crafting, Mining, Firemaking and Magic, and the camulet, which talks to camels and teleports to the temple four times; camel dung recharges it.
        Done whenEnakhra's Lament is complete, or you have set it aside for after the glassblowing.

        Eblis, jackals and vultures

        Eblis stands south-east of the Bandit Camp. Buy the ancient staff from him: the wiki gives a one-time price of 80,000 coins after Desert Treasure I, and it needs 50 Attack and 50 Magic to wield. Its +50 melee strength makes it a good melee weapon for Slayer later in this section. The wiki advises ironmen not to alch or drop it, because a master clue step uses it and the only other sources are rare boss drops and a very rare mummy drop. Kill jackals and vultures in the desert for Rag and Bone Man II and the desert diary on your way to Nardah.

        Done whenthe ancient staff is bought and the desert bones are collected.

        Coins: about 230,000 gp · Slots: about 22/28 for the quest, 18/28 after · Requirements: 50 Crafting (you are on about 46, so unless you have trained since Chapter 1, skip Enakhra's Lament here and do it right after the glassblowing at the end of 2.1), 43 Prayer, 39 Magic, 45 Mining for granite and 45 Woodcutting or another source of maple logs for Enakhra's Lament; Desert Treasure I for the ancient staff

        Wiki referencesSandstoneEnakhra's LamentEnakhra's Lament/Quick guideAncient staffDesert Diary

        Nardah: the desert diary tasks and Spirits of the Elid

        Nardah is the base for one quest and a cluster of desert diary tasks. Do them in one pass so you only cross the desert once.

        Gate in33 Magic, 37 Ranged, 37 Mining and 37 Thieving (boostable) for Spirits of the Elid; the standard spellbook; 40 Defence for rune platelegs; the grimy harralander; 60,800 gp for the platelegs.
        Carry inthe stage items for each part of the quest, the ancient staff and rune sword for the golems, the necklace of passage and enough food not to die: about 22/28.
        ExitSpirits of the Elid and the Nardah diary tasks done; the combat potion made; the rune platelegs carried; about 165,000 gp.
        Carries forwardthe rune platelegs (worn after the Altar of Guthix), the waterskin, the orange salamander task (after 47 Hunter in 2.2).

        Nardah on arrival

        On the way in, kill a goat north-west of Nardah for its horn and grind it to goat horn dust. In town:

        • leave the mayor's balcony for later: burning a yew log there is a Desert hard diary task, which Chapter 3 claims;
        • have Zahur clean your grimy harralander for the diary: it costs 200 gp;
        • buy rune platelegs (or a plateskirt) at Seddu's Adventurer's Store for 60,800 coins. They are your legs from the Altar of Guthix onwards, including Dagannoth Rex later in 2.1. Carry them until the initiate armour has done its job at the altar, then wear them.
        Done whenthe goat horn is ground, the harralander is cleaned and the rune platelegs are bought.

        Spirits of the Elid

        Talk to Awusah the Mayor to start Spirits of the Elid. You must be on the standard spellbook. The wiki splits the items by stage:

        • start: Telekinetic Grab runes for the ancestral key, a needle and one or two thread to repair the robes of Elidinis. The costume needle does not work for this quest. The robes are the two torn pieces in the cupboards of the building with Shiratti the Custodian, and the ancestral key comes from casting Telekinetic Grab at the enclosed area. Ghaslor the Elder, in the house just north of the mayor's, gives you a ballad; the wiki says not to drop it, because you need it at the golem door and to finish;
        • golems: the ancestral key, the ballad, the robes of Elidinis (worn at the dungeon door), a rope for the root above the waterfall, a pickaxe, a ranged weapon with ammo, and a crush, a stab and a slash weapon. The ancient staff covers crush and the rune sword covers stab and slash, so you need no extra weapons;
        • genie: a light source, a knife and a rope. The knife is for the mayor's shoes, which lie beside his door and need their soles cut off for the genie; if you skip the soles you need a second rope to climb back out of the crevice west of the general store.

        The three golems are level 75 and each resists two attack styles: black is weak to crush, grey to slash and white to stab. Don't open a golem's door before its death animation ends, or another one spawns. Use your necklace of passage to Eagles' Eyrie to save some of the walk to the source of the Elid. Unnoted food doesn't survive a deathpile, so bring enough food to make sure you don't die here.

        Rewards2 quest points, 8,000 Prayer, 1,000 Thieving and 1,000 Magic XP.

        The wiki says the Visit the Genie task is ticked automatically when you reach the genie during the quest (it needs a rope and a light source, which you carry anyway), so after the quest only the statuette task is left: pray at the statuette in the Nardah temple with at least one Prayer point missing, or the task does not count. The wiki says the statuette also restores Hitpoints, Prayer, run energy and special attack and cures poison, venom and bleeding, so it is a free top-up whenever you pass through Nardah. On the run back south to Nardah:

        • catch an orange salamander (47 Hunter, with a small fishing net and a rope) if your Hunter is there; you left Chapter 1 at 46, so leave it until Hunter reaches 47 in 2.2 (the blue crabs pass it) and catch it on your next desert visit;
        • make a combat potion from the harralander potion (unf) and the goat horn dust (36 Herblore);
        • cut open a cactus to fill a waterskin.
        Done whenSpirits of the Elid is complete, the follow-up diary task is done and the combat potion is made.

        A money check before Sophanem

        After the ancient staff and the platelegs your stack is about 165,000 gp, because Chapter 1 ended about 150,000 gp above the 1.27 million this chapter was first planned on. That covers the roughly 50,000 gp of small purchases up to the Yanille stop (Olaf's Quest pays 20,000 gp back), so this block no longer needs the hour of blackjacking it used to (a quarter hour of it now sits in the Yanille top-up): only if your stack is below about 100,000 gp here, take the carpet to Pollnivneach and blackjack Menaphite thugs, as in Chapter 1, at roughly 200,000 gp an hour. The big top-up waits until the Yanille block, which spends about 835,000 gp.

        Done whenyou hold about 165,000 gp.

        Coins: about 165,000 gp · Slots: about 22/28 for the golems · Requirements: 33 Magic, 37 Ranged, 37 Mining and 37 Thieving (boostable) for Spirits of the Elid; 47 Hunter for orange salamanders; 36 Herblore for the combat potion; 40 Defence for rune platelegs

        Wiki referencesSpirits of the ElidSpirits of the Elid/Quick guideDesert DiaryNecklace of passageBlackjacking

        Sophanem: Icthlarin's Little Helper, Pyramid Plunder and the start of Contact!

        This block needs your cat, so it starts with a trip home.

        Gate inGertrude's Cat and the cat; Prince Ali Rescue for Contact!; 31 Prayer and the fully stamped Barcrawl card for Scorpion Catcher (the wiki marks a completed Alfred Grimhand's Barcrawl as required to start, and you hand the card in during this block); a full waterskin.
        Carry inthe cat and the quest's item list, a light source, antipoisons and food for a level 91 fight: about 21/28. Treat it as a no-death block.
        ExitIcthlarin's Little Helper and the Pyramid Plunder diary tasks done; Contact! stopped at Osman; Alfred Grimhand's Barcrawl complete and Scorpion Catcher started; the cat back in the menagerie; about 165,000 gp.
        Carries forwardthe catspeak amulet (A Tail of Two Cats), the scorpion cage (Taverley Dungeon, Edgeville Monastery, Barbarian Outpost).

        Fetch the cat

        1. Break a house tablet, pick up your cat from the pet house in the menagerie and come back to the desert. Any of the regular cat, overgrown cat, hellcat or kitten works, and it needs no food.
        2. Use a Nardah teleport scroll, fairy ring DLQ or the Pollnivneach carpet (then walk south to Nardah), whichever is closer to where you left off, and find the Wanderer in the camp west of the Agility Pyramid, where Icthlarin's Little Helper starts. The cat matters here: the wiki says you lose it if you die during a flashback or to the guardian, or if you answer the Sphinx's riddle wrongly, and you then need a new one from Gertrude. Treat this as a no-death block. A death in the pyramid leaves your pile where you died, but you respawn in Falador, a long way off, with the 60 minutes running.
        Done whenthe cat is with you at the Wanderer's camp.

        Icthlarin's Little Helper

        Icthlarin's Little Helper needs Gertrude's Cat and the ability to beat a level 91 enemy. The wiki lists:

        • the cat;
        • a tinderbox and a willow log (cut one from a willow tree at 30 Woodcutting);
        • a bag of salt, or an empty bucket to make a pile of salt in Sophanem (the wiki also lets you collect a bucket of salt water at the pond west of the Wanderer);
        • a bucket of sap (use a knife on an evergreen or pointy common tree, such as the one outside Lumbridge Castle, with an empty bucket in your inventory);
        • a full waterskin to give away (cut a cactus to fill it, as in the diary task);
        • one linen, which Raetul in Sophanem sells for 30 coins, but only once you have spoken to the Embalmer. Hand the Embalmer the sap, linen and salt first, then collect the holy symbol from the Carpenter right away, which skips a wait.

        Bring a light source and one or two antipoisons and enough food for the Possessed Priest (level 91) and one guardian picked by your stats: Apmeken (75, Magic), Scabaras (75, Melee) or Het (81, Melee); Crondis is only available to players who started the quest before April 2022. The jar you pick up decides which one: an ape-lid jar means Apmeken, a bug-lid jar Scabaras and a man-lid jar Het, so take it before the pit jump, which needs 20% run energy; if you forget it, leave the pyramid and re-enter. The Sphinx's riddle has the answer 9, and the cat shakes its head if you are about to pick a wrong one. The pyramid also has level 84 mummies, which you can walk past, and level 96 scarab swarms, which stop you in place even with auto-retaliate on. The wiki says Protect from Magic is enough for the Possessed Priest.

        Rewards2 quest points, 4,500 Thieving, 4,000 Agility and 4,000 Woodcutting XP, the catspeak amulet, access to Sophanem and carpet rides from Pollnivneach to Sophanem and Menaphos.
        Done whenIcthlarin's Little Helper is complete and the cat is alive.

        Pyramid Plunder for the diary

        Right after the quest, enter Pyramid Plunder for the desert diary. The pyramid is in Sophanem, behind one of four anonymous doors; only one door is correct, it changes every 25 minutes, and a failed attempt stuns you and deals 1 to 4 damage. Both easy tasks need Icthlarin's Little Helper started, and the sarcophagus task needs 21 Thieving. Open the sarcophagus in the first room (it uses Strength, not Thieving) for the first easy task, and keep one artefact from the pyramid to sell to Simon Templeton, at the Agility Pyramid just north-east of Sophanem, for the other. You don't need to stay longer than that.

        Done whenboth Pyramid Plunder easy tasks are done.

        Start Contact! and Scorpion Catcher

        Start Contact! with the High Priest in the Great Temple in the south-west of Sophanem and continue until it sends you to Osman in Al Kharid, outside the palace; stop there. The route goes through Jex, in the temple at the north-eastern corner of the city, then down the trapdoor into the Sophanem Dungeon, to Kaleef's corpse and to Maisa across a chasm. Turn auto-retaliate off in there, because retaliating at the wrong moment can set off a trap, and use Evade on floor traps and Disable on wall traps. A scarab trap puts your light out and drops you to the bottom level, and the wrong ladder leads to a much more dangerous multicombat level. Carry teleport jewellery for the way out.

        Then leave the desert. The wiki lists a completed Alfred Grimhand's Barcrawl as required to start Scorpion Catcher, so first rub your games necklace for the Barbarian Outpost (or use the Barbarian Assault minigame teleport if the necklace is low on charges) and show the signed card to the barbarian guard by the agility course entrance; any guard there takes it once every line is signed, and a lost card means redoing the whole miniquest. That frees the card's slot. Then go on to Kandarin and talk to Thormac, on the top floor of the Sorcerer's Tower south of Seers' Village, to start Scorpion Catcher (fairy ring BLR is nearby, or walk down from Camelot), and take the scorpion cage he gives you. Teleport to Seers' Village and speak with a seer to continue the quest: the wiki says the secret room in Taverley Dungeon will not open until you have done so. The three scorpions are in Taverley Dungeon, upstairs in the Edgeville Monastery and in the Barbarian Outpost; collect them as you pass each place, and the wiki notes that scorpions respawn after being caught if one goes wrong. Take the cat home again before the next block: the Taverley Dungeon multiquest later doesn't need it, and the menagerie keeps it safe from deaths.

        Done whenContact! waits at Osman, the seer has been spoken to and the cat is home.

        Coins: about 165,000 gp · Slots: about 21/28 with the cat · Requirements: Gertrude's Cat for Icthlarin's Little Helper; Prince Ali Rescue and Icthlarin's Little Helper for Contact!; 31 Prayer and a completed Alfred Grimhand's Barcrawl for Scorpion Catcher

        Wiki referencesIcthlarin's Little HelperPyramid PlunderContact!Scorpion Catcher

        The ice gloves and the Entrana fire feather

        These are two Heroes' Quest steps that wait until here. With more than 50 Mining they fit here, between Seers' Village and Taverley. The order is fixed by the feather: it is too hot to pick up without ice gloves, so the Ice Queen comes before Entrana, and the celestial ring comes last because the Entrana loop ends in Falador, where the Mining Guild is. You don't hand the feather in yet: Achietties wants it together with the cooked lava eel and the armband, so it stays in your inventory until the Heroes' Quest block that ends in Burthorpe.

        Gate in50 Mining (boostable) and a pickaxe; Heroes' Quest started; respawn in Falador; 59 Mining or more and 2,000 stardust for the celestial ring.
        Carry inIce Queen fight gear and food (about 18/28); for Entrana, only the ice gloves and a Falador teleport (2/28).
        Exitthe ice gloves and the fire feather; the celestial ring; the Falador pile collected; about 165,000 gp.
        Carries forwardthe ice gloves (the rest of the game), the fire feather (Heroes' Quest).

        The Ice Queen

        Requirementsa pickaxe and 50 Mining, boostable, and that no one else can mine it for you.

        Walk from Catherby to the north side of White Wolf Mountain (or take the gnome glider to Sindarpos) and mine the rock slide north-west of the glider. At 59 Mining or more you need no boost for the slide. Past it, the lair holds level 53 ice giants, level 57 ice warriors and level 61 ice spiders, which is why the wiki's route below is worth following exactly. The wiki's route:

        1. Past the rock slide, go down the southernmost ladder.
        2. Take the south-west tunnel and climb the ladder at the end, killing ice giants for Rag and Bone Man II on that floor. The bone is called ice giant ribs, and each kill has a one in four chance of dropping it.
        3. Avoiding the ice giants, go down the easternmost ladder.
        4. Go north through the long tunnel and climb the ladder at the end.
        5. Climb down the ladder next to it.
        6. Follow the tunnel north into the room with the Ice Queen.

        The Ice Queen is level 111, and Protect from Melee makes the fight simple. She has 104 Hitpoints, hits with crush for up to 10 every 2.4 seconds and doesn't attack on sight, so you start the fight yourself. The wiki marks the clusters of stalagmites in the maze as good places to stop and eat: they lie outside her wander range and that of her minions. She always drops ice gloves, and you can kill her again for another pair if you ever lose them. She respawns in about 30 seconds, so a replacement pair costs one more fight, and a spare pair would cost you a slot for the rest of the game. If you do die down here, you respawn in Falador with a pile under the mountain and an hour to walk back. The hour counts only logged-in time, but the way back is the whole walk from Catherby and the maze again, so eat at the stalagmites rather than gamble on a second trip. Keep the gloves for the rest of the game: Blast Furnace and Giants' Foundry use them too, and so does the Flambeed fight in Recipe for Disaster, where the wiki lists them for wielding weapons against him.

        Done whenyou wear the ice gloves.

        Entrana with a Falador deathpile

        Entrana bans weapons and armour, and the wiki's allowed list includes ice gloves, so keep only the gloves. The monks on the Port Sarim dock search you before the boat sails: the wiki says most equippable weapons and most armour with attack or defence bonuses are banned, while runes, ammunition other than darts and plain clothing are fine. A UIM can't bank the rest, so use the Falador respawn you set during Recruitment Drive:

        1. Teleport to Falador. In the Falador Castle courtyard, take off everything and die to a White Knight, as in Recruitment Drive. You respawn in Falador with nothing, and your hour of logged-in time starts the moment you die.
        2. Walk back to the pile and pick up only the ice gloves, 300 coins and a Falador teleport if you have runes for one. The death destroyed the Chronicle, and the coins buy it back on the way home. Everything else stays in the pile, which is hidden from other players and lasts 60 minutes of logged-in time.
        3. Walk to Port Sarim and take the monks' boat to Entrana. The search happens before you sail, so check your worn items and inventory once more: you should hold only the gloves, the coins and the teleport.
        4. Kill the firebird (level 2) on the northern half of the island, near the Law Altar, and pick up the fire feather with the ice gloves on. The bird never attacks first, hits for 1 at most and always drops the feather, so bare hands are enough. Wear the gloves before the kill, since the wiki says you can't pick the feather up without them; the bird respawns in about 21 seconds if you need a second one. Equip nothing else on the island: the wiki says a monk knocks you out and wakes you on the Port Sarim ferry if you log in on Entrana, or leave the Law Altar, in banned gear.
        5. Take the boat back to Port Sarim and walk to Falador, then buy the Chronicle back from Perdu upstairs in the Falador general store (500 gp; its teleport charges come back with it). The whole loop is well under the hour. Pick up the rest of your pile and re-equip. Keep the feather in your inventory rather than in a pile, as the chapter's carry table says.
        6. Buy the celestial ring from Dusuri in the Mining Guild with 2,000 stardust. The guild's above-ground entrance is in the south-east of Falador, guarded by a dwarf who turns away anyone under 60 Mining; Falador Teleport lands in the town square just north-west of it. Chapter 1 mined shooting stars toward it; if your stack is short, mine stars until it isn't (the wiki says each mining action gives one or two). The guild needs 60 Mining, boostable; the sandstone block left you about 10,000 XP short (still 59 after Enakhra's Lament), so drink a dwarven stout from the Rising Sun Inn for the +1 first. The ring then adds an invisible +4 Mining boost; take it off at Motherlode Mine in 2.2, as that block says.
        Done whenyou hold the fire feather, the whole pile is back on you and you wear the celestial ring.

        Coins: about 165,000 gp · Slots: about 18/28 for the Ice Queen, 2/28 on Entrana · Requirements: 50 Mining (boostable) and a pickaxe for the rock slide; Heroes' Quest started for the fire feather

        Wiki referencesIce Queen's LairIce QueenIce glovesHeroes' QuestEntrana

        Taverley: the Altar of Guthix and the Taverley Dungeon multiquest

        You come back from Entrana into Falador still in full initiate, which is exactly what the first stop needs. Then one trip through Taverley Dungeon advances four quests: Wanted!, Heroes' Quest, Scorpion Catcher and Bear Your Soul. They follow the dungeon's layout: the Black Knights' base is in the first half, before the dusty key's gate, and the lava eel, the scorpion and Cerberus' Lair are past it, so you walk in once and out once. Many monsters down there are poisonous, so don't let your Hitpoints run low waiting for a poison to wear off. A Tail of Two Cats starts after Olaf's Quest, because the cat lives in your Rellekka menagerie, next to Olaf, and a death in Taverley Dungeon is safer without it.

        Gate infull initiate worn; 53 Fishing; Bear Your Soul started; the oily fishing rod, bait, scorpion cage, commorb and damaged soul bearer; 40 Defence for the rune platelegs.
        Carry inthe dungeon list in the first step, the anti-dragon shield, the rune sword, food and prayer potions: about 23/28.
        ExitFalador diary altar task done and initiate dropped; Wanted! sent on to Varrock; Bear Your Soul done; the raw lava eel and the Taverley scorpion; about 165,000 gp.
        Carries forwardthe raw lava eel (cooked in Edgeville), the scorpion cage, the soul bearer, a spare dusty key if you took one.

        The Altar of Guthix in full initiate

        Walk to the stone circle in Taverley and pray at the Altar of Guthix while wearing full initiate, for the medium Falador diary. The task says full Initiate armour, so check that every piece is on before you click. That was the set's last job: put on the rune platelegs and drop the initiate pieces, which frees the slots before the dungeon.

        Carry into the dungeon: the oily fishing rod and the fishing bait, the scorpion cage, the commorb, the damaged soul bearer from Chapter 1, an anti-dragon shield, the rune sword, food and prayer potions, and your usual teleports. The commorb is for Wanted!; the wiki says Sir Tiffy lends you his if you lose it.

        Done whenthe altar task is done, the rune platelegs are worn and the initiate pieces are gone.

        The Black Knights' base: Wanted! and the dusty key

        The wiki's route to the Black Knights goes north, then east, then south past the magic axes, poison scorpions and chaos dwarves (don't cross the bridge), then past the hill giants into the Black Knights' rooms. Black Knights are aggressive below 67 combat. The base is in the first half of the dungeon, so it needs no dusty key. If a crowd gathers, the wiki says you can hop worlds from the north-western corner of their room.

        For Wanted!, talk to Lord Daquarius in the south-west room first. Then kill a level 33 Black Knight in that room without leaving or being interrupted, so let his dialogue run to the end first; a kill made before talking to him, or after leaving the room, doesn't count. Daquarius then tells you Solus is somewhere with lots of fur, and Wanted! continues in Varrock. His hint is that Solus is among a lot of fur that doesn't come from bears; the Mage of Zamorak's tip, in the Varrock block after Kourend, turns that into Canifis.

        In the same base, kill the level 47 jailer, by the jail in the far right of the base's second area, for the key to Velrak's cell, free Velrak, who is in the southern cell, and ask him "So... do you know anywhere good to explore?" for the dusty key. You can get more than one, so take a second key while you are here if a slot is free. That matters for a UIM: if you die past the gate, the key is in your pile on the far side, and Velrak can give you another to get back to it. The wiki also notes a spare key spawns near the lava eel fishing spots, and 70 Agility gives a pipe past the gate, which is why the requirements line offers either.

        Done whenDaquarius has pointed you to Varrock and you hold a dusty key.

        Past the dusty key gate

        Use the dusty key on the gate between the lesser demons and the blue dragons. Left-click the gate with the key in your pack. Beyond it the dungeon gets harder, with Cerberus (level 318) deep in the second half. On the far side:

        • fish a lava eel with the oily fishing rod and bait for Heroes' Quest (53 Fishing). Don't cook it yet; you cook it in Edgeville later in 2.1, and until then keep it on you rather than in a deathpile: the wiki doesn't say whether raw fish survives one, so this guide never relies on it. Guard the rod as well: the wiki warns that a death while holding it will probably cost you the rod, and as Gerrant may not give more blamish snail slime, the fallback is a Myre blamish snail's meat ground into an empty sample bottle, sold in the Canifis general store. Lava eels don't burn when cooked, so cooking it later in Edgeville risks nothing;
        • catch the scorpion for Scorpion Catcher with the cage. The wiki puts it in a small room behind a false wall, south of two coffins: pass south of the blue dragons, go west past the black demons, then north to the poison spiders and search the wall. It only opens for a player who has spoken to a seer, which you did when you started the quest. Picking the scorpion up stings for 3 Hitpoints, and caught scorpions respawn, so a lost one can be caught again;
        • kill baby blue dragons for Rag and Bone Man II and keep one set of babydragon bones; each kill has a one in four chance of dropping one. The anti-dragon shield is for the adult blue dragons on the way.
        Done whenyou hold the raw lava eel, the scorpion and the babydragon bones.

        Cerberus' Lair: Bear Your Soul

        Carry on to Cerberus' Lair. The Key Master is inside the lair, whose entrance is a cave in the east wall of the hellhound area. The wiki says entering the lair no longer needs a hellhound Slayer task, so you can talk to him without one. Give him the damaged soul bearer and he repairs it into a soul bearer, which completes Bear Your Soul. If you lose the damaged soul bearer, the wiki says you can dig up another in the crypt of the Arceuus church, east of the library, with a spade. Keep Protect from Melee up near the hellhounds. If you die here you respawn in Falador, and the pile is past the gate; get a second dusty key from Velrak on the way back.

        Done whenBear Your Soul is complete.

        Coins: about 165,000 gp · Slots: about 23/28 · Requirements: full initiate for the altar step; 53 Fishing for the lava eel; the dusty key or 70 Agility for the deep dungeon; Bear Your Soul started in Chapter 1

        Wiki referencesWanted!Dusty keyHeroes' QuestScorpion CatcherBear Your SoulFalador Diary

        Kourend, the hourlies and Olaf's Quest

        A short block that ends at your house, ready for the cat quests. It comes first because Olaf's Quest finishes in Rellekka, where your house and the cat's menagerie are.

        Gate inThe Fremennik Trials, 40 Firemaking and 50 Woodcutting for Olaf's Quest; 22 Agility and 20 Thieving for The Writing's on the Wall; the dramen staff for fairy rings AJR and DJR; a limpwurt root and a tarromin potion (unf) if the Kourend easy diary is still open.
        Carry inan axe, a tinderbox, a spade and ropes, with four free slots: about 20/28.
        ExitThe Writing's on the Wall and Olaf's Quest done; hourlies run; the Kourend easy lamp used if it was open; about 185,000 gp; the cat collected for the next block.
        Carries forwardthe cat.

        Lovakengj and the Kourend easy diary

        Chapter 1 dropped Kharedst's memoirs, so use the fairy rings: break a house tablet to Rellekka, walk to fairy ring AJR at the Fremennik Slayer Cave south-east of the town, take it to DJR at the Chasm of Fire, and walk to Lovakengj. If you didn't finish the Kourend & Kebos easy diary in Chapter 1, make the strength potion in the Lovakengj pub now, from a tarromin potion (unf) and a limpwurt root. Then walk to the castle, collect the reward and put the lamp on Herblore. Elise gives it, in the castle courtyard south-east of the statue of King Rada I, and the strength potion task needs 12 Herblore. Stay on the standard spellbook and do your hourlies (farming contracts and bird houses) if you haven't done them recently. The Arceuus teleports cost soul runes, which you cannot buy yet (see the end of 2.1), so the Morytania blocks below travel by the Falador, Lumbridge and Varrock teleports and fairy rings.

        Done whenthe Kourend easy diary is claimed and the hourlies are done.

        The Writing's on the Wall

        RequirementsThe Writing's on the Wall: 22 Agility and 20 Thieving, neither boostable, and no items; the wiki recommends a few free slots and stamina or energy potions.

        While you are in Kourend, do The Writing's on the Wall, one of the Quest Month quests (November 2026). It starts with Betty outside the north-easternmost house in Land's End and moves through Hosidius, Port Piscarilius, the Shayzien Encampment and an island in the River Hos. There is nothing to kill, but at Port Piscarilius Sergeant Hudson arrests you and Betty, a guard who catches you sends you back to the cell, you can't teleport out of the cell, and near the end a hooded killer knocks you out: none of it is a death.

        Rewards1 quest point, 2,500 Agility XP, 2,500 Thieving XP and an amulet of sight; drop the amulet unless you want it, since Sergeant Hudson sells more later.
        Done whenThe Writing's on the Wall is complete.

        Olaf's Quest

        RequirementsOlaf's Quest: The Fremennik Trials, 40 Firemaking and 50 Woodcutting.

        Break a house tablet (the house is in Rellekka since the Underground Pass block) and do Olaf's Quest. The wiki lists an axe, a tinderbox, a spade and six ropes, which you can get during the quest. Olaf stands north-east of Rellekka, near the entrance to the snow hunting area. After the carvings he has you light damp planks on embers and gives you Sven's last map: read it, and dig beside the windswept tree to fall into the Brine Rat Cavern; it asks for four free slots if you bring your ropes and ten if you don't.

        • Cut windswept logs from the windswept tree on the hill east of Rellekka and give them to Olaf. He makes the crude carving for Ingrid and the cruder carving for Volf.
        • Kill a skeleton fremennik (level 40) for the gate key. If you fall after opening the gate, or lose the key, you need another one. The picture wall's lever puzzle takes the levers in the order right, up, left, down, then the bottom one. The rotten bridge is the risky part: the bridge is twelve tiles long, Agility is the only factor, and the wiki says crossing the whole bridge is guaranteed at 78 Agility and about even at 73, so below 78 expect to fall. A fall puts you back near Olaf to dig in again. Use a rotten barrel from the cave on each broken section, with the six ropes if you didn't bring them.
        • Captain Ulfric (level 100) has 60 Hitpoints and uses only melee, so Protect from Melee covers him. His grave works as a safespot, but he can teleport to you. Once he falls, search the chest again to claim the treasure: the quest doesn't end until you do.

        The rubies would cost four slots for most of a section, so High Alchemy them: 2.2 buys rubies at a gem shop when it makes digsite pendants.

        Rewards12,000 Defence XP, 20,000 coins, four cut rubies, access to the Brine Rat Cavern and a parchment for The Graveyard.

        Finish by breaking a house tablet: your house is in Rellekka. Pick up the cat from the menagerie for the next block.

        Done whenOlaf's Quest is complete and the cat is with you.

        Coins: about 185,000 gp · Slots: about 20/28, 21/28 with the cat · Requirements: The Fremennik Trials, 40 Firemaking and 50 Woodcutting for Olaf's Quest; a limpwurt root and a tarromin potion (unf) for the diary potion

        Wiki referencesOlaf's QuestKourend & Kebos DiaryFarming contractBird house trapping

        Burthorpe and Varrock: the cat quests, Wanted!'s essence and Horvik's steel bars

        The first half of this block uses the cat; the second half is a planned death. Wanted! needs 20 unnoted essence in your inventory at once, and One Small Favour needs three steel bars for Horvik, which a UIM has to mine and smelt. Neither fits in a full inventory, so you do both from an empty one while your gear waits in a Falador deathpile.

        Gate inGertrude's Cat and Icthlarin's Little Helper; the catspeak amulet and five death runes; Keldagrim access and The Giant Dwarf started for Ratcatchers; 30 Mining and 30 Smithing; respawn in Falador.
        Carry inthe cat for the first half; for the planned death, only a pickaxe and a few thousand coins (25/28 at most).
        ExitA Tail of Two Cats and Ratcatchers started; three steel bars with Horvik and the steel chainbody bought; 20 essence given to the Mage of Zamorak; the Falador pile collected; about 180,000 gp.
        Carries forwardtwo steel bars and one coal (Burgh de Rott furnace), the steel chainbody (Ivan Strom), the rat pole (Ratcatchers in 2.2).

        A Tail of Two Cats and the start of Ratcatchers

        From your house in Rellekka, with the cat following, take a games necklace to Burthorpe. Wear the catspeak amulet and talk to Unferth to start A Tail of Two Cats, then pay Hild five death runes to enchant the amulet into a catspeak amulet (e), which finds Bob. You must have a cat or kitten with you throughout the quest (it can stay in your inventory). Hild lives in the house north-east of Unferth's, and the five death runes are the whole fee. The amulet shows where Bob is, but he roams Misthalin, Asgarnia and the northern Kharidian Desert and can't enter the Wilderness or pass the Shantay Pass.

        Use the chronicle to the Champions' Guild and run to Varrock. Bob often hides in the anvil building south of Varrock West bank, so hop worlds there until you find him. The wiki lists that building, the Catherby archery shop and the fence north of Draynor Manor as where he gets stuck. Then:

        1. Talk to Gertrude, west of Varrock, for A Tail of Two Cats, and start Ratcatchers with her; she is south of the Cooking Guild.
        2. Talk to Reldo in the palace library.
        3. Find Bob again and suggest the Sphinx. The Sphinx and Unferth's chores follow in 2.2.
        4. For the Varrock diary, chop a yew log behind the palace and burn it at the top of the church.
        5. Go into the Varrock Sewers east of the palace exit. Let both Sisters Grime sniff your cat before you chase anything (the wiki says otherwise the rats don't count), then use the Chase option on your cat for eight rats; the sisters and your quest journal both give the count. The wiki says an overgrown cat can't catch rats here, so check that your menagerie cat hasn't grown past a normal one, and if the cat vanishes, log out and back in or use the Call follower button. They give you a rat pole and send you to Jimmy Dazzler in Ardougne, which is the 2.2 stop. Kill rats for Rag and Bone Man II while you're down there, and a moss giant for its bone (the wiki says the web on the way needs a slash weapon or a knife); Crandor's moss giants came before Rag and Bone Man II had started.

        What not to buy here. Don't buy dairy and baking items in Lumbridge for these quests, or 11 furs from Baraek here. The wiki puts the dairy uses in 2.2: four cheese and a bucket of milk for Hooknosed Jack in Ratcatchers, a chocolate cake and a bucket of milk for Unferth's chores, and a bucket of milk, an egg, a pot of flour and a cake tin for the Lumbridge Guide in Recipe for Disaster. The Culinaromancer's Chest in the Lumbridge Castle basement sells the Lumbridge Guide items, so buy them there when those steps are next. The furs are ten furs (bear fur is accepted, and Baraek sells it) for The Great Brain Robbery in 2.3, so buy them from Baraek then.

        Break a house tablet and put the cat back in the menagerie before the next part.

        Done whenthe Sisters Grime have sent you to Jimmy Dazzler, the Varrock yew task is done and the cat is home.

        The planned death: essence for Wanted! and steel bars for Horvik

        Teleport to Falador. In the Falador Castle courtyard, take off everything and die to a White Knight, as before. You respawn in Falador with nothing, and the pile's hour of logged-in time starts at that moment. From the pile, take only:

        • your pickaxe (wield it);
        • a few thousand coins.

        The death destroys the Chronicle, as every death does; you buy it back in Draynor on the way to Ernest the Chicken.

        You carry no nature runes, so don't plan on Superheat. Smelting at a furnace works: steel bars don't fail, unlike iron bars. In the Dwarven Mine under Falador, mine five iron ore and eleven coal, then smelt five steel bars at the Falador furnace (30 Smithing, boostable). A steel bar takes one iron ore and two coal, so five bars use ten of the eleven coal and the eleventh is the spare. Three bars go to Horvik and two wait for the Burgh de Rott furnace. Keep the spare coal: In Aid of the Myreque needs two steel bars and one coal for the Burgh de Rott furnace. That leaves seven slots used.

        Run east from Falador to Varrock. Give three steel bars to Horvik in his armour shop, north-east of the Varrock square, to continue One Small Favour (the wiki has him ask next for medicine and pigeon cages, which 2.2's Varrock stop collects), and buy one steel chainbody from him (750 coins; Ivan Strom takes it in In Aid of the Myreque). Talk to your Heroes' Quest gang leader (Straven for the Phoenix Gang, Katrine for the Black Arm Gang). This talk starts the Master Thieves' Armband, the third item Achietties wants; the job itself is in Brimhaven, in a later Heroes' Quest block. Then go to the rune essence mine through Aubury's shop and mine 20 essence; above 30 Mining you get pure essence. Wanted! wants 20 unnoted essence of one type, and a UIM must hand all 20 over at once, so mine them in one trip. With the bars handed over and the chainbody bought, that is 25 slots in total.

        Give the 20 essence to the Mage of Zamorak to continue Wanted!. He stands in the Zamorakian chapel in south-east Varrock; ask him about Solus Dellagar, and he trades his tip for the essence. Because Chapter 1 already had you talk to him in the Wilderness, he doesn't first send you out there. Don't wear Saradomin, Guthix or Void items for this, and you're wearing almost nothing. His tip sends you to Canifis. Walk back to Falador and pick up the rest of your pile. The whole errand is well under the pile's 60 logged-in minutes.

        Done whenHorvik has three steel bars, the chainbody is bought, the Mage of Zamorak has the essence and your pile is collected.

        Coins: about 180,000 gp · Slots: about 20/28 plus the cat; 25/28 at most during the errand · Requirements: Gertrude's Cat and Icthlarin's Little Helper (for the catspeak amulet) for A Tail of Two Cats; Gertrude's Cat, Icthlarin's Little Helper, Keldagrim access and The Giant Dwarf started for Ratcatchers; 30 Mining and 30 Smithing for the steel bars

        Wiki referencesA Tail of Two CatsRatcatchersWanted!One Small FavourIn Aid of the MyrequeRecipe for Disaster/Freeing the Lumbridge Guide

        Wanted!'s chase, Canifis and Ernest the Chicken

        Wanted! finishes with a chase that sends you around the map, and a UIM can't stop at a bank between legs. Carry your full set of teleports for the whole chase, and the Dorgesh-Kaan sphere from the Lumbridge block. Falador Teleport (or a tablet) brings you back to Falador between legs, and the Morytania blocks after it travel by Varrock Teleport and the fairy rings, not by the Arceuus teleports, which need soul runes you cannot buy yet. The chase moves you around by force: leg two throws you to Camelot and leg four sends you to Falador.

        Gate inthe commorb and the Dorgesh-Kaan sphere; 40 Agility for the rooftops; earmuffs and 15 Slayer for banshees; the wolfbane dagger from Drezel; a cowhide; for Animal Magnetism, The Restless Ghost, Priest in Peril, 18 Slayer, 19 Crafting, 30 Ranged and 35 Woodcutting.
        Carry inyour full set of teleports for the whole chase and one free slot for Solus's hat: about 24/28.
        ExitWanted! done; the Canifis diary tasks and a Mazchna task done; Ernest the Chicken done and Animal Magnetism started; about 175,000 gp.
        Carries forwardthe hard leather (Animal Magnetism), the ring of charos (a).

        Canifis and the chase

        Go to Canifis, where the Mage of Zamorak's tip pointed, on foot through the Paterdomus temple east of Varrock: Drezel, in the dungeon below it, gives you a new wolfbane dagger for the werewolf task below (the wiki says he replaces a lost one), since Chapter 1 didn't carry it. Use the commorb's scan option around the town until Solus appears, speaks briefly and vanishes. Savant explains the scan; if you skip his conversation, step out of town to the fairy ring to trigger it again. The wiki gives seven chase locations: the third is the Champions' Guild, the fifth is the Dorgesh-Kaan mine (use the sphere; the wiki lists a light source for this leg, and the lit sapphire lantern you carry from Dorgesh-Kaan covers it (relight it with your tinderbox if a scarab trap in the Sophanem Dungeon put it out, since the wiki lists it as extinguishable); the wiki also says a woman claiming to be a hostage is Solus, who punches you and escapes) and the seventh is the rune essence mine. The others are random, and each one leaves an item that hints at the next. At the third you must already be inside the Champions' Guild before you scan. One random leg is the Grand Tree, near King Narode Shareen, another the ghosts north of the Observatory, and the giant frog legs one is the end of the Lumbridge Swamp Caves, where the wiki lists a light source and a spiny helmet. Use the sapphire lantern rather than a candle or torch: the wiki's light source table marks a lit sapphire lantern as one that doesn't ignite the swamp gas, while candles, torches and oil lamps can explode it in parts of these caves. The helmet is only for the wall beasts on the way, and you can avoid them without one by stopping on the square just before a hole in the wall and then clicking past it, so you don't need to buy it. At the second Solus teleports you to Camelot. At the fourth he hits you with Flames of Zamorak, scaled to your current Hitpoints and ignoring protection prayers, but Savant prevents the death and sends you to Falador with your items intact. At the sixth he summons a level 32 Black Knight, which you can run from rather than fight.

        For the final fight, have a weapon and armour on. Solus has 40 Hitpoints and a weak but fast melee attack. The wiki warns that if your inventory is full when you kill him, you lose his hat (Savant can replace it), so keep one slot free. Start the fight by talking to a wizard who teleports to the rune essence mine; Savant blocks other teleports once you are in. Fifteen rangers appear and Solus kills them with Ice Barrage, which weakens him. Afterwards teleport to Falador and give the hat to Sir Amik Varze in the White Knights' Castle to finish.

        UIM riskThe fight is an instance, so drop nothing in it: the wiki says items left on its floor are lost.
        Rewards5,000 Slayer XP and access to the White Knights' armoury.

        While you pass through Canifis during the chase, pick up its diary tasks and bones:

        • a lap of the Canifis Rooftop Course (40 Agility);
        • a werewolf in human form, killed with Drezel's wolfbane dagger; drop it before the next kills;
        • several werewolves in wolf form for Rag and Bone Man II, without the wolfbane dagger, which stops the bone dropping. You can safespot them on a table in the pub;
        • green dragonhide tanned at Sbott, if you have any, and one cowhide tanned into hard leather there for Animal Magnetism (kill a cow at the Lumbridge pen or west of Falador before the chase);
        • a banshee in the Slayer Tower, with earmuffs;
        • a Slayer task from Mazchna.
        Done whenSolus is defeated and the Canifis tasks and a Mazchna task are done.

        Ernest the Chicken and the start of Animal Magnetism

        Teleport to Falador and put on your ring of charos (a): Ghosts Ahoy, the Ratcatchers snake charmer and several charters are cheaper with it. If you have lost it, pickpocket Dr Fenkenstrain at Fenkenstrain's Castle (25 Thieving) for another. Then teleport to Lumbridge, run west to Draynor, buy the Chronicle back from Diango in the market, and run north to Draynor Manor and do Ernest the Chicken, which Veronica starts at the gate south of the manor. Everything it needs is inside the manor, including the spade. The fish food, poison and key come from inside, and the oil can sits behind the basement lever puzzle; Telekinetic Grab can't take it. The fish food is in a blue box on the first floor, in the room south of the main staircase, and the poison is in a green bottle in the small north-western ground-floor room south of the kitchen. Use the poison on the fish food, then use that on the fountain in the south-western grounds to kill the piranhas, then search the fountain for the pressure gauge. A spade lies beside the south-eastern room's outside door. With it, search the compost heap on the west side of the grounds for a key, then go back in through the front door to the room behind the stairs for the tube. An aggressive skeleton waits there, so take it and leave quickly. The levers reset if you leave the secret basement room, so finish the puzzle in one go. Take all three parts to Professor Oddenstein (centre staircase, then the spiral stairs west) and leave through the back doors: the quest isn't complete until you do.

        Rewards4 quest points, 300 coins, Killerwatt access and a 500 XP lamp: use it on Herblore straight away, like the other lamps.

        In the same manor, search the bookcase to open the secret door and talk to Ava to start Animal Magnetism. Ava is on the ground floor of the west wing; if she won't start the quest, you probably picked Trade instead of Talk-to.

        Done whenErnest the Chicken is complete and Ava has started Animal Magnetism.

        Coins: about 175,000 gp · Slots: about 24/28 with teleports and the steel bars for the Myreque · Requirements: the commorb and 20 essence handed in for Wanted!; 40 Agility for Canifis rooftops; earmuffs and 15 Slayer for banshees; The Restless Ghost, Ernest the Chicken, Priest in Peril, 18 Slayer, 19 Crafting, 30 Ranged and 35 Woodcutting for Animal Magnetism

        Wiki referencesWanted!Ernest the ChickenAnimal MagnetismMorytania Diary

        Morytania: ecto-tokens, Ghosts Ahoy, desert lizards and the Myreque steel kit

        The ecto-tokens are the slot-heavy part of this block, so the Myreque steel kit waits until the tokens are made.

        Gate inPriest in Peril and The Restless Ghost; 22 Slayer and ice coolers; The Fremennik Trials for Skulgrimen's shop; the ghostspeak amulet; a silver bar for the dust.
        Carry infour bones, four pots and four buckets per Ectofuntus batch (about 26/28), then the steel kit (about 26/28).
        Exitabout 40 ecto-tokens; Ghosts Ahoy started; the desert lizard tasks and A Shell of a Friend done; the six steel weapons and the med helm bought; about 170,000 gp.
        Carries forwardthe ecto-tokens, the silver dust (Guthix balance), the steel kit (both Myreque quests).

        Bones and the Ectofuntus in two batches

        Teleport to Varrock and take fairy ring DKR, by the Grand Exchange, to ALQ, in the Haunted Woods, from where Alice's farm is a short run north. For Animal Magnetism, talk to Alice and her husband Malcolm at their farm with the ghostspeak amulet itself on (the wiki says Morytania legs don't count for the quest's ghostspeak amulet). Go back and forth between the two until Alice mentions the witch who changes ghostspeak amulets, who is the Old Crone; stop there, because you visit her for both quests in the next block.

        You need about 40 ecto-tokens with the ring of charos (a) worn: Animal Magnetism takes 20 for two undead chickens, and the wiki lists 31 tokens for Ghosts Ahoy, 16 with the ring. Without the ring, make about 55 (eleven worships) instead. Get 8 bones from an experiment, undead cows and undead chickens (the experiment and the undead cows also count for Rag and Bone Man II), and worship 8 times at the Ectofuntus. The wiki gives 5 ecto-tokens per worship, not per bone, so eight worships make about 40, collected from a ghost disciple while you wear the amulet. Each worship needs a bones-full pot of bonemeal from the grinder and a bucket of slime from the basement pool. The grinder is up the stairs east of the Ectofuntus: use the bones on its loader instead of burying them. The pool is down the trapdoor west of it, and one click with a bucket fills every empty bucket you carry. For a UIM, eight bones, eight pots and eight buckets are 24 slots, so do it in two batches of four. Kill, grind, fill and worship four, then repeat. Grind a silver bar in the grinder as well: In Aid of the Myreque's Guthix balance uses the silver dust.

        Done whenyou hold about 40 ecto-tokens and the silver dust.

        Start Ghosts Ahoy

        In Port Phasmatys, start Ghosts Ahoy with Velorina and continue until it sends you to the Old Crone. Stop there for now. You pay 2 tokens at the energy barrier to get into town, and the wiki says to keep enough tokens to travel in and out. The nearest bank outside is in Canifis, no use to a UIM, so leave the 20 for the chickens alone. If you took Chapter 1's Isle of Souls option and finished Ghosts Ahoy there, skip its steps here and in the Ectophial block; Animal Magnetism still needs its 20 ecto-tokens.

        Done whenVelorina has sent you to the Old Crone.

        Desert lizards

        Take fairy ring ALQ, in the Haunted Woods west of Port Phasmatys, to DLQ, which the wiki lists as the quick way in; it lands north of Nardah, so you still walk to the lizards. The wiki puts them south-east of the Shantay Pass across the River Elid and west of the Ruins of Ullek, so you walk the rest. Desert heat applies there; the spot without it is the edge of Uzer Oasis, south-west of the tea shop. Kill a level 24 desert lizard for the desert diary and a level 42 one for Rag and Bone Man II, finishing each with an ice cooler (22 Slayer). The cooler only works on a lizard below 5 Hitpoints; used earlier it is consumed and does nothing, so wait until the lizard is nearly dead and bring spares. The wiki says ice spells don't count for the final blow.

        Done whenboth desert lizards are killed.

        A Shell of a Friend

        RequirementsA Shell of a Friend: Priest in Peril, 16 Herblore and 15 Construction, and an axe: a bronze axe spawns near the fairy ring, so you don't need to carry one.

        Take fairy ring ALQ, east of Canifis, and cross the stepping stone south-east of the small island just west of it to start A Shell of a Friend with the Juvenile Snail, a short Quest Month quest. The Haunted Woods have aggressive feral vampyres, you kill a leech (level 39), and the last fight is against SnelmLover500 (level 50); the snail can't die. Your current combat gear and a little food are enough.

        Rewards2 quest points, 2,500 Herblore XP, 2,500 Construction XP and a pink snelm; drop the snelm and the axe.
        Done whenA Shell of a Friend is complete.

        The Myreque steel kit

        In Search of the Myreque needs a steel longsword, two steel shortswords, a steel mace, a steel warhammer and a steel dagger in your inventory at the same time: Cyreg Paddlehorn in Mort'ton won't continue the dialogue unless all six are there. Buy them from shops rather than smithing them, and only now, after the ecto-token batches:

        • break a house tablet: your house is in Rellekka, and Skulgrimen's Battle Gear there sells steel warhammers;
        • from Rellekka, walk to fairy ring AJR at the Fremennik Slayer Cave and take it to DJR at the Chasm of Fire, then walk to Shayzien. Briget's sells everything except the warhammer, which is why you collect it in Rellekka first. Briget's Weapons sells steel swords, longswords, daggers and maces, and Blair's Armour sells the steel med helm for In Aid of the Myreque.

        With the chainbody from Horvik and the platelegs from Al Kharid, that is the whole kit: six weapons for In Search of the Myreque and three armour pieces for Ivan Strom. Then walk to the fairy ring at the Chasm of Fire (DJR, near Shayzien) and take it to CKS in Canifis for the Myreque block.

        Done whenyou hold the six steel weapons and the three armour pieces.

        Coins: about 170,000 gp · Slots: about 26/28 per token batch; about 26/28 with the steel kit · Requirements: Priest in Peril and The Restless Ghost for Ghosts Ahoy; 22 Slayer and ice coolers for desert lizards; The Fremennik Trials for Skulgrimen's shop

        Wiki referencesEctofuntusAnimal MagnetismGhosts AhoyDesert lizardSteel warhammerBlair's ArmourIn Search of the Myreque

        In Search of the Myreque

        You arrive in Canifis with the steel kit. The cat is already in your menagerie, which matters for the next block: the wiki says pets aren't allowed in the Ivan Strom escort instance.

        Gate inNature Spirit, Priest in Peril and The Restless Ghost; 25 Agility (boostable); the six steel weapons; the silver sickle from the Al Kharid furnace; 50 Agility for fairy ring BIP; the cat left at home.
        Carry inthe weapons, the silver sickle, 17 planks in the plank sack and the nail stack, with the druid pouch and its fungus collected at the grotto: about 25/28.
        ExitIn Search of the Myreque done; the DLS fairy ring open; Ghouls and bats for Rag and Bone Man II; about 165,000 gp; about 18/28.
        Carries forward11 planks and 44 nails (In Aid of the Myreque).

        The Nature Grotto: the druid pouch and the blessed sickle

        Chapter 1 left the druid pouch and its silver sickle behind after Nature Spirit, and this quest's ghasts drain the pouch on the walk to the Myreque. Take fairy ring BIP, which the wiki says needs 50 Agility and lands you on the small island west of the grotto, and jump the broken bridge to the cave.

        1. Pick up a druid pouch: the wiki says a lost one can be reclaimed in the grotto, where one respawns near the pool.
        2. Bless the silver sickle you cast in Al Kharid; the wiki's silver sickle (b) page says you use it on the altar of nature in the grotto.
        3. Outside, cast Bloom with the blessed sickle next to rotting logs, pick the Mort myre fungi and fill the pouch. Each fill takes three blossomed items and a fungus is worth one charge, so six fungi give the 5 charges the quest needs with one to spare. Bloom costs Prayer points; praying at the altar of nature restores them. The pouch won't fill with fewer than three blossomed items in your inventory, and Bloom costs 1 to 6 Prayer points a cast. Keep the blessed sickle in your inventory whenever you are in the swamp: the wiki says carrying it stops swamp decay, an area effect that hits for 1 to 3 and can kill.

        Then take fairy ring BIP to CKS for Canifis.

        Done whenyou hold a druid pouch with at least 5 charges and the silver sickle (b).

        Ghouls, bats and Mort'ton shopping

        Kill ghouls west of Canifis, by the gravestones near fairy ring CKS, for Rag and Bone Man II and the Morytania diary, and bats just outside the Slayer Tower entrance for Rag and Bone Man II. Then talk to Vanstrom Klause in the north-east corner of the Hair of the Dog in Canifis to start In Search of the Myreque. He sends you to deliver weapons and mentions a boatman in Mort'ton. Each ghoul and bat kill has a one in four chance of dropping its bone (a bat wing, for bats).

        Shades of Mort'ton, done in Chapter 1, lets you buy planks and a hammer in Mort'ton. The swamp paste for In Aid of the Myreque's bank repair is the one item nothing earlier in the guide supplies, so buy one at the Razmire Builders Merchants in Mort'ton (31 gp) while you are there; it stacks, and it is used up at the bank in the same trip, so it costs you a slot only for that stretch. The nails for both quests (75 per bridge plank; in In Aid of the Myreque, 12 per roof or wall repair and 8 for the bank booth) are steel nails from any sawmill operator, who stocks 1,000 at a time. Buy all the planks for both Myreque quests at once, 17 in all: 6 for this quest (3 for the boat, 3 for the bridge) and 11 for In Aid of the Myreque. They go into the plank sack, so they cost one slot. Carry the nails as one stack: 225 steel nails for this quest's bridge and 44 for the next quest, of which the wiki says 42 can be found during it.

        The druid pouch needs at least 5 charges. Ghasts drain it on the walk, so bring 6 Mort myre fungus (or 3 pears or 3 stems) to refill it on arrival, from blooms with your silver sickle (b). Ghasts are aggressive, rot food in your inventory and drain Prayer, so check the pouch's count before every crossing.

        Done whenIn Search of the Myreque is started and you hold 17 planks, the nails and a charged druid pouch.

        The boat, the bridge and the Myreque

        1. Find Cyreg Paddlehorn by his boat at the eastern edge of Mort'ton and persuade him. The wiki gives the replies in order: "Well, I guess they'll just die without weapons...", "Resourceful enough to get their own steel weapons?", "If you don't tell me, their deaths are on your head!" and "What kind of man are you to say that you don't care?". Pick them in exactly that order, with the six weapons in your inventory.
        2. Show him the druid pouch and hand over the planks he asks for. He refuses to take you himself and rents the boat only if the pouch holds at least 5 charges. He takes 3 planks for his boat, and the other 3 stay in your bag for the Hollows bridge (the wiki's item list for the whole quest is 6 planks). So check that you still have 3 planks and the 225 nails before you cross, and buy 3 more in Mort'ton if you are short.
        3. The boat trip costs 10 coins, or nothing with your ring of charos (a). The Morytania medium diary asks you to board the swamp boat at the Hollows, which the free ride back to Mort'ton covers, so do that when you pass.

        The wiki's order puts the bridge repair after the boat and the rope-bridge climb, and before Curpile Fyod. From the landing, go north, avoiding the ghasts, climb the strange tree to the damaged rope bridge and climb down on the far side. Don't repair the bridge before the boat steps are done, or you will repair it twice. Mend it as you cross: 75 nails per plank, 3 planks. At the hollow tree, Curpile Fyod asks six questions. The wiki's answers: Sani Piliu, Veliaf Hurtz, Cyreg Paddlehorn, Polmafi Ferdygris, Ivan Strom and Drakan. A wrong answer knocks you out and returns you to the boat; the bridge stays mended.

        In the hideout, talk to Veliaf, greet the other five members and give Veliaf the six steel weapons. The path runs north-west, and halfway up the hidden cave entrance is in the south-east corner of the eastern alcove. Vanstrom then reveals himself and summons a level 97 Skeleton Hellhound. It has 55 Hitpoints, no Magic defence and is weak to Earth spells. It hits with crush for up to 12 every 2.4 seconds, and if you leave mid-fight it respawns near the entrance. Use the safespot in the upper east corner of the room, south of the chair, with Protect from Melee. If you die in the chamber, your items are placed outside the room; a UIM has no gravestone, so it is a normal deathpile with the one-hour timer. The boat back needs no pouch charges and the bridge stays mended. The DLS fairy ring only opens as a quest reward, so the way back to that pile is the long walk through the swamp and over the bridge.

        Ask Veliaf how to leave and take the trapdoor passage to Canifis; any other exit means coming back to do it. Search the wall at the end of the tunnel to open the trapdoor behind the Canifis pub and climb the ladder; you surface south of the tavern. Walk back inside and find a Stranger, not Vanstrom, who finishes the quest.

        Rewards2 quest points, 600 XP each in Attack, Strength, Defence, Hitpoints and Crafting, and access to The Hollows and the DLS fairy ring.
        Done whenIn Search of the Myreque is complete.

        Coins: about 165,000 gp · Slots: about 24/28 with the weapons, 18/28 after handing them in · Requirements: Nature Spirit, Priest in Peril, The Restless Ghost and 25 Agility (boostable); a level 97 hellhound to defeat

        Wiki referencesIn Search of the MyrequeDruid pouchMorytania Diary

        In Aid of the Myreque to the rod mould

        Burgh de Rott has no working bank until you repair it, and for a UIM the bank itself changes little. What matters is that the general store's stock is 23 items that you don't need to carry all at once. This block goes as far as the rod mould; the Rod of Ivandis waits for 2.2.

        Gate inIn Search of the Myreque; 25 Agility and 7 Magic (boostable); 25 Crafting and 15 Mining (not boostable); two steel bars and one coal; the steel chainbody, platelegs and med helm; one soft clay; 22 Herblore for the Guthix balance.
        Carry inone food, a hammer, a pickaxe, a spade, one to five buckets, the planks and nails, a tinderbox and five free slots: about 25/28.
        ExitBurgh de Rott's store, bank and furnace repaired; Ivan escorted; the rod mould taken; the Guthix balance made; about 160,000 gp.
        Carries forwardthe rod mould (the silvthrill rod in 2.2), one harralander (the Guthix rest in 2.2), Efaritay's aid.

        Into Burgh de Rott

        Talk to Veliaf in the Myreque Hideout (fairy ring DLS) to start In Aid of the Myreque. For the first entry the wiki lists one food (you use it on the open chest at the gate), a hammer, a pickaxe, a spade, one to five buckets, the planks and the nails, plus six free slots. Florin at the gate refuses you until you use the food on the open chest in front of it; after that the shades stop attacking you. Mine the rubble in the bar cellar, bucket it and empty the buckets on the pile outside the bar. Anything you drop in the cellar vanishes when you leave, and mined rubble comes back if you didn't remove it, so don't leave until it is cleared. Repair the general store's roof and wall with 3 planks and 12 nails each.

        Done whenthe rubble is cleared and the general store is repaired.

        Stocking Aurel's store

        Aurel wants 10 bronze axes, 3 tinderboxes and either 10 mackerel or 10 snail meats, raw or cooked. The items can't be noted and go into a crate that you then return to him. It also says you can reroll the fish type by leaving his dialogue before he hands you the crate.

        The crate is one slot, so fill it in batches instead of carrying 23 items:

        1. Take the crate and check whether he wants mackerel or snails; if it is mackerel, leave his dialogue and ask again until it is snails.
        2. Teleport out and buy the bronze axes and four tinderboxes (three for the crate, one kept to light the furnace), putting the axes and three tinderboxes into the crate as you go. Bob's Brilliant Axes in Lumbridge sells bronze axes, and general stores sell tinderboxes.
        3. Catch the snails in the Mort Myre swamp, close to Burgh de Rott; the druid pouch keeps the ghasts off. The wiki says the snails are aggressive and only Protect from Missiles seems to blunt their attack, and that the long path south to Mort'ton has plenty of snails and no ghasts. Mackerel would mean a trip to Frankie's Fishing Emporium in Port Piscarilius, which Chapter 1's dropped memoirs no longer makes quick.
        4. Return the full crate to Aurel.
        Done whenAurel has the full crate.

        The bank, the furnace and the juvinates

        Repair the bank with 5 planks, 20 nails and a swamp paste, then talk to Cornelius. Inspect the bank booth first, repair the wall on the bank's west side and then ask Cornelius what to do next; he agrees to become the banker. The wiki lists the repair items as 5 planks, a hammer, swamp paste and 20 nails, which in a normal game you fetch from your bank and a UIM carries in. Repair the furnace in the south-east of town with the two steel bars, add the coal and light it with a tinderbox. That starts a fight with Gadderanks and two Vampyre Juvinates in the general store. Wear Efaritay's aid: the wiki recommends it or a silver weapon, and says juvinates can be flinched or safespotted. Leaving the room resets the fight.

        Done whenthe bank and furnace are repaired and the juvinates are dead.

        Escort Ivan Strom

        Give Ivan the steel chainbody, platelegs and med helm; you won't get them back. Start the escort from Veliaf at the hideout, with pets left at home. The wiki also suggests a silver sickle for him. He accepts up to 15 stews, salmon, snails or slimy eels. You can bring fewer than 15 salmon on the short route, because if Ivan runs out of food or health he only teleports out and the escort resets. Take the short route (two level 75 juvinates), keep Ivan behind you and keep Efaritay's aid on. After the first vampyre dies, stand behind Ivan, talk to him and step into his square so he moves behind you. If he teleports out, leave south toward Canifis, come back and retry; he keeps his gear but needs food again. Afterwards, Drezel in the Paterdomus basement gives you a library key when you ask about Ivandis and then press him with the option about the lives of those few left.

        Done whenIvan is escorted and Drezel has given you the library key.

        The rod mould and the Guthix balance

        Read The Sleeping Seven in the Paterdomus library, behind the keyhole wall that the key opens (use it on the empty east wall, then search the middle bookcase facing the west wall). Reading it matters: the wiki says nothing happens at the coffin if you skipped that step.
