Then hammer the boards near the hideout to open the cave, and use a soft clay on the coffin (keep one from the house-tablet run) to get the rod mould. Stop there. The silvthrill rod is made in 2.2 at the Neitiznot furnace, and the quest ends much later in 2.2.

        Make a Guthix balance in Morytania for the diary: a harralander potion (unf), red spiders' eggs, garlic and the silver dust from the Ectofuntus. That uses one of your two remaining harralanders; the other is for the Guthix rest in One Small Favour in 2.2.

        Done whenyou hold the rod mould and the Guthix balance task is done.

        Coins: about 160,000 gp · Slots: about 25/28 on the first entry · Requirements: In Search of the Myreque, Nature Spirit, Priest in Peril, The Restless Ghost, 25 Agility and 7 Magic (boostable) and 25 Crafting and 15 Mining (not boostable) for In Aid of the Myreque; 22 Herblore for the Guthix balance

        Wiki referencesIn Aid of the MyrequeBurgh de RottEfaritay's aidGuthix balance

        Ghosts Ahoy to the Ectophial, and Animal Magnetism to the magnet

        The Old Crone, east of the Slayer Tower, is the next stop for both quests, so go there once. Her house sits between the Slayer Tower and the Mausoleum. Fairy ring CKS is the closest.

        Gate in25 Agility and 20 Cooking (boostable) for Ghosts Ahoy; about 40 ecto-tokens; the ghostspeak amulet; 30 Mining; an oak longbow (25 Fletching).
        Carry inthe nettle tea items, the model ship materials, a spade and the ring of charos (a): about 24/28.
        ExitGhosts Ahoy done with the Ectophial; Animal Magnetism at the bar magnet; the mithril axe and three balls of wool ready; Troll Romance's iron bar, rope, swamp tar, cake tin and bucket collected; about 155,000 gp.
        Carries forwardthe Ectophial (Morytania teleport), the mithril axe (the blessed axe), three balls of wool (the holy symbol's string, the monkey amulet and the amulet of strength), the five Troll Romance materials (the next block).

        The Old Crone and the model ship

        Wear the ghostspeak amulet and keep it on, since without it ghosts speak only in ghost language. For Animal Magnetism, the Old Crone gives you a crone-made amulet; take it to Malcolm and buy the two undead chickens for 10 ecto-tokens each. For Ghosts Ahoy, make nettle tea (nettles picked with gloves, a bowl of water, logs and a tinderbox, 20 Cooking), add a bucket of milk and give it to her for the model ship. The gloves must be closed-fingered (the wiki says Slayer, ranger, Infinity, Moonclan and Lunar gloves fail); pick the nettles near Mazchna, heat the bowl over a fire without drinking it, pour the tea into the porcelain cup she gives you and then add the milk.

        Repair the model ship with silk, a needle, thread and a knife; the knife, needle and thread are sold in the Canifis general store. It then has to be dyed in the colours on the flag of the ship's mast, which the wiki says are a mix of red, blue and yellow. Check the mast of the wrecked ship north of the farm at least three times: the wiki says that is how you get all the colours the flag needs. Nine dyes (three of each colour) are nine slots, so look at the flag first and buy only what it needs: Aggie in Draynor makes red, yellow and blue dyes, and if the flag shows orange, purple or green you mix it from two of them. Return the dyed ship to the Old Man for a chest key.

        Done whenyou hold the crone-made amulet, the undead chickens and the chest key.

        The map, Dragontooth Island and the robes

        Open the chest with the key for the first map scrap, cross the rocks (25 Agility) for the second and kill the level 32 giant lobster for the third, then combine them. The lobster can be safespotted, but if it isn't dead within two minutes it disappears and you fight it again at full health. Take the ghost captain's boat to Dragontooth Island and dig at the marked spot with a spade for the Book of Haricanto; the ring of charos (a) cuts the token cost of the trips from 25 to 10. Win Robin's signature at Rune-Draw (100 coins) and trade it to Ak-Haranu for the translation manual. Bring an oak longbow for Robin to sign: fletch one from an oak log and a bowstring (25 Fletching) before you start.

        Get a bedsheet from the Ghost innkeeper, dye it with a bucket of slime (fill one at the Ectofuntus pool, and don't charter a ship while you carry the dyed sheet, because the crewmember refuses you) and wear it to collect ten petition signatures for Gravingas. Ghosts may refuse, agree, or want a token, and the wiki says not to ask the same ghost twice in a row. Show the petition to Necrovarus, who drops a bone key. Use it on the locked door upstairs and search his coffin for the mystical robes. Take the robes, book, manual and amulet to the Old Crone, use the enchanted amulet on Necrovarus and return to Velorina for the Ectophial. From now on the Ectophial is your Morytania teleport.

        Rewards2 quest points, 2,400 Prayer XP and free passage into Port Phasmatys.

        While in Port Phasmatys, cook a thin snail on the range for the diary. Smelting cannonballs at the Phasmatys furnace counts for the diary, but you have no steel bar left now, so leave that for the first time you pass with one; the ammo mould stays with you for it.

        Done whenGhosts Ahoy is complete and you hold the Ectophial.

        Animal Magnetism to the magnet

        1. Teleport to Lumbridge and pick up three of Troll Romance's sled materials while you are here: a cake tin and a bucket from the general store north of the castle (13 and 2 gp) and a swamp tar from the spawns in the Lumbridge Swamp. Then run north-west to Draynor Manor and give Ava the two undead chickens, and buy a rope from Ned in Draynor (18 gp) for the sled.
        2. The witch near Ava swaps five unnoted iron bars for a selected iron. A UIM makes the bars on the spot: take the Pest Control minigame teleport and its boat to Port Sarim, walk to Rimmington mine, mine iron and smelt it at the Falador furnace. Iron bars fail about half the time at a furnace, so mine about twelve ore for six bars: five for the witch and one for Troll Romance's sled.
        3. Then use a hammer on the selected iron while facing north in the Rimmington mine for the bar magnet, and give it to Ava. Stand in the centre of the mine north-east of Rimmington first; you receive 50 Crafting XP when you hand the magnet to Ava.

        The last part of Animal Magnetism needs a blessed axe from Turael in Burthorpe, which takes a mithril axe and a blessed holy symbol. Turael accepts only a mithril axe, and don't ask him for a Slayer task by accident. Chapter 1 dropped its symbol, so the Family Crest block casts a new one at the Al Kharid furnace (silver ore from the mine there and a holy mould from Dommik, 5 gp) and the Edgeville block has Brother Jered bless it. The wiki sells the mithril axe at Perry's Chop-chop Shop in the Woodcutting Guild. Make three balls of wool before you go, one to string the symbol, one for the monkey amulet in Monkey Madness I and one for the amulet of strength: buy shears at the Lumbridge general store (1 gp), shear three sheep in the field beside Fred the Farmer's house, spin the wool at the Lumbridge Castle spinning wheel and drop the shears. The Heroes' Quest block ends in Burthorpe, so Animal Magnetism finishes there.

        Done whenAva has the bar magnet, and you hold the mithril axe, three balls of wool and the five Troll Romance materials.

        Coins: about 155,000 gp · Slots: about 24/28 at the peak · Requirements: 25 Agility and 20 Cooking (boostable) for Ghosts Ahoy; 30 Mining for iron ore

        Wiki referencesGhosts AhoyEctophialAnimal MagnetismMithril axeHoly symbol

        Catherby and Trollheim: Caleb and Troll Romance

        Chapter 1 moved both of these here. Two Catherby stops open the block: Caleb, the first Family Crest brother, and the wax for Troll Romance's sled. The rest of Troll Romance is Trollheim and Burthorpe, and the sled's iron bar, rope, swamp tar, cake tin and bucket came with you from the Animal Magnetism block. Caleb goes first because the whole Family Crest chain hangs on one question to him (below), and the wax stop sits in the same town because the insect repellent spawns in a Catherby house.

        Gate inFamily Crest started with Dimintheis in Chapter 1; Troll Stronghold, Death Plateau and 28 Agility (boostable) for Troll Romance; 61 Magic for Trollheim Teleport (or climbing boots); 46 Fishing and 45 Cooking for Caleb's fish; 45 Woodcutting for a maple log; Plague City for Ardougne Teleport.
        Carry inthe core kit, the five sled materials, Fire Bolt or earth-spell runes and the staff, food, two prayer potions and the rune sword for Arrg, and a few free slots for the fish and the tools: about 24/28.
        ExitCaleb's crest piece; Troll Romance done; two cut rubies for Avan; about 155,000 gp.
        Carries forwardCaleb's crest piece and the two cut rubies (the Family Crest block).

        Caleb's fish

        1. Teleport to Ardougne and walk into West Ardougne. Buy one cooked salmon at the West Ardougne General Store, which the wiki's Family Crest guide gives as the ironman source; Catherby has no salmon. The cooked salmon is the only fish you buy ready-made, so it needs no range.
        2. Teleport to Camelot and walk east to Catherby. Unnote the raw swordfish from your stack at the bank booth (you get one per free slot), keep one and drop the rest. At Harry's Fishing Shop buy a small fishing net (5 gp), a harpoon (5 gp) and a big fishing net (20 gp), and catch one shrimp, one tuna (35 Fishing) and one bass (46 Fishing) on the Catherby shore. Cook the four on a range (43 Cooking for the bass, 45 for the swordfish) and sell the tools back to Harry. Caleb takes all five fish (shrimps, salmon, tuna, bass and swordfish), so check that every one is cooked and in your inventory before you walk to him.
        3. Caleb is in the building with the sink south of the Farming shop, or just outside it. Ask him what happened to the rest of the crest first (the wiki says you can't collect the other pieces otherwise), then give him the five fish for his piece. The wiki's start is 'Are you Caleb Fitzharmon?', then 'So can I have your bit?', then 'Ok, I will get those.' The question about the rest of the crest is the one that matters: if you skip it, you can't collect the other pieces, so don't leave Catherby until you have asked it.
        Done whenCaleb's crest piece is in your inventory.

        The wax and the maple log

        1. Pick up the insect repellent that spawns in the house north of the Catherby bank. Use it on a beehive in the apiary west of Catherby, then use the bucket on the hive for a bucket of wax. Use the swamp tar on the bucket of wax: the wiki's sled page says that makes the wax, held in the cake tin. Drop the repellent. The hives lie south of Camelot, and an empty bucket spawns near them if you need a spare. The order matters: use the tar on the bucket of wax, not the wax on the tar.
        2. Walk west to Seers' Village and chop one maple log (45 Woodcutting). Dunstan accepts maple or yew logs, so the maple is the one you can cut at this level.
        Done whenyou hold the wax, the maple log, the iron bar and the rope.

        Troll Romance

        1. Cast Trollheim Teleport (61 Magic, 2 law and 2 fire runes, open since Eadgar's Ruse); the wiki says it saves the climbing boots. In the Troll Stronghold, talk to Ug in the south-west corner of the middle level to start Troll Romance, then to Aga in the room to the north, then to Ug again. Take the wiki's dialogue options. With Ug, choose 'Aww, you poor troll. What seems to be the problem?' and then 'Don't worry now, I'll see what I can do.' With Aga, ask 'So... how's your... um... love life?' and finish with 'Errr... I've got to go.'
        2. Go down to Burthorpe. Talk to Tenzing in his hut west of the Warriors' Guild: choose 'Do you know where I can find Trollweiss?' and then 'What would I need to make such a sled?' Then go to Dunstan at the anvil in the house north-east of Burthorpe Castle. Choose 'Talk about a quest', then the 'I need a sled' option, then 'Yes.' He turns the iron bar, the maple log and the rope into a sled, and you collect it when you talk to him again and choose 'Talk about a quest' once more. Use the wax on the sled.
        3. Trollheim Teleport again. Run north-west to the cave north-west of the troll family. A troll child may stand near it if you haven't done Desert Treasure; and never go through the icy gate east of the child. Go through the cave past the ice trolls and out through the crevasse at the end, a narrow gap in the north-west corner that is easy to miss (no trolls stand near it inside). Equip the sled, go south and slide down the first slope only: a second slope further south leaves you climbing the mountain again. After the cutscene, walk west and pick the trollweiss before the next cutscene starts. You need a free slot for it, and the wiki warns that teleporting away mid-slide with a full inventory loses the sled. Don't stand about in the snow, which drains your stats.
        4. Back at Ug, give the trollweiss to Ug first: the wiki says you can't talk to him while you hold it. Then talk to Arrg (level 113), choosing the option that says you are here to kill him, and he takes you into his arena. The wiki gives two safespots: stand on the raised ground in the north-west of the arena and run to the west gate so he catches on the mountain wall, or lure him to the northern gate and step onto the raised ground to the west. He disappears after a few minutes if you're slow (the wiki gives no exact time) and reappears where Ug stands. He attacks the moment you arrive, with melee that hits up to 38 and a more accurate ranged attack up to 30, and either can occasionally get through a protection prayer, so wear Protect from Melee from the start and eat early. The spectator trolls don't work as obstacles, because Arrg walks through them. A death here isn't safe, but you can walk back and fight him again; your pile waits by the arena, and the wiki says he won't strike first when you walk back in. Return to Ug to finish.

        Cut the two rubies with your chisel for Avan's jewellery in the next block. Nothing later in Chapter 2 needs these emeralds or this diamond (Monkey Madness I gives three diamonds soon, more than the dragon weapons need), so High Alchemy them, and drop the sled: Dunstan makes another from the same materials if a clue ever asks for one.

        Rewards2 quest points, 8,000 Agility and 4,000 Strength XP, four uncut emeralds, two uncut rubies and an uncut diamond.
        Done whenTroll Romance is complete and you hold the two cut rubies.

        Coins: about 155,000 gp · Slots: about 24/28 arriving; seven free slots for Arrg's gems at the end, after the fish and the sled materials are gone · Requirements: Troll Stronghold, Death Plateau and 28 Agility (boostable) for Troll Romance; 61 Magic for Trollheim Teleport; 35 and 46 Fishing for the tuna and the bass; 43 and 45 Cooking for the bass and the swordfish; 45 Woodcutting for the maple

        Wiki referencesTroll Romance/Quick guideSledInsect repellentFamily CrestHarry's Fishing Shop

        Family Crest: Avan, Boot, the perfect gold and Johnathon

        The rest of Family Crest is a loop from Al Kharid to the Dwarven Mine, Witchaven and back, ending with Johnathon in Varrock just before the Edgeville block's Chronozon fight. The Al Kharid furnace visit also casts the holy symbol that Animal Magnetism's blessed axe needs. The order is fixed by the quest: Avan won't take his jewellery until Boot has spoken to you, and Johnathon's cure is what makes him say that Chronozon holds the last piece.

        Gate inCaleb's crest piece and the two cut rubies; 40 Mining, 40 Smithing, 40 Crafting and 59 Magic for Fire Blast (all boostable); 16 Crafting for the holy symbol.
        Carry inthe core kit, a pickaxe, the crest piece, the rubies, an antipoison for Johnathon (buy one, or make it from a marrentill potion (unf) and unicorn horn dust at 5 Herblore), a ball of wool, food for the hellhound room and about six free slots: about 20/28.
        ExitAvan's crest piece; Johnathon cured; the holy symbol strung but not yet blessed; about 155,000 gp.
        Carries forwardCaleb's and Avan's crest pieces (Chronozon and Dimintheis), the holy symbol (Brother Jered).

        Avan and Boot

        1. Take a ring of dueling to Emir's Arena and walk to Al Kharid. Ask the gem trader 'I'm in search of a man named Avan Fitzharmon', then talk to Avan, a man in a yellow cape at the entrance to the northern Al Kharid mine pit. He wants a ring and a necklace of perfect gold, and you need your pickaxe and the two cut rubies for the steps that follow.
        2. At Dommik's Crafting Store buy a ring mould, a necklace mould and a holy mould (5 gp each).
        3. Teleport to Falador and go into the Dwarven Mine underneath Ice Mountain and run to its far western end, where Boot stands by two rock columns; the wiki says Avan won't take the jewellery until you have spoken to Boot. He tells you where the perfect gold is; open with 'Hello. I'm in search of very high quality gold.' The stop is easy to skip because you can make the jewellery without him, but Avan refuses it until Boot has sent you on.
        Done whenBoot has sent you to Witchaven and you hold the three moulds.

        The perfect gold in the Witchaven Dungeon

        Teleport to Ardougne, walk east to the old ruin entrance and climb down into the Witchaven Dungeon. Follow the wiki's five-step lever sequence between the north and south rooms; it opens the locked east room without your going through its wall. The order is: outer lever in the north room up; lever in the south room up (go in by the east door, leave by the west); back in the north room pull the outer lever down, go in and lift the inner lever up; leave and lift the outer lever up again; last, the south room's lever down (enter by the west door, leave by the east). Out of order is the usual way to stall here, and once the east room opens it stays open. Level 28 hobgoblins attack you on the way in below combat level 57, and level 53 ogres can get in the way below 107, but all of them only use melee, so Protect from Melee stops their damage. Mine two perfect gold ores there (40 Mining). The level 122 hellhounds in that room have a short aggression range, so zigzag past them; the wiki adds that you can safespot for about 10 minutes until they stop being aggressive, and then they leave you alone while you mine the southern rocks. If one has already locked on, hide behind the rock column directly south of the entrance and hop worlds. A rock takes a while to respawn, so mine one, move to another or hop. A death here leaves your pile in the dungeon, so treat it as a no-death room: Protect from Melee and food at hand.

        Done whenyou hold two perfect gold ores.

        The Al Kharid furnace and Avan

        1. Take the ring of dueling to Emir's Arena and walk to the Al Kharid furnace. Smelt the two ores into perfect gold bars (40 Smithing), then make one perfect ruby ring and one perfect ruby necklace with the moulds and the cut rubies (40 Crafting each). Choose each item once: the wiki warns that make-all makes two of each, and avoid the plain gold ring and necklace in the menu.
        2. Mine one silver ore in the Al Kharid mine, smelt it, cast an unstrung holy symbol with the holy mould (16 Crafting) and string it with a ball of wool. Sell the three moulds back to Dommik.
        3. Give Avan the ring and the necklace for his crest piece. He then tells you where Johnathon is.
        Done whenyou hold Caleb's and Avan's crest pieces and the strung holy symbol.

        Johnathon

        Teleport to Varrock and walk to the Jolly Boar Inn north-east of the city. Upstairs, in the north-western room, Johnathon is poisoned. Use the antipoison on him, and since he takes the whole dose whatever its strength a one-dose potion is enough; it is used up. He tells you where his piece is: Chronozon has it, by the Earth Obelisk in the Wilderness part of the Edgeville Dungeon, which is the next block's first fight.

        Done whenJohnathon is cured.

        Coins: about 155,000 gp · Slots: about 20/28 · Requirements: 40 Mining for the perfect gold, 40 Smithing for the bars and 40 Crafting for the ruby jewellery and 59 Magic for Chronozon's Fire Blast (all boostable); 16 Crafting for the holy symbol

        Wiki referencesFamily Crest/Quick guideFamily CrestHoly symbolDommik's Crafting Store

        Edgeville and the Gnome Stronghold: Scorpion Catcher, Family Crest and Monkey Madness I

        Gate in31 Prayer; 59 Magic (boostable) for Fire Blast; the raw lava eel, the scorpion cage and the strung holy symbol; Caleb's and Avan's crest pieces; Blast spell runes and an air staff; The Grand Tree and Tree Gnome Village for Monkey Madness I.
        Carry inthe Monkey Madness I item list and three free slots for the diamonds: about 26/28.
        Exitthe lava eel cooked; the holy symbol blessed; the monastery scorpion caught; Family Crest and Monkey Madness I done; the goldsmith gauntlets collected; Gnome Stronghold diary tasks done; about 140,000 gp.
        Carries forwardthe cooked lava eel (Heroes' Quest), the goldsmith gauntlets (Blast Furnace), premade chocolate bombs, three diamonds.

        Edgeville and the monastery

        Take fairy ring DKR to Edgeville. Cook the lava eel on a range or fire there. Have Brother Jered, in the Edgeville Monastery, bless the holy symbol you strung in Al Kharid; he is on the upper floor, so the 31 Prayer (not boostable) needed for the stairs applies to him as well. The monastery scorpion is on the same floor, either at the top of the south-east ladder or in the bedroom directly north of it. Catch it with the cage for Scorpion Catcher. Use the cage on it. The wiki only warns of a 3 Hitpoint sting for the Taverley scorpion, so keep a few Hitpoints spare in case this one does the same.

        Done whenthe lava eel is cooked, the holy symbol is blessed and the scorpion is caught.

        Chronozon and Family Crest

        In the Edgeville Dungeon, defeat Chronozon for Family Crest. He has to be hit with Wind, Water, Earth and Fire Blast, so bring an air staff and the runes for all four. He is level 170. A spell that splashes doesn't count and the chat message "Chronozon weakens" confirms each one, so bring runes for several casts of each. Poison spiders can attack on the way to him, so carry a poison cure for yourself too. The wiki's route is the trapdoor in the south-east corner of the building south of the Edgeville bank, then north and east through the skeleton gate, north through the thug gate past the thugs, west past the red spiders and black demons and south to the Earth Obelisk. The four spells can land in any order, but the weakness can wear off, in which case you weaken him again. Once all four have landed, finish him with any style; the wiki's safespot for Magic or Ranged is one square in front of the eastern standing torch, and if the spiders go for you, stand behind the stalagmite and hop worlds. This is the Wilderness, so use a quiet world: another player can attack you here, and a PvP death sends your items to the killer. Bring Caleb's and Avan's crest pieces from the last two blocks. Teleport to Varrock and give the crest to Dimintheis to complete the quest: 1 quest point, 2,000 XP each in Mining, Smithing and Crafting, and steel gauntlets. The brothers imbue the gauntlets for free the first time (Caleb for cooking, Avan for goldsmith, Johnathon for chaos), and changing them later costs 25,000 coins. You can hold only one effect at a time, so take Avan's goldsmith imbue, the only one this guide uses, and know that Dimintheis hands out a replacement pair if you lose them. Collect the goldsmithing gauntlets from Avan (ring of dueling to Emir's Arena, then walk to Al Kharid): they are for the Blast Furnace block.

        Done whenFamily Crest is complete and the goldsmith gauntlets are collected.

        Monkey Madness I

        Start Monkey Madness I with King Narnode Shareen in the Grand Tree (Chapter 1 no longer starts it) and complete it. The item list for it is food and prayer potions, four or more energy potions, antipoison, a gold bar and a ball of wool for the monkey amulet, a lockpick, a wind staff with chaos, law and water runes, an axe, a tinderbox, a knife, an oak log, a big fishing net and a bow string. Along the way, for the Karamja diary, catch a bass and chop and burn teak and mahogany logs on Ape Atoll. Follow the wiki's steps to set aside greegree materials for Recipe for Disaster and Monkey Madness II if you have room, because Ape Atoll is a long trip. On Crash Island, Lumdo refuses at first: talk to Waydar (not his 'Travel' option) and then to Lumdo again. On the island nearly everything is aggressive until you wear the monkey greegree, and hugging the coast reduces snake and spider fights. Don't close the story tablet with its X, because that cancels the cutscene and you repeat the chat option, and finish all of Garkor's dialogue before you use items on Zooknock, or he won't accept them. Don't talk to the Monkey's Uncle upstairs, who calls the guards. If a greegree conversation is cancelled, ask how to make the monkey talisman to pick it up again, and if you lose the talisman tell the Monkey Child so for another.

        Treat the jungle demon as a no-death fight: a death sends you to Falador with your pile on Ape Atoll. It is level 195. Fight with Magic, pray against its Magic and stay three or more squares away, which the wiki says avoids its melee (it hits up to 32 with both magic and melee, and the right prayer brings that to zero). Don't wear the 10th Squad sigil until you are ready, because wearing it is what starts the fight. If you lose the sigil, Waymottin at the end of Zooknock's tunnel returns it. You must land the final blow yourself: if a gnome does, the demon heals. The wiki also notes a safespot across a bridge on the outside edge of the arena. Afterwards, talk to Daero and choose Attack and Defence: those two get 35,000 XP each and Strength and Hitpoints get 20,000 each.

        Rewards3 quest points, 10,000 coins and 3 diamonds, so keep 3 slots free.
        Done whenMonkey Madness I is complete and Daero's XP is chosen.

        The Gnome Stronghold diary tasks

        In the Gnome Stronghold:

        • buy a stack of premade chocolate bombs from Gianne as food for the next fights, and one premade toad crunchies;
        • make a chocolate bomb for the diary;
        • fletch an oak shortbow for the diary. Keep it only if you want to woo the princess in Throne of Miscellania later;
        • score a goal in Gnome Ball, and do a lap of the Gnome Agility Course;
        • take a delivery order for the gnome restaurant, take the agility shortcut to Otto's Grotto for the diary, and complete the delivery for the diary;
        • kill terrorbirds for Rag and Bone Man II.

        Don't buy 20 potatoes, 10 cabbages and 10 onions in sacks at Grand Tree Groceries for Dream Mentor and Mourning's End Part II, and don't collect 50 or more toad's legs: those quests are much later, so buy the vegetables, and collect the toad's legs, when they are next.

        Done whenthe Gnome Stronghold tasks are done.

        Coins: about 140,000 gp · Slots: about 26/28 for Monkey Madness I · Requirements: 31 Prayer for the monastery's upper floor; Blast spells for Chronozon; The Grand Tree and Tree Gnome Village for Monkey Madness I

        Wiki referencesScorpion CatcherFamily CrestGoldsmith gauntletsMonkey Madness IKaramja DiaryKandarin Diary

        Haunted Mine, Heroes' Quest and the end of Animal Magnetism

        Gate inPriest in Peril, 15 Agility and 35 Crafting for Haunted Mine; for Heroes' Quest, a partner from the other gang, the fire feather and the cooked lava eel; the mithril axe and blessed holy symbol; the hard leather and the polished button.
        Carry inthe Iban's staff with death and fire runes, food and potions, and two free slots inside the mine: about 22/28.
        ExitHaunted Mine, Heroes' Quest and Animal Magnetism done; the salve amulet and Ava's device; about 135,000 gp.
        Carries forwardthe crystal-mine key (the Lair of Tarn), the salve amulet.

        Haunted Mine

        RequirementsCutting the salve shard needs 35 Crafting (boostable): use your chisel on a crystal outcrop in the south-east corner of the crystal room, then use the ball of wool on the shard.

        Take the Shades of Mort'ton minigame teleport and do Haunted Mine, which lies in the south-west corner of Morytania with the Zealot who starts it standing outside. The 15 Agility lets you climb the cart that blocks the front entrance (the southern entrance works without it), and feral vampyres roam near the cart track outside. The items are the Iban's staff with death and fire runes, a ball of wool, a chisel, energy and prayer potions, food and the premade chocolate bombs. Keep two slots free inside, one for the Zealot's key and one for the glowing fungus. Pickpocket the Zealot for the key, which falls on the floor if your inventory is full; it works a valve in the flooded area that starts the water for the lift. The fungus is the only light that works on the lowest level, so take one from the stagnant pool near the ladder on the fourth level, put it in a searchable mine cart and set the Points panel so the cart reaches the ladder. Moving carts hurt if you stand in their path. Treus Dayth is level 95 and the wiki calls Magic the easiest style because of his low Magic defence; he is weak to Wind spells and Crumble Undead. Pray Protect from Missiles against his thrown pickaxes (up to 15 each, up to 10 with the prayer). He turns invisible at times, and a flashing yellow arrow on the minimap shows where he is. Keep clear of the cart tracks, since a cart does up to 9 a tick, and stay away from the cranes, which hit for up to 10 when you stand next to them. Use the eastern door as a safe zone to regenerate when you need to. If you die, your pile waits in the mine and you need a new glowing fungus sent through the mine cart again; the wiki says the levers stay set and you don't need the valve again. A random event that teleports you also destroys the fungus. Keep the crystal-mine key afterwards: the Lair of Tarn block at the end of this section uses it. Don't collect a full inventory of spare salve shards or buy a limestone, a plank and eight limestone bricks from Razmire here: keep only what your salve amulet needs, and buy the limestone items when the building that uses them is next.

        UIM riskIf the cart sinks, the fungus is lost and you send another.
        UIM riskThe fungus turns to ash if you leave the cave with it or drop it, and a random event teleport destroys it.
        Rewards2 quest points, 22,000 Strength XP, the salve amulet, a shortcut to the crystal outcrops and access to Tarn's Lair.
        Done whenHaunted Mine is complete and you hold the salve amulet and the crystal-mine key.

        Heroes' Quest

        Go to Brimhaven for the gang mansion. Heroes' Quest needs a partner from the other gang; use the "osrs soa" clan chat or the Join button on the Grouping interface's Shield of Arrav category to find one. The wiki says the armband steps need none of the quest's skill requirements, and that someone who has finished the quest can go back and help. Follow your gang's steps from the wiki: the Black Arm Gang needs full black armour (helm, platebody, platelegs) to enter as Hartigen, and the Phoenix Gang needs a ranged or magic setup to shoot Grip. The Black Arm side is given the key by Grip and trades it to the Phoenix partner, who must land the killing blow: Black Arm members can never kill Grip, and Straven won't accept a candlestick until the Phoenix player has killed Grip. The treasure chest comes up empty if you already carry a candlestick. If you keep Grip's keyring after helping someone else, drop it and ask Grip for a new one. Return to your gang leader in Varrock for the armband, then take a games necklace to Burthorpe and give Achietties the fire feather, the cooked lava eel and the armband to complete Heroes' Quest. Kill a blue dragon for the diary while you're near Taverley. The wiki warns that blue dragon fire can hit for up to 50 without protection, so bring an anti-dragon shield.

        Done whenHeroes' Quest is complete.

        Finish Animal Magnetism

        You're in Burthorpe, so give Turael the mithril axe and the holy symbol for the blessed axe. He takes only a mithril axe, and take care in his dialogue not to accept a Slayer task you don't want. If you lose the blessed axe, Turael makes another from a new mithril axe and holy symbol. Return to Draynor Manor, chop the undead trees around the manor with the blessed axe, which is the only axe that cuts them, for undead twigs, and give them to Ava. The wiki doesn't say how many twigs she wants, so keep chopping until she is satisfied. Translate her research notes, then combine the hard leather, the polished button and the pattern into a container and give it to her.

        Rewards1,000 Crafting, 1,000 Fletching, 1,000 Slayer and 2,500 Woodcutting XP and Ava's device: the attractor below 50 Ranged, the accumulator at 50 or more.
        Done whenAnimal Magnetism is complete and Ava's device is worn.

        Coins: about 135,000 gp · Slots: about 22/28 · Requirements: Priest in Peril, 15 Agility and 35 Crafting for Haunted Mine; 55 quest points, Shield of Arrav, Lost City, Merlin's Crystal, Dragon Slayer I, 53 Cooking, 53 Fishing, 25 Herblore and 50 Mining for Heroes' Quest

        Wiki referencesHaunted MineHeroes' QuestAnimal MagnetismBlessed axe

        Money, Yanille runes, Big Chompy, Zogre Flesh Eaters and the Blast Furnace to 50 Smithing

        This is the most expensive block of 2.1. This block budgets about 835,000 gp, mostly runes and 1,100 gold ore, and you arrive with about 135,000.

        Gate inabout 1,000,000 gp after blackjacking; 30 Ranged (not boostable) for both ogre quests; 49 Crafting for The Hand in the Sand; 66 Magic for the Magic Guild (63 plus a wizard's mind bomb); 40 Smithing for gold; the goldsmith gauntlets and ice gloves; 42 Crafting for fishbowls.
        Carry inthe quest items for each part; for the Blast Furnace, the gauntlets, ice gloves, nature runes and an inventory of ore cycling: about 22/28.
        ExitBig Chompy, Zogre Flesh Eaters, The Hand in the Sand and Freeing Pirate Pete done; 61 Crafting; 50 Smithing; Magic close to 65; the Kandarin easy diary claimed; about 165,000 gp.
        Carries forwardthe rune stacks (law, nature, death, about 40 soul runes), two steel bars (Prying Times and the cannonball task), one raw chompy, the amulet of strength.

        Top up the stack first

        Blackjack Menaphite thugs in Pollnivneach before you start, as in Chapter 1, until you have about 1,000,000 gp. You arrive with about 135,000, so that is about 865,000 gp to earn: at the rate above, roughly 200,000 gp an hour, about four and a quarter hours, and it also pushes Thieving toward the next level. If you'd rather blackjack less, cut the law and death runes below, not the gold ore or the natures: those two are what get you to 50 Smithing and 65 Magic.

        Done whenyou hold about 1,000,000 gp.

        Big Chompy Bird Hunting and Zogre Flesh Eaters

        RequirementsBig Chompy Bird Hunting: 30 Ranged (not boostable), 5 Fletching and 30 Cooking (both boostable).

        Do Big Chompy Bird Hunting, killing the nearby wolves for Rag and Bone Man II. Bring an axe you can use (the wiki says the blessed axe doesn't work), 100 feathers to be safe (or 50 coins for 25 from Fycie), and a knife and chisel, or 10 coins to buy both from Bugs. The cabbage, tomato, onion, potato and leaves are found during the quest, and a Draynor cabbage doesn't work. Save one raw chompy for later and keep it on you: nothing confirms that raw meat survives a deathpile.

        Rewards2 quest points, 262 Fletching, 1,470 Cooking and 735 Ranged XP, an ogre bow and the ability to make ogre arrows.

        Rantz is on the eastern coast of the Feldip Hills, south-east of Gu'Tanoth (fairy ring AKS is the quick way). Talk to him before anything else, or you can't make the arrowtips, and ask every question he offers before you enter the cave north of him: skip one and the chest inside won't open later. Each failed attempt on the chest drains 1 Strength, and you should search it the moment it unlocks. Wolf bones come from the level 64 wolves in the crater south-west of Rantz, with a safespot by the roots in the north-east of the swamp, and Rantz only accepts arrows you made yourself. The bellows fill at the swamp bubbles at the south end of the pond (one fill gives three uses), you can hold three bloated toads at most, and you must shoot the chompy yourself, because a bought raw chompy doesn't advance the quest. If the bait disappears with no bird, drop another toad. Rantz wants three ingredients in all, and one bird is enough. If you pick a seed instead of a vegetable, pick another plant.

        Done whenBig Chompy Bird Hunting is complete and one raw chompy is kept.

        Zogre Flesh Eaters

        RequirementsZogre Flesh Eaters: 30 Ranged (not boostable) and 4 Smithing and 8 Herblore (boostable), and 5 free slots near the start, where the wiki recommends 5 to 10.

        Then do Zogre Flesh Eaters, using rings of dueling to speed up the travel, and kill zogres for Rag and Bone Man II along the way. Below 89 Combat, level 44 zogres and skogres may attack, and their disease lowers a random stat by up to 10 every 18 seconds. Slash Bash is level 111 and takes only 25% damage from most attacks: Crumble Undead does 50%, and he is fully vulnerable to brutal arrows. He can be safespotted, but his ranged attack still reaches you, so use Protect from Missiles (40 Prayer); his attacks drain Prayer while you pray against them. The wiki's tip is to sell the black prism to Yanni Salika in Shilo Village for 5,000 coins instead of 2,000 from Zavistic Rarve. Give Uglug a 2-, 3- or 4-dose relicym's balm to unlock Uglug's Stuffsies. Don't buy 16 relicym's balms there and decant them into 12 four-dose potions: those are 12 slots, so come back for them when a step that needs them is next.

        Rewards1 quest point, 2,000 XP each in Fletching, Ranged and Herblore, and the ability to make relicym's balm and brutal arrows.

        Grish waits in Jiggig, south of Castle Wars and east of fairy ring BKP, and hands you two super restore potions and three cooked chompy birds at once, which is why the free slots matter from the first conversation. The ogre guard east of him lets you over the barricade. In the dungeon the lectern gives a torn page, a level 39 zombie next to the skeleton drops a ruined backpack holding a tankard, rotten food and a knife, and the knife opens the coffin with the black prism. The wiki's ironman advice is to drop the rotten food straight away and the knife once the coffin is open. Take the tankard to the Dragon Inn's bartender, then the prism and page to Zavistic Rarve at the Wizards' Guild (ring the bell on the eastern side of the building if you lack 66 Magic). Sithik Ints is in the guest house north of the guild, in the western-most room upstairs. When you draw his portrait, he must admire its 'honesty': if he admires its 'truth', draw another with fresh papyrus from his drawers. Rarve keeps the necromancy book, the H.A.M. book, the tankard and the portrait when he gives you the strange potion, and you should drop the sample bottle after you poison the tea. Slash Bash appears when you search the stand at the end of the long room below; stand east of the stand first, and if he despawns after five minutes search it again. Grish takes your key when you go back to him, so collect everything from the boss room before then. If you die, he gives you another ogre gate key.

        Done whenZogre Flesh Eaters is complete and Uglug's Stuffsies is unlocked.

        Glassblowing to 61, The Hand in the Sand and the Yanille runes

        Crafting to 61 before 2.2: glassblowing at Port Phasmatys. Nothing earlier in 2.1 trains Crafting, and 2.2 needs it: The Fremennik Isles needs 46 Crafting and Lunar Diplomacy 61. From about 46, where Chapter 1 leaves you, that is roughly 235,000 XP: about six hours at the wiki's rate, and more without a bank. Do it before the Blast Furnace below: the Yanille rune store (law, death, nature and soul runes) needs The Hand in the Sand, which needs 49 Crafting, and the gold-bar alching at the Blast Furnace needs those nature runes.

        The wiki's UIM Crafting guide recommends glassblowing at Port Phasmatys for this range. Ectophial there (it lands you at the Ectofuntus outside the town), buy buckets of sand and soda ash from the Trader Crewmembers on the charter ships (about 13 of each fills an inventory with the glassblowing pipe; the shop holds only 10 of each and restocks on a one-minute cycle, and each port has its own shop), plus a glassblowing pipe from the same trader if you don't have one; smelt them into molten glass at the furnace just south-west of the docks, and blow the best item your level allows, dropping the products. The wiki's guide expects about 38,000 to 45,000 XP an hour; with no bank and world hops for stock, plan on less; hop worlds when the traders' stock runs low. Ghosts Ahoy is done by now, so the town gate no longer charges ecto-tokens. The Blast Furnace isn't an option for this: on its official worlds the dwarves do the maintenance, so nothing breaks, and running the furnace alone on another world is too slow to be worth it. The traders sell sand and soda ash at 5 gp each (wiki Trader Stan's Trading Post), so each piece of glass costs about 10 gp; at roughly 53 XP a piece the 256,000 XP that glassblowing has to give is about 4,800 pieces, which is about 50,000 gp of materials. When the glassblowing passes 42, blow two fishbowls and keep them: one for Freeing Pirate Pete, one for the Kandarin diary's pet fish (see below).

        Done whenyou have reached 49 Crafting, with two fishbowls blown.

        The Hand in the Sand and the Yanille runes

        The Hand in the Sand, the Magic Guild store and the Yanille runes. Crafting passes 49 during the glassblowing, which is what The Hand in the Sand needs (49 Crafting, not boostable; talk to Bert near the sand pit in Yanille; it gives 9,000 Crafting XP and access to the Wizards' Guild rune store). This stop comes after the first stretch of glassblowing and before the Blast Furnace for that reason: stop blowing glass as soon as you reach 49, take the Watchtower Teleport to Yanille and finish the quest. Bert lives in the house next to the house portal by the sand pit, and the Guard Captain he sends you to is in the Ye Olde Dragon Inn, where a beer is the price. Ring the bell on the eastern side of the Wizards' Guild and Zavistic Rarve comes out to you, so the 66 Magic isn't needed for the quest itself. Later the quest sends you to Brimhaven to pickpocket Sandy for sand (expect several tries, and keep the sand), to Betty's shop in Port Sarim for the truth serum and, last, to Entrana for Mazion's wizard's head. For the serum, add the redberries to the water before the white berries, and use the rose-tinted lens on Betty's counter from the doorway; the lens shatters, which is expected. The quest also asks for 17 Thieving, which you passed long ago, and the wiki lists white berries and a lantern lens as items to bring. Cold War and While Guthix Sleeps need a lantern lens again later, so blow a second one at 49 Crafting if you don't have a spare. White berries come from a whiteberry seed at 59 Farming, cave crawlers (10 Slayer), chaos druid warriors in the Yanille Agility Dungeon (40 Agility), and ground spawns on Lava Dragon Isle or in Isafdar. Entrana bars most weapons and armour with bonuses, and the wiki suggests leaving them in the Port Sarim deposit box, but this guide handles Entrana with the Falador Castle deathpile, as in the ice gloves block earlier, so carry only what the quest needs. Then walk into the Magic Guild, which needs 66 Magic, with a wizard's mind bomb (+3 Magic from level 50 up; the Rising Sun Inn in Falador sells it for 3 gp, and you left Chapter 1 at about 63 Magic, so it takes you to the 66 the guild asks for). Buy 300 law runes, 1,100 nature runes plus about 200 more for the gold-bar alchs below, death runes with whatever budget is left (the store sells them at 180 gp, so 3,000 would cost 540,000 gp alone), and about 40 soul runes at 300 gp each (12,000 gp), which pay for the Arceuus teleports in 2.2 (Respawn Teleport costs 1, Salve Graveyard Teleport 2). A UIM's rune pouch takes three stacks and the rest sit in your inventory as one slot each. If you'd rather blackjack less, buy fewer law and death runes, not the natures: those are what get you to 65 Magic. You already have a rune pouch from the Mage Training Arena, so skip the Last Man Standing trip for one. Then go back to Port Phasmatys and finish the glassblowing to 61. You stay on the standard spellbook until a block tells you to swap.

        Done whenThe Hand in the Sand is complete, the runes are bought and the glassblowing has reached 61.

        Gold at the Blast Furnace

        Go to the Blast Furnace with the goldsmith gauntlets and the ice gloves, on a themed world where the dwarves run the furnace and the fee is paid into a coffer (about 72,000 gp an hour). You pay it into the coffer by the bank chest, it drains only while you stand in the basement (also while the chest window is open), unspent coins can be taken out at any time, and walking up the stairs stops it. On other worlds the foreman asks 2,500 gp per 10 minutes instead, doesn't carry the payment across a world hop, and the ring halves it only if you finish his dialogue. You already have Keldagrim access from The Giant Dwarf, and fairy ring DKS leaves you a short walk from the entrance. The ring of charos (a) only halves the foreman's fee on normal worlds, where you would have to work the pumps and pipes yourself. Buy gold ore and smelt it until 50 Smithing, about 1,100 ore; the goldsmith gauntlets make gold the best XP per ore at this level. Carry ore in, put it on the belt, and take the bars off the dispenser with the ice gloves on, then switch to the goldsmith gauntlets in the withdrawal message before you close it: the XP is given when the message closes, and the gauntlets lift gold from 22.5 to 56.2 XP a bar. Ice gloves still on at that moment cost you more than half the XP. The furnace takes only 28 primary ores at a time and gives an error if you add more before collecting the bars, so empty the dispenser every cycle. The furnace is a safe zone on PvP worlds.

        The UIM difference is the bars. A UIM can't bank the bars and can't hold more than an inventory of them, so alch each load of gold bars as it comes off the dispenser (see below) rather than keeping it: the XP is the point. Keep a handful only if a step soon needs them.

        Don't smelt 20 steel and 27 mithril bars here for later steps. Make only what the next blocks use, because each bar is a slot. The exception is six iron bars (one iron ore each on the belt) for the skiff's iron helm in the Sailing block that follows. Two steel bars are enough for now: one for Thurgo in Prying Times and one for the Port Phasmatys cannonball diary task, and you have the ammo mould. The rest (the weathervane in One Small Favour, the Cold War clockwork, the silvthrill rod's mithril bar, the grapples) can be smelted on a later Blast Furnace visit when those steps are next; the weathervane's three bars are the exception, because 2.2 smelts those at the Falador furnace.

        Alch the gold bars instead of dropping them. The gold bars are where most of the Magic XP before Lunar Diplomacy (65 Magic) comes from. You can't bank them, so cast High Level Alchemy on each load as it comes off the dispenser, before the next load goes on the belt: 65 Magic XP a bar, at the cost of a slower Smithing rate, so the Yanille visit above buys 1,100 nature runes, plus about 200 more. You left Chapter 1 at about 63 Magic, and alching all 1,100 bars (about 71,000 XP) still leaves you roughly 10,000 XP short of 65. Superheat the last couple of hundred ores instead of using the belt (53 Magic XP each, then alch the bars they make) to close the gap, and check your Magic before 2.2. The wiki's UIM Smithing guide also describes buying gold ore from Ordan and superheating it, which trains Magic and Smithing in one cast.

        Fishbowls, Freeing Pirate Pete and Harry's pet fish. Chapter 1 left these for here, because a fishbowl needs 42 Crafting. You blew the two fishbowls during the glassblowing above, once it passed 42: one for Freeing Pirate Pete, one for the Kandarin diary's pet fish. Do the errands below once the Blast Furnace is done.

        Done whenyou are 50 Smithing, the gold bars are alched and two steel bars are made.

        Freeing Pirate Pete

        The wiki's requirements for Freeing Pirate Pete are 31 Cooking (boostable), Another Cook's Quest and the ability to beat five level 30 monsters. Bring at least three raw cod (catch them with a big fishing net at the Catherby shore, where the next errand is anyway; keep one seaweed from the same net for Harry's fish), a pestle and mortar, at least three bread (steal them from the baker's stall in the East Ardougne market, 5 Thieving), a knife, an empty fishbowl, a regular needle (the costume needle doesn't work) and three bronze wire (4 Smithing; smelt three bronze bars from copper and tin ore first, since each bar makes one wire). While diving you must weigh under 27 kg (the quick guide's figure; the full page says 28), carry no cat and have at least seven free slots (ten recommended), so leave heavy gear behind. In the Lumbridge Castle dining room, Pirate Pete is already started and the cook already asked about the recipes; if you skipped the cook's questions, inspect Pete and talk to the cook again. Grind a cod with the pestle and mortar and cut a bread into breadcrumbs, then use the Fishing Trawler minigame teleport to Port Khazard. Murphy turns the fishbowl into a fishbowl helmet and a diving apparatus; wear both and ask him to take you diving. You can't drop items underwater, so don't dive with a full inventory. Kill a crab for its meat, grind the crab meat and the kelp separately, return to the cook, make and cook the fishcake (take extra of each, as it can burn) and use it on Pirate Pete without eating it. If it burns, grind new crab meat and kelp, cod and bread; you don't need new wire or hides. It gives 1 quest point and 1,000 XP in Cooking, Crafting, Fishing and Smithing.

        UIM riskUnderwater, pick kelp from a tall plant (take extra), find Nung in the crab pen to the north, pick up five rocks by the mudskipper cave on the western wall, kill five mudskippers, give Nung the hides before you surface, because hides you still hold when you leave are lost, and then the needle and wire to be let into the pen.

        For Harry's pet fish, talk to Harry in his Catherby fishing shop with a fishbowl filled with water, the seaweed from your big-net fishing (Chapter 1 dropped its seaweed stack) and 10 gp; he gives you the pet fish, the last Kandarin easy diary task. Then teleport to Camelot, claim the Kandarin easy diary reward and put its lamp on Herblore.

        At 50 Crafting you can also make the amulet of strength that the green-dragon setup in 2.2 uses: a ruby amulet from a ruby and a gold bar at a furnace with an amulet mould, strung with a ball of wool, then Enchant Level 3 (49 Magic). If you skipped Enakhra's Lament in the sandstone block, do it now, with your Crafting past 50.

        Go past 61 if you can, because Sins of the Father needs 56, green d'hide bodies 63 and Chapter 3 needs 70, and the chapter ends at 68 Crafting. From 77 Magic, Superglass Make is the wiki's UIM method for the rest; 2.3 comes back to it before Chapter 3.

        Done whenFreeing Pirate Pete and the Kandarin easy diary are complete.

        Coins: about 1,000,000 gp after blackjacking, about 165,000 gp after this block (about 700,000 gp of runes and gold ore, about 72,000 gp of coffer fee, 12,000 gp of soul runes and about 50,000 gp of glassblowing materials) · Slots: about 22/28 with an inventory of ore cycling · Requirements: 30 Ranged, 30 Cooking and 5 Fletching for Big Chompy Bird Hunting; Big Chompy Bird Hunting, Jungle Potion, 30 Ranged, 30 Fletching, 8 Herblore and 4 Smithing for Zogre Flesh Eaters; Priest in Peril and a glassblowing pipe for Port Phasmatys glassblowing; 40 Smithing for gold bars

        Wiki referencesBlast FurnaceUltimate Ironman GuideBig Chompy Bird HuntingZogre Flesh EatersGoldsmith gauntlets

        Sailing: Pandemonium, Prying Times, Current Affairs and the skiff to 39

        Sailing eases a UIM's storage problem a little. Your ship's cargo hold is storage you can return to, and the wiki's UIM restrictions on it were removed on 18 February 2026, but it only takes Sailing supplies (salvage, cargo crates, charting supplies, repair kits, cannonballs), cider, beer glasses, whirlpool surprises and fish with their tools and bait. It doesn't take gear, potions, bars, quest items or noted items. From this block on, keep repair kits, cannonballs and fish in it; everything else stays on you, in the costume room or in a deathpile.

        Gate inPrying Times: 30 Smithing and 12 Sailing (not boostable), a steel bar and a redberry pie; Current Affairs: 22 Sailing (not boostable), 10 Fishing and 50 coins; 15,000 coins for the skiff; 31 Sailing and 23 Construction for the teak hull; 30 Sailing for the Tempor Tantrum; 40 Sailing and 30 Fishing for Crab Quest.
        Carry inthe ring of charos (a), the build materials one set at a time (the teak hull's final load is about 17 slots), 25 repair kits in the cargo hold.
        ExitPandemonium, Prying Times, Current Affairs and Crab Quest done; the skiff with iron helm, oak mast, teak hull, inoculation station and keg; 39+ Sailing (about 46-47 after Crab Quest); the Ardent Ocean charted; about 115,000 gp.
        Carries forwardthe cargo hold contents (repair kits, whirlpool surprises), the medallion fragment, the storm cruiser's outfit.

        Pandemonium and the first 14 levels

        Keep the ring of charos (a) on. Take the Pest Control minigame teleport and the boat to Port Sarim, then start and complete Pandemonium. It has no requirements. Talk to Will or Anne on the south docks of Port Sarim and take the salvage job, sail south through the Bay of Sarim to the east side of the Karamja Shipyard, trim the sails when a gust arises, un-set them when Will says you have arrived and deploy the salvaging hook, which drops you in the water. Junior Jim then gives you the raft for the old cup, and a hammer and a saw lie in the crates just south of him. The cargo courier part needs free hands, so unequip your weapon. Trim the sails whenever a burst of wind comes: it gives XP and speed, and you count on that XP to reach 22 later. Do port courier tasks between the Pandemonium and Port Sarim until 14 Sailing, picking up every task between those two ports and chaining them, and finish the level at Port Sarim. You can alch or fletch while sailing. Complete these charting tasks along the way:

        Rewards1 quest point, 300 Sailing XP plus 100 more for delivering the cargo (400 in all), the raft, a spyglass for the views below, 2 repair kits and 25 sawmill coupons (wood plank).
        • look at the port task board in Port Sarim;
        • interact with the salvaging station in Port Sarim;
        • chart the treacherous rock formation near Draynor Village;
        • use the spyglass for a view of the Wizards' Tower from the north;
        • use the spyglass for a view of the Karamja Shipyard from the east;
        • chart the wreck of the Salty Grouper south-west of Tutorial Island;
        • use the spyglass for a view of the Pandemonium from the cave entrance, and dig up the small key for the salvaging station schematic;
        • use the spyglass to admire Chartin' Charles McAtless in the cave on the Pandemonium, and mine 10 lead ore in the cave and superheat them into the 5 lead bars the teak hull needs. Bars don't fit in the cargo hold, so skip mining lead ore for the steel keel and the salvaging hooks here and buy it at Port Roberts in 2.2, where you buy lead ore anyway.
        Done whenPandemonium is complete, you are 14 Sailing at Port Sarim and the five lead bars are made.

        Prying Times

        RequirementsPrying Times needs 30 Smithing and 12 Sailing (neither boostable) and the Captain's log, and Thurgo uses up a steel bar and a redberry pie, so have both on you before you start.

        The wiki also lists The Knight's Sword as a requirement and an open port task slot, because Steve Beanie adds the task to your Captain's log; with no free slot he can't give it. A hammer is in the crate by Junior Jim if you have none. Make the bar at the Blast Furnace in the block before this (one iron ore and one coal there). Buy the pie at the Pie Shop in the Cooks' Guild (32 Cooking and a chef's hat to enter), or bake one from a pie shell and a redberry (10 Cooking).

        Rewards1 quest point, 1,000 Smithing and 800 Sailing XP, forgotten-drink charting, 25 sawmill coupons (oak plank) and unlimited crowbars from the crate of crowbars.
        1. From Port Sarim, charter to the Pandemonium and start Prying Times.
        2. Charter or sail back, doing another courier task if you sail.
        3. Pick up the crate of looty from the ledger table on Port Sarim's dock and return to the Pandemonium with a second courier task on the way. Carry the crate in the cargo hold, then take it out and deposit it on the ledger table on the Pandemonium docks.
        4. Charter to Port Sarim and give Thurgo, in his hut with the anvil at Mudskipper Point south of the port, a steel bar and a redberry pie; with a hammer he makes the crowbar, and a hammer can be found during the quest.
        5. Return to the Pandemonium.
        6. Take your raft to the sealed crate, charting the wreck of the Pandemonium II and the crashed gnome glider to the west.
        7. Open the crate, sail to the Pandemonium docks and drink the drink. The level 14 drink troll appears when you drink; you can kill it or disembark, and if you return to the buoys before drinking you skip it altogether.
        8. Complete the quest, then search the nearby crate for the waterlogged journal and read it to unlock the Medallion of the Deep.
        Done whenPrying Times is complete and the waterlogged journal is read.

        Charting to 22 and Current Affairs

        Chart around the area along the 14–22 charting route, finishing at Catherby at 22 Sailing; it starts deep in the Lumbridge Basin, and don't forget the spyglass for Keep le Faye just north of it. Whenever you chart, collect the drinks and only drink them after docking. The wiki's sealed crate page says which drinks have negative effects.

        RequirementsCurrent Affairs: 22 Sailing (not boostable), 10 Fishing and Pandemonium, and the wiki's item list is 50 coins (Harry in the easternmost building of Catherby sells the mayoral fishbowl and tiny net for that) plus charcoal, which the cabinet in the council office gives you if you didn't bring any.

        Then start and complete Current Affairs. On the form, any answers work, but you must repeat the same ones later. Have your boat docked at Catherby: the duck is released from the ripple just west of the Obelisk of Water and ends at the shore by Holgart. For the current duck charting, you don't need to follow the duck: set it loose and pick it up at the endpoint, where the wiki says it despawns two minutes after it stops; if you lose it, it reappears in the tools compartment of your boat's cargo hold. That lets you combine it with other sailing nearby.

        Rewards1 quest point, 1,400 Sailing and 1,000 Fishing XP, current charting, the current duck and 25 sawmill coupons (oak plank).
        Done whenCurrent Affairs is complete.

        The skiff and the Ardent Ocean

        1. Buy a skiff from a shipwright (15 Sailing, 15,000 coins) and build an iron helm (3 oak planks and 6 iron bars; 17 Sailing and 14 Construction) and an oak mast and linen sails (10 oak logs, 40 iron nails and 5 bolts of linen; 24 Sailing and 11 Construction) on it; all four levels are boostable. The iron bars come from your last Blast Furnace load; buy the 40 iron nails from any sawmill's construction supplies shop (5 gp each) rather than smithing them; spin 10 flax into linen yarn at a spinning wheel and weave the yarn into the 5 bolts of linen at a loom (12 Crafting; the Falador farm has one); and chop the oak logs into your log basket. If you aren't 24 Sailing yet, chart nearby until you are before building the mast. The two sets of materials are about 25 slots together, so carry and build one at a time.
        2. Buy 25 repair kits and put them in the cargo hold, and repair at every shipwright you pass as well.
        3. Chart most of the Ardent Ocean along the 24–30 route, which starts near Catherby.
        4. After the Ruins of Unkah, drink the bottle of slug's mind balm last: it teleports you to Witchhaven.
        5. Run to the Ardougne docks, charter to Musa Point, recover your skiff and sail the second leg past the current duck spots. Dock at the Pandemonium.
        Done whenthe skiff has its iron helm and oak mast, 25 repair kits are in the hold and you are docked at the Pandemonium.

        Tempor Tantrum and the Unquiet Ocean

        At 31 Sailing, upgrade the skiff with a teak hull at the boat schematics: 10 teak hull parts, 300 steel nails, 20 swamp tar and the 5 lead bars, with 31 Sailing and 23 Construction (both boostable), for 345 Construction XP. Each hull part is 5 teak planks made at a shipwrights' workbench with a saw and a hammer (23 Construction, 225 XP), so that is 50 teak planks. The parts don't stack, so the final load is about 17 slots: ten parts, the nails, the tar and five bars. If you aren't 31 yet, sail south and complete the swordfish rank of the Tempor Tantrum for it, then upgrade.

        Done whenthe skiff has its teak hull.

        The Tempor Tantrum trial

        Requirements30 Sailing (not boostable), a skiff with at least an iron helm and an oak mast, and no crew aboard (Rum-dashed Ralph can dismiss them).

        Keep doing laps of the Tempor Tantrum (unlock every difficulty, then the marlin rank for XP) until at least 35,000 Sailing XP (level 39). Each rank must be earned before the next. Laps are worth 595, 1,025 and 1,790 XP at swordfish, shark and marlin, and beating the target times of 1:48, 2:51 and 4:30 gives a one-time 1,000, 2,000 and 3,000 XP. Collect the stormy key (swordfish), the barrel stand and a whirlpool surprise (shark) and Ralph's fabric roll (marlin). The stormy key opens the chest in Pandemonium Cave, next to the mining area: the first opening gives the storm cruiser's outfit, 10 noted uncut sapphires and 10,000 coins, so have slots free; if you lose the key, Ralph gives you another.

        From the Tempor Tantrum, chart a good part of the Unquiet Ocean along the 39–45 route. At the end, make sure your ship is stationary before drinking the bottle of alone at sea: it plays a 75-second cutscene while the ship keeps moving. Teleport out, take the minigame teleport to Port Khazard, recover the skiff and build an inoculation station. Complete the mermaid diving charts in the Oo'glog Channel and Gu'tanoth Bay, which finishes the Ardent Ocean charting.

        Done whenyou have 35,000 Sailing XP (39) and the stormy key chest is opened.

        Crab Quest and the Unquiet Ocean

        RequirementsCrab Quest needs 40 Sailing and 30 Fishing, has no enemies and recommends 9 empty slots, so arrive with them free; the big fishing net is given during it.

        At Dognose Island, dock, start and complete Crab Quest, and open the rusty chest for a medallion fragment. This also unlocks coral farming.

        Rewards1 quest point, 10,000 Sailing XP, 3,000 Fishing XP and the shell collection.
        1. Teleport home and charter to the Pandemonium. Collect the Ardent Ocean XP reward from Chartin' Charles McAtless and buy 48 whirlpool surprises in the Pandemonium pub.
        2. Build a keg on the skiff and fill it with whirlpool surprise; spares can go in the cargo hold, which takes them.
        3. Don't buy the 25 kraken ink stout yet: they fill the sloop's second keg in 2.2, the cargo hold doesn't take them and they would be 25 slots until then. Buy them from Charles on the way to the sloop.
        Done whenCrab Quest is complete, the Ardent Ocean reward is collected and the keg is full.

        Coins: about 115,000 gp · Slots: your inventory plus the cargo hold · Requirements: the Sailing levels above; 31 Sailing for the teak hull

        Wiki referencesSailingCargo holdPandemoniumPrying TimesCurrent AffairsSealed crate

        Rellekka, Slayer to 50 Defence and Barbarian Assault

        Gate inThe Fremennik Trials; about 200,000 gp from an hour of blackjacking; 45 Defence for the berserker helm; 50 Attack for the ancient staff; a Barbarian Assault team; 50 Defence and 50 Strength to wear the granite body.
        Carry inmelee gear and Slayer kit: about 18/28.
        ExitFremennik easy diary claimed; 50 Defence; a Penance Queen kill; the berserker helm and granite body, worn (and the fighter torso if you stayed); about 145,000 gp.
        Carries forwardone pet rock (the Waterbirth doors).

        The Fremennik easy diary and pet rocks

        Break a house tablet to Rellekka and collect the Fremennik easy diary reward (lamp on Herblore; the wiki makes it 2,500 XP in a skill you have at level 30 or above) from Thorodin, who stands just south of Rellekka. Get a few pet rocks from Askeladden in Rellekka: he gives you one after The Fremennik Trials and another whenever you talk to him again, so a lost rock costs you a walk, not a quest. Put one in your menagerie and collect it again for the diary. The wiki lists that pet rock task under the medium Fremennik diary, so it is banked now and pays out when you claim the medium diary later in this chapter. Keep one pet rock with you: it is your partner for the Waterbirth Island doors in the Dagannoth block, where it stands in for a second player. You hold about 115,000 gp here, and the berserker helm (78,000 gp) plus the granite body in the Barbarian Assault part below (95,000 gp) need 173,000 gp between them, so blackjack in Pollnivneach for an hour (about 200,000 gp) before you buy either. Then buy a berserker helm from Skulgrimen for 78,000 gp. It is a bridge piece: it carries you through the Rex trip and the blocks after it, until 55 Defence lets you wear the Helm of Neitiznot in 2.2.

        Done whenthe diary is claimed, a pet rock is kept and the berserker helm is bought.

        Slayer to 50 Defence

        Your Attack should be at least 50 by now. Train Slayer, using the ancient staff as a melee weapon, until 50 Defence. If you want the fighter torso for this training, do Barbarian Assault first; the torso needs only 40 Defence to wear, so it works for you well before 50. Slayer suits a UIM: your gear is already on you, and the task drops you don't keep can stay on the floor. The 50 Defence is also the Defence half of what the granite body asks for just below, and the Helm of Neitiznot in 2.2 wants 55.

        Done whenyou are 50 Defence.

        Barbarian Assault

        Take the Barbarian Assault minigame teleport and do at least one full queen run in any role, then buy a granite body from Commander Connad for 95,000 gp (it needs 50 Defence and 50 Strength, and you must have killed the Penance Queen once). Equip it straight away: the Kandarin hard diary task is to purchase and equip a granite body, the wiki counts the buying and the equipping as two steps, and completing or updating any other diary task in between resets the task's progress. Done like this, the task is ticked now and the body can be alched later without a second purchase. The minigame is at the Barbarian Outpost, just north of Baxtorian Falls, and Connad sells from the Outpost's ground floor. The queen needs a full team of five to make the omega eggs, so join a Barbarian Assault clan chat for a team, and do or skip Captain Cain's tutorial first, since you must speak to him before you can play. If no clan chat has room, join the arrow room, which matches you automatically with Accept Aid switched on. Barbarian Assault is a safe minigame: a death there costs a UIM nothing, it only fails the wave, and a dead teammate makes the group restart the wave from the beginning. Prayers are off, most healing consumables are banned, and the wiki says a rune pouch or looting bag can't be taken in even when empty, and no runes or ammunition either. A UIM has nowhere to leave them, so use a planned deathpile: make one Falador teleport tablet at your lectern first (it is a tablet, not a rune), die to a White Knight in the Falador Castle courtyard as before, pick up everything except the rune pouch and your rune stacks, and take the Barbarian Assault minigame teleport. One full queen run fits inside the pile's hour; break the tablet when it ends and collect the pile. The death destroys the Chronicle, so buy it back from Perdu or Diango afterwards. If you stay for the fighter torso, repeat this for each hour of games. The 50 Defence and 50 Strength are for wearing the body, not for buying it, and the fighter torso costs 375 points in each of the four roles plus a queen kill. The queen appears on wave 10 once the other penance are beaten or after four minutes, and only omega eggs hurt her: the collector passes yellow eggs to the healer to dip in poison, the attacker spikes them, the defender dips them in lava and the collector loads the finished eggs into the egg launcher. Eight omega egg hits kill her, and one role is doubled to make the five. Staying for a fighter torso is worthwhile; use Ranged as Attacker even with weak shortbows, and flexible or defensive gear in the other roles. The torso is a Strength body that costs no inventory space once you wear it, so a UIM loses nothing by getting it now. The torso gives +4 Strength and the granite body none, so the torso is the better body for training.

        UIM riskYou keep everything you carry in, but anything you drop by hand inside is lost.

        The Barcrawl you completed before Scorpion Catcher is what lets you onto the Barbarian Outpost agility course, where Scorpion Catcher's last scorpion is, and Horror from the Deep needs it too; both are in the next block.

        Done whenthe Penance Queen is killed and the granite body is bought and worn.

        Coins: about 145,000 gp after the blackjacking hour, the granite body (95,000 gp) and the berserker helm (78,000 gp) · Slots: about 18/28 in melee gear · Requirements: The Fremennik Trials for Skulgrimen's shop; 50 Defence and 50 Strength to wear the granite body, with the Penance Queen killed once

        Wiki referencesFremennik DiaryPet rockBarbarian AssaultFighter torsoGranite body

        The Barbarian Outpost, Horror from the Deep and Dagannoth Rex

        Gate inAlfred Grimhand's Barcrawl; 35 Agility (boostable) for the pipe; the scorpion cage; the Horror from the Deep item list; a pet rock and a rune thrownaxe; Iban's staff (u) with charges.
        Carry inthe Rex setup in the fourth step, plus a second pet rock and a spare thrownaxe. Plan it as a no-death trip.
        ExitScorpion Catcher and Horror from the Deep done; the berserker ring; about 145,000 gp plus drop alchs.
        Carries forwardthe berserker ring (imbue next block).

        Scorpion Catcher and Horror from the Deep

        Catch the last scorpion inside the Barbarian Outpost agility course for Scorpion Catcher. The wiki puts it in the north-eastern gated building of the course, wandering just inside the entrance, and the Barcrawl is what lets you in. Do a lap of the course for the diary; return the cage to Thormac to complete the quest when you next pass the Sorcerer's Tower. That is in 2.2, in the What Lies Below block, so there is no detour now: Thormac is on the tower's top floor, south-east of the Ranging Guild.

        Rewards1 quest point and 6,625 Strength XP, and from then on Thormac turns a battlestaff into a mystic staff for 40,000 coins.
        Done whenthe last scorpion is caught.

        Horror from the Deep

        Then do Horror from the Deep, which Larrissa starts outside the Lighthouse, north of the Barbarian Outpost. You cross to the Lighthouse over basalt platforms that fail often and can hurt for up to 10 damage each time, so bring food, or take fairy ring ALP to the island. The items are a bronze sword and an arrow (one of the iron arrows from Underground Pass), an air staff with earth, water, fire and chaos runes, a molten glass, a tinderbox, a hammer, 60 steel nails and a swamp tar, plus two planks from the plank sack for the bridge. Use a plank on the near side of the broken bridge east of Larrissa, jump across and use the second on the far side; the bridge looks unchanged afterwards, so check the quest log. Then go through the pipe on the Outpost agility course (the 35 Agility) and tell Gunnjorn that Larrissa needs her spare key to get the Lighthouse key. The sword (any sword or longsword except a rusty or prop sword), the arrow (not ogre or training arrows, and bolts don't work) and the four runes are used up on the door in the basement cavern: use the four runes, then the sword, then the arrow, in that order. Only one of each rune is taken, so full stacks are safe to carry, but the sword and arrow don't come back, so choose ones you can spare. The first dagannoth, level 100, can be safespotted from the southern corner of the pond to the west. It comes out of the sea when you suggest going back upstairs for help after Jossik is hurt on the ladder, and killing it brings out the Mother. The Dagannoth Mother, also level 100, changes colour every 18 seconds and can only be damaged by the matching style: white air, blue water, brown earth, red fire, orange melee and green ranged. Keep Protect from Missiles on for the whole fight: she uses ranged at a distance and melee up close, and may switch if she sees you change prayers. Earth spells hurt her most because her Magic defence is very low. While she is the wrong colour, wait in a safespot or up the ladder and heal. Killing her ends the quest and drops a rusty casket, which Jossik upstairs holds for you if your inventory was full. The quest gives no Fremennik shield; Chapter 3 gets one from Bardur with a ring of charos (a) for The Fremennik Exiles. The quest is also required for the Culinaromancer in Recipe for Disaster and for the medium Fremennik diary.

        UIM riskInside, the Lighthouse is an instance: put the molten glass and swamp tar on the lighting mechanism on the second floor and light the tar with the tinderbox.
        UIM riskDon't drop anything to make room in her cave: the wiki warns that dropped items are unobtainable once she is defeated, though if you die before killing her you can still loot your items.
        Rewards2 quest points, 4,662.5 XP each in Magic, Strength and Ranged, a damaged book of your choice, and the Lighthouse shop and the dagannoth caves below it.
        Done whenHorror from the Deep is complete.

        Dagannoth Rex for the berserker ring

        Get the berserker ring from Dagannoth Rex. Bring the pet rock for the dungeon doors (the wiki says it stands in for a second player on the button) and a rune thrownaxe for the doors that block the ladder (Authentic Throwing Weapons in the Ranging Guild sells them for 572 gp; buy two, so the spare below is covered): its special attack bounces between the three doors and breaks them all. The wiki describes three paths east from the entrance cavern, each ending at a door with two buttons that need two players: giant rock crabs (level 137, melee) hold the north path, Wallasalkis (level 98, magic) the middle and ranged dagannoth (level 88) the south. Drop the pet rock in place of the second player. In the central room three door supports block the ladder, and you can't knock down the one in front of you, so use the thrownaxe special from the north or south path on the middle door. The entrance cavern is full of level 90 dagannoth, and the rock crabs and lobsters pose as rocks until you walk up to them, so keep Protect from Melee on. A second way in at the island's highest point needs 85 Agility, far above you. Reach Rex's safespot solo by "xlogging": pray Magic on entering. If Prime is on you, go back up. If not, run south and swap to Protect from Missiles, run along the south edge to the east side, trap Rex in the safespot, sip a prayer potion to at least about 40 points and close the client. Wait up to a minute for Supreme to walk away, then log back in. You are then on the east side with only Rex in range. The wiki agrees: Prime wanders east and north-east, so stay in the south-eastern part of the arena and attack when Rex is nearest the east; Prime's area attack can still kill you there.

        The setup is the granite body, rune platelegs, Iban's staff with death and fire runes, berserker helm, amulet of magic, antipoison and premade chocolate bombs; the tome, cape and helm can be downgraded, steel gloves are better than mystic gloves here, and an adamant kiteshield is a slightly better option than the tome of fire. If the Iban's staff runs out of charges, you have to walk through the Underground Pass to recharge it. Rex is level 303 and hits only in melee, which is why a safespot works at all, and his Magic defence is extremely low while his melee and ranged defences are high, so Iban Blast (50 Magic) is the right tool.

        The UIM differences:

        • Plan a Rex trip as a no-death trip. A death sends you to Falador, with your pile behind the Waterbirth doors, and you have 60 minutes of logged-in time to reach it. Carry a second pet rock and a spare thrownaxe so you can get back in; if you run out of rocks, a ladder in the dungeon leads up to Askeladden, who gives another.
        • Alch the drops. Your gold bars were alched at the Blast Furnace, so a long hunt for the ring is paid by alching Rex's drops; blackjack in Pollnivneach if the stack runs low.

        Optionally stay for a dragon axe, which is also 1 in 128 a kill. Leave by the long route to the Lighthouse for the diary: its ladder at the far end of the dungeon works only once Horror from the Deep is done and leads into the dagannoth caves below the Lighthouse, and kill dagannoths on the way out for Rag and Bone Man II.

        If it doesn't dropthe ring is 1 in 128 a kill, so about one player in ten still has none after 300 kills. Stop there and carry on with the route, using the best other ring you have; the imbue in the next block waits for it, and you come back to Rex between later blocks rather than stalling the chapter.
        Done whenyou hold the berserker ring (or have stopped at about 300 kills) and have left by the Lighthouse.
