        Coins: about 145,000 gp, plus drop alchs · Slots: Rex setup plus a pet rock and thrownaxe · Requirements: Alfred Grimhand's Barcrawl for Horror from the Deep (35 Agility, boostable, for the pipe shortcut); no quest for the Dagannoth Kings, and Horror from the Deep only for the Lighthouse exit

        Wiki referencesScorpion CatcherHorror from the DeepWaterbirth Island DungeonDagannoth RexBerserker ring

        The imbue, Slayer to 40, and the starts of The Slug Menace, Eagles' Peak and Making History

        Gate inthe berserker ring; 60 Attack, Monkey Madness I, a greegree and a diamond for the dragon weapons; about 250,000 gp after half an hour of blackjacking; 30 Crafting, Runecraft, Slayer and Thieving (none boostable) for The Slug Menace; 27 Hunter for Eagles' Peak; the commorb.
        Carry inSlayer gear; for the runes leg, a chisel, the essence pouches, about six loose essence and five free slots (about 17/28).
        Exitthe berserker ring (i); 40 Slayer; the dragon scimitar and dragon dagger; the house in Kourend; The Slug Menace, Eagles' Peak and Making History started; the five blank runes chiselled.
        Carries forwardthe five blank runes (empowered in 2.2), the Eagles' Peak feathers.

        Imbue the berserker ring and train Slayer

        1. Imbue the berserker ring at the PvP Arena, either with a friend on another account (go at off-peak hours, hope to be matched, and trade quick deaths for points) or by losing quickly to random players for points. PvP Arena deaths don't cost items, so this is safe for a UIM: you can't take items in or out, and a death costs nothing, even from a deathbank. The arena is Emir's Arena in Al Kharid (a ring of dueling lands you at its gate), and Mubariz's shop in the welcome centre sells the scroll of imbuing for 200 reward points, on an ordinary world only. A ranked loss pays 12 points and a win 16, with streaks adding up to 10 more. Only fights the game sets up pay anything, so unranked duels and fights you organise yourself give nothing, and since the game picks the opponent a friend only helps if you both queue and happen to be matched. Queue with the PvP Arena button in the Grouping tab and hop to the world you are sent to.
        2. Then alch the granite body, which the wiki values at 48,000 gp for high alchemy, the figure in the coin line below.

        Train Slayer to 40 on easy tasks with the ancient staff, with melee on accurate to 60 Attack. If you cross 70 combat near the end, get a Chaeldar task for the diary. At 60 Attack, buy a dragon scimitar from Daga on Ape Atoll (100,000 gp, and you need a greegree and Monkey Madness I done) and a dragon dagger (Jukat's shop in Zanaris sells it for 30,000 gp; access costs a diamond). You have about 145,000 gp after the granite body and helm, so blackjack in Pollnivneach for about half an hour (roughly 105,000 gp) until you hold about 250,000 gp before you buy the weapons. Finish your last task at the end. Combat levels from here on are estimates, so don't worry about being a few levels off.

        Done whenthe ring is imbued, you are 40 Slayer and you hold the dragon scimitar and dragon dagger.

        The Slug Menace, Eagles' Peak and Making History

        Before the chiselling leg, get back the small pouch that Chapter 1 dropped. The wiki says the Mage of Zamorak will not replace a lost small pouch, but the Dark mage in the Abyss will, free of charge (Astral Contact reaches him too, but you have no Lunar spells yet). Enter the Abyss from the Mage of Zamorak north of Edgeville with everything this trip doesn't need left in the Edgeville Monastery deathpile, as in Chapter 1 and in the altars block of 2.2, go through to the centre of the Abyss, talk to the Dark mage and ask for a new small pouch, then teleport out. Buy the Chronicle back afterwards if the planned death destroyed it. Then check Runecraft: The Slug Menace needs 30, and What Lies Below in 2.2 needs 35. Make up whatever Tears of Guthix hasn't given you at Guardians of the Rift, behind the Temple of the Eye portal (27 Runecraft to play), and spend 30 of the abyssal pearls it pays at the Temple Supplies shop on a mind, an earth and a water talisman (10 pearls each) for the altars in 2.2. The minigame turns the fragments you mine into essence, so you bring only a pickaxe (and a chisel only if you craft guardians).

        1. Then, on the standard spellbook, go to Falador and start The Slug Menace with Sir Tiffy (the commorb must be in your inventory to start; Sir Tiffy can replace it). He upgrades it to a commorb v2, and its contact option is how you travel to Witchaven.
        2. Pay the estate agent to move your house to Kourend.
        3. Teleport to Ardougne with the cloak and start Eagles' Peak at the Ardougne Zoo, where Charlie asks you to find the missing huntsman Nickolaus. The disguise at the end needs 50 coins, a yellow dye and a swamp tar, which later steps bring.
        4. Continue The Slug Menace in Witchaven: Col. Jake O'Niall is on the pier in the north-east of town, Mayor Hobb in the north-west house (finish that talk with "Nothing at the moment, thanks" and the Savant asks you to scan him with the commorb v2), then Brother Maledict and Holgart, and back to O'Niall.
        5. Use the necklace of passage to the Outpost and start Making History with Jorral, in the outpost just north-west of West Ardougne (Priest in Peril and The Restless Ghost are its requirements). The start is a cutscene, after which you visit Erin in East Ardougne, Droalak in Port Phasmatys and Dron in Rellekka in any order, so the quest stays only started here and is finished later in 2.2.

        Walk to Eagles' Peak. Collect two dashing kebbit furs for the Varrock hard diary if your Hunter is high enough, catch a copper longtail for the diary, mine some iron ore, and continue Eagles' Peak until you have to make the disguise, collecting the feathers on the way. The ten feathers are for the two disguises Asyff makes in Varrock in 2.2, so you only collect them now. Leave the jungle eagle's nest for last, since its vine takes about 40 minutes to grow.

        Teleport to Ardougne and continue The Slug Menace until you have chiselled the five special blank runes. The wiki's route is the shrine west of Witchaven: down the stairs, then push the red wall section on the east side to reach level 45 giant lobsters and an imposing door. Don't open the door, which hits you for 5; scan it from beside it with the commorb v2 and pick up the dead sea slug on the floor before you leave. Take the transcription to Jorral with a spare slot free. Three pages complete the set: one from the desk in Hobb's house, one from Ezekial Lovecraft, who owns the fishing shop, and a torn one from O'Niall. Jeb, next to Holgart, takes you to the Fishing Platform, where Bailey in the small south-west room makes sea slug glue from the dead slug. Glue the fragments and turn and flip the pieces until the puzzle closes. The wiki says at least five essence, but advises as many as you can hold because the chiselling can fail (about 60% success at 30 Runecraft), plus a chisel and five free slots. A UIM carries that with the essence pouches: the small and medium pouches hold nine, so with about six essence loose, a chisel and five free slots you need twelve empty slots plus the two pouches, not twenty. Mine the 15 essence at Aubury's rune essence mine first (pure essence above 30 Mining). Leave everything not needed for this leg in a deathpile or the costume room first. Use the chisel option on the glued pages, not the chisel on the essence, and don't throw away the pages or the chisel before the quest is done: the wiki says empowering a rune at an altar can fail and break it, and you replace it from the pages. The runes are empowered at the five altars in 2.2.

        Done whenthe five blank runes are chiselled and all three quests are started.

        Coins: about 120,000 gp after the dragon weapons (about 170,000 gp counting the granite body's 48,000 gp alch), once you have blackjacked up to about 250,000 gp for them · Slots: about 17/28 for the runes leg · Requirements: Wanted!, Sea Slug and the other listed quests, 30 Crafting, 30 Runecraft, 30 Slayer and 30 Thieving (none boostable) for The Slug Menace; 27 Hunter for Eagles' Peak; Priest in Peril for Making History; 60 Attack for dragon weapons

        Wiki referencesPvP ArenaThe Slug MenaceEagles' PeakMaking HistoryEssence pouch

        Miscellania, Royal Trouble and the Lair of Tarn

        Gate inThe Fremennik Trials and Heroes' Quest; an iron bar, a non-silver ring, a favour source and a cake or bow; 9 free slots and 5 coal for the lift; Haunted Mine, the crystal-mine key and 40 Slayer (not boostable) for the Lair of Tarn.
        Carry inmelee gear, food, energy and prayer potions and an antipoison: about 20/28.
        ExitThrone of Miscellania, Royal Trouble and the Lair of Tarn done; the salve amulet (e); about 500,000 gp after blackjacking; the checkpoint below met.
        Carries forwardthe salve amulet (e).

        Throne of Miscellania and Royal Trouble

        Take fairy ring CIP and do Throne of Miscellania and Royal Trouble, killing rabbits for Rag and Bone Man II. The ring drops you on Miscellania, and you start by speaking to King Vargas upstairs in the castle, who asks which child you want to pursue; you can switch later by talking to him again. The items are an iron bar, any common non-silver ring such as a gold ring, a favour source (an axe and 45 Woodcutting for maples, or a pickaxe and 35 Mining for coal), a cake or a shortbow or longbow (cake to befriend, bow to woo the princess; your choice), energy and prayer potions, melee gear and food. Astrid wants you to agree with her until she runs out of questions, then the Blow Kiss emote once she calls you "dear" before the ring. Brand wants flattery, then the cake (don't eat it) and the ring. Impressing either royal too much without talking to them can block progress, which talking to Vargas and switching child fixes. The peace with Etceteria runs alongside: ask Advisor Ghrim, then Queen Sigrid, get the awful anthem from Prince Brand for Ghrim to turn into a good one, take it to Sigrid, and give Derrik (the anvil house on the north shore) your iron bar for a giant nib. The nib goes on logs from the islands' evergreen trees to make a giant pen for Vargas to sign the treaty. Throne of Miscellania needs 75% approval to finish; exactly 2,280 gp of flowers takes you from the base 25% to 75%, and it doesn't need 100%. The wiki's requirements list quotes a different flower number, so check your rating with Ghrim before you go back to Vargas. You start at 25% (less if you killed villagers or stole from stalls), and the labourers don't recognise you until the quest is done.

        Rewards1 quest point, 1,000 XP each in Woodcutting, Mining, Fishing and Farming, and 10,000 coins that go into the Miscellania coffers, not your inventory.
        Done whenThrone of Miscellania is complete.

        Royal Trouble

        Advisor Ghrim in Miscellania Castle starts Royal Trouble, and the quest runs through the Miscellania dungeon. Repairing the lift needs 9 free slots and 5 coal: use the mining prop on the crevice, take the engine, beams, pulley beams and rope from the crates, put the coal in the engine and then build the scaffold, pulleys and platform. Past the lift you collect five burnt diary pages, use a plank on the slippery rocks, avoid the steam vents and falling rocks, and read the finished diary before the last crevice. The Giant Sea Snake is level 149 with 100 Hitpoints and its poison hits 9, so bring an antipoison; 40 Agility and 40 Slayer are boostable and not needed to start. Use Protect from Melee close up and Protect from Missiles at range or when casting; the wiki says 60 Magic with Iban Blast kills it quickly, which your Iban's staff (u) can do. Don't plan on Managing Miscellania afterwards: its resources aren't available to an Ultimate Ironman, so for you the kingdom is just these two quests.

        UIM riskThe fight is an instance, and the wiki says anything left on its floor is lost.
        Rewards1 quest point, 20,000 coins and 5,000 XP each in Agility, Slayer and Hitpoints, handed out after you take the heavy box the snake drops to Sigrid and then to Vargas.
        Done whenRoyal Trouble is complete.

        The Haunted Mine and the Lair of Tarn

        Take the Shades of Mort'ton minigame teleport to the Haunted Mine and use the crystal-mine key for the quick way in. The crystal mine key is the innocent-looking key from the Haunted Mine quest, and it opens the locked room on Level 2 of the Abandoned Mine, which has its own entrance on the surface. Mine a mithril ore in the Abandoned Mine for the Morytania hard diary (55 Mining): the wiki recommends the key because it gives you the Level 2 veins right by that entrance. Then visit the Lair of Tarn Razorlor and complete the miniquest, killing a terror dog on the way for the Morytania medium diary (terror dogs aren't on the Rag and Bone Man II list). Turn Auto Retaliate off in the lair, and walk around or disarm the traps. The entrance is the Haunted Mine cave, west of Burgh de Rott: climb over the mine cart, crawl down the cart tunnel and follow the path north to its end. Floor traps hit for about 7 to 8, trapdoors for about 8 and a fall, wall traps for 7 to 10 and the log traps on the pillar jumps for 15 to 20 with a knockdown; search a trap to disarm it. In the zigzag pillar room two level 94 skeleton mages attack, so use Protect from Magic there. Arrive at Tarn's room with at least 2 prayer potions or 15 pieces of food left. Tarn is a level 69 mutant and then a level 69 ghost; turn on Protect from Melee when the cutscene ends and watch your Prayer, which drains fast. Turn the prayer on before you eat, since food has a delay. Poison carries over from the mutant to the ghost, and Tarn is the only kill the miniquest needs. Two level 110 terror dogs join the fight after the cutscene: the wiki says to position them behind Tarn so only he hits you, and a terror dog killed anywhere in the lair is the diary task. Once Tarn dies the north door opens onto his diary, and you don't have to make the amulet in the boss room.

        UIM riskThe fight is an instance, and the wiki says anything left on its floor is lost.
        Rewards5,000 Slayer XP and Tarn's diary, which turns your salve amulet into a salve amulet (e).

        This ends 2.1. Your stack is low at this point, because the gold bars went into Magic XP rather than coins. Blackjack back up to about 500,000 gp before 2.2 (from about 120,000 gp after the dragon weapons that is about two hours, less with the granite body alch), because 2.2 opens with Barbarian Fishing and doesn't cost much.

        Done whenthe Lair of Tarn is complete and the salve amulet (e) is made.

        Coins: about 500,000 gp after blackjacking · Slots: about 20/28 · Requirements: The Fremennik Trials and Heroes' Quest for Throne of Miscellania; Throne of Miscellania for Royal Trouble, with 40 Agility and 40 Slayer (both boostable, neither needed to start); Haunted Mine and 40 Slayer (not boostable) for the Lair of Tarn

        Wiki referencesThrone of MiscellaniaRoyal TroubleManaging MiscellaniaLair of Tarn Razorlor

        Checkpoint at the end of 2.1. Compare yourself with this before you start 2.2; the figures are the plan's, not guarantees. If you are short on coins, blackjack until you are back on the line; if you are short on a skill, finish the block that trains it.

        • Coins: about 500,000 gp after the blackjacking above. Spellbook: standard.
        • Skills the section aimed at: Crafting 61 (Port Phasmatys glassblowing), Smithing 50 (Blast Furnace), Magic 65 (gold-bar alchs), Slayer 40, Attack 60 (dragon scimitar), Runecraft about 31.
        • Gear you should hold: Iban's staff (u), ancient staff, rune platelegs, berserker helm, dragon scimitar and dragon dagger (the granite body was alched after the imbue; keep the fighter torso if you earned it).
        • Done: Underground Pass, Heroes' Quest, The Hand in the Sand, Throne of Miscellania, Royal Trouble, the Lair of Tarn Razorlor. Started only: The Slug Menace, Eagles' Peak, Making History.

        2.2: Barbarian Fishing, green dragons and the Hallowed Sepulchre

        You start 2.2 with about 500,000 after the blackjacking at the end of 2.1. This section is long and quest-dense: The Fremennik Isles, Lunar Diplomacy, the long middle of One Small Favour, Tai Bwo Wannai Trio, Contact!, most of Recipe for Disaster, Cold War, Legends' Quest, Between a Rock..., Swan Song, then green dragons, Varlamore, Sailing to 50 and the Hallowed Sepulchre.

        Three things make it easier for a UIM than 2.1:

        • Storage. The skiff's cargo hold carries repair kits, cannonballs, spare whirlpool surprises and fish, nothing else. Long-term gear lives in the costume room, and the rest is carried or left in a deathpile. Hespori, at 65 Farming, adds the item retrieval service Chapter 1 pointed to (the seed comes only from farming contract seed packs, so until one does, keep every session inside a deathpile's hour): die in its fight and everything you carry is stored until you collect it (25,000 gp a collection), but any unsafe death while items are stored deletes them. That makes it storage for safe skilling only, so the Falador Castle deathpile stays your planned-death route for 2.2's quests and fights.
        • Stackables stay one slot. Several steps here use items that would normally be carried noted (semi-precious gems for Gabooty, fish offcuts, essence from Wizard Cromperty). A UIM can't note anything, and a bank booth only unnotes, so carry them in a form that costs few slots: cut gems for Gabooty (a cut gem pays five times the trading sticks of an uncut one), fish offcuts, which stack on their own, and essence in your pouches. Anything you do hold noted, such as the 20 mahogany logs from Chapter 1, is unnoted at a bank booth where a step needs it unnoted.
        • Fewer planned deaths. The quest chains here rarely need an empty inventory. The deaths that matter are the ones to avoid: the cat (soon a hellcat) is in several of these quests, so keep it in the menagerie whenever the next steps don't use it.

        Tithe Farm to 85 Farming and the herb runs

        Chapter 1 stopped the Tithe Farm at 62 Farming, because that was all the Hespori and the contracts needed. The rest of the guide needs far more: Song of the Elves wants 70 and it cannot be boosted, the spirit tree in 2.3 wants 83 (boostable), the hard farming contracts start at 85, and a UIM cannot buy tree seeds, so tree runs and contracts alone would leave you around 83 to 84 by the end of the chapter (an estimate from the wiki's seed-pack odds, not a timed figure). The Tithe Farm is the only reliable Farming XP, so go back to it once, now, and let the daily runs carry the rest.

        Gate in62 Farming and the Tithe Farm kit from Chapter 1 (34 Farming to start, 54 for bologano seeds, 74 for logavano).
        Carry inthe farm kit and nothing else you can leave in the costume room.
        Exit85 Farming; A New Leaf done; hard contracts from Jane at the Farming Guild; the daily runs and the herb runs running; about 500,000 gp, as before (the gardeners cost about 3,000 gp a day from here on).
        Carries forwardthe daily runs; the herb routine below.

        The Tithe Farm from 62 to 85

        Use bologano seeds from 54 and logavano from 74: a full sack of 100 is worth about 22,540 XP on bologano and about 37,030 on logavano, bonus included. At about 22 minutes a sack (19 on the wiki's best layout; time your own first sack and rescale) that is about 34 hours for the whole 62 to 85, about 21 hours of it to reach 80, and about 6.5 hours to reach the 70 that Song of the Elves needs. Do the first 6 or 7 hours before anything else in 2.2, and split the rest into sessions of a few hours: nothing here has a deadline, and a contract or bird house can wait the hour or two a session lasts.

        Done whenyou are 85 Farming.

        A New Leaf

        RequirementsA New Leaf: Priest in Peril, 45 Farming, 35 Cooking, 32 Herblore and at least one completed Farming Guild contract, all of which you have.

        You are in Hosidius, so do A New Leaf, a Quest Month quest. Start it by picking and eating a communication cabbage in the cabbage field east of the Hosidius farm patch; Dishop Dennis is at the farm east of the Farming Guild. The quest gives you its cabbages (not Draynor Manor ones), compost (supercompost and ultracompost don't work for its potion), seeds and four bowls; bring a vial of water, a spade, a seed dibber and a rake. It travels to Falador, Hosidius, the Gnome Stronghold, Port Phasmatys and Al Kharid, so the Ectophial and a ring of dueling or glory help. At the end you fight five melee enemies at once (levels 50 to 64) in the wheat field south of Dennis's house: Protect from Melee negates all their damage, and leaving through the hay bales resets the fight. It also protects the Farming Guild allotments from disease for good, and gives seed packs a 1 in 100 chance of an extra seed of growth each time you take seeds, which helps the herb runs below.

        Rewards2 quest points, 10,000 Farming XP, 2,000 Cooking XP, 2,000 Herblore XP and a 2,500 XP lamp for any combat skill.
        Done whenA New Leaf is complete.

        The daily runs

        From 85 on, do these every day you play: the tree, fruit tree and hardwood runs, one hard contract from Jane, and the bird houses. Every seed a contract pack or a seed nest gives is worth planting: yew, magic, palm, papaya, dragonfruit, mahogany and teak are grown in a plant pot into a sapling first (a UIM has no shop sapling; check in game how you get the soil for the pot), and the spirit seed is for the spirit tree in 2.3. Pay the gardeners in coins (about 1,400 gp for a tree run, 2,000 for a fruit tree run), or with your own produce once you have it; allow about 3,000 gp a day, roughly 400,000 gp over the chapter, which is felt in the early blocks of 2.2 because they start near 500,000 gp. Keep the herb seeds for the herb runs below. The wiki's figures put the daily runs plus hard contracts at about 90 Farming by the end of the chapter (an estimate; seed luck decides it), and the Tithe Farm to 94 step in 2.3 covers whatever is left.

        Done whenyour daily runs are running.

        Herb runs that feed Herblore

        RequirementsSong of the Elves needs 70 Herblore, which cannot be boosted, and the lamps and quests in this chapter bring you to only about 64 (an estimate from the lamp values; check yours; A Shell of a Friend and A New Leaf add 4,500 of it).

        About 311,000 XP has to come from potions, about 2,500 potions, so about 2,500 herbs (a few hundred more if you mix in cheaper herbs), and seeds are the limit, not secondaries. From the first herb run of 2.2, plant a seed only when it makes something worth drinking:

        • irit for super attack (45 Herblore, 100 XP, an eye of newt at 3 gp from Jatix in Taverley, who stocks them in bulk);
        • kwuarm for super strength (55, 125 XP, a limpwurt root);
        • cadantine for super defence (66, 150 XP, white berries);
        • avantoe for fishing potions (50, 112.5 XP, snape grass);
        • snapdragon for super restore (63, 142.5 XP, red spiders' eggs from the Forthos Dungeon spawns);
        • ranarr for prayer potions (38, 87.5 XP, snape grass).

        Keep the cheap herbs (guam to harralander) for contracts that ask for them. Grow limpwurt, snape grass and a white berry bush in the patches beside your herb patches, and use ultracompost and magic secateurs on every herb patch (a seed gives about nine herbs, an estimate). Once you hold the herb sack, put grimy herbs straight into it; before that, hand each harvest to the tool leprechaun, who notes herbs, and unnote them at a bank booth. Make the potions at Catherby in rounds: 9 grimy herbs from the sack, clean them, add 9 vials of water, then 9 secondaries, and drop the 9 potions or sell them to the general store, since potions do not stack and nothing here buys them for real money. Do a round for every batch you harvest rather than saving them for one long session. Contract seed packs are the main supply (about 440 herb seeds over the chapter, an estimate from the wiki's pack tables), the Sepulchre's coffins add ranarr seeds as a bonus, and pickpocketing the Master Farmers is a weak fallback (about 10,000 Herblore XP of potions an hour). Mastering Mixology is a poor fit here: its passive refining gives only about 6 XP a herb against 100 to 150 for potions, and the wiki does not say how many herbs a potion order uses, so you cannot size it. Chapter 3 returns to it once the herb supply is not the bottleneck.

        Done whenyou are making potions as you harvest; the checks at Sins of the Father and before the elf quests tell you whether you are on pace.

        Coins: about 0 gp at the farm, about 3,000 gp a day for the gardeners, about 10,000 to 15,000 gp of eyes of newt and vials in all · Slots: about 20/28 at the farm and 18/28 during the potion rounds · Requirements: 34 Farming for the Tithe Farm, 54 for bologano, 74 for logavano; the potion levels above

        Wiki referencesTithe FarmFarming contractSeed packJatix's Herblore ShopUltimate Ironman Guide/Herblore

        Barbarian Fishing to about 88 Fishing

        Do Barbarian Fishing at the Mount Quidamortem spot by the Chambers of Xeric until about 88 Fishing; Agility, which Chapter 1's Canifis laps left near 62, ends up near 69. Reach the spot with the Arceuus Library Teleport (the spell, not Kharedst's memoirs; swap to the Arceuus spellbook first by talking to Tyss at the Dark Altar north of Arceuus, now that you hold soul runes) into the minecart, which is faster than the memoirs to Lovakengj. The barbarian rod is the one under Otto Godblessed's bed in his grotto above Baxtorian Falls. The wiki puts AFK Barbarian Fishing at about 23,000 Fishing XP an hour at 48 Fishing and about 57,000 an hour at 99, so the early levels are the slow ones. Feathers stack, so buy as many as you need in one go.

        Gate inBarbarian Training's fishing section; 48 Fishing, 15 Agility and 15 Strength; the Arceuus spellbook (Tyss) and soul runes for the Library Teleport.
        Carry inthe barbarian rod, feathers, a knife for cut-eating and the hourly kit: about 8/28.
        Exitabout 88 Fishing and about 69 Agility; at least 1,700 fish offcuts in the cargo hold; about 440,000 gp.
        Carries forwardthe fish offcuts (blue crabs later in 2.2), the Barblore progress.
        • Cut-eat the fish for Cooking XP: you need 70 Cooking for Recipe for Disaster's Awowogei subquest, and skipping this means the Mess Hall later, which is slower.
        • Keep at least 1,700 fish offcuts. They stack, and the Hunter block later in 2.2 uses them for crab trapping. Offcuts are used as bait before feathers, so a stack kept in your inventory gets eaten while you fish. Store each batch in your ship's cargo hold, which the wiki says keeps fish offcuts. On a successful cut, trout give offcuts 1 time in 2, salmon 3 in 4 and sturgeon 5 in 6.
        • Barblore. Do as much Barblore as you can. A UIM can't bank the sturgeons from the Mount Quidamortem Barblore, so drop them or make the Barblore products on the spot. Roe is enough for the lower mixes and caviar is needed for the higher ones. Keep them in the bottom row of your inventory and pick "use" right after right-clicking, so you don't eat them.
        • Hourlies. Keep up cats, farming contracts and hardwood and fruit tree runs, and the bird houses; the herb runs are your Herblore plan (see the Tithe Farm block above). If you run low on digsite pendants, buy about 80 rubies in a gem shop (Ardougne or Kourend before Perilous Moons, Cam Torum after) and make them as you need them: a ruby necklace from a gold bar and the necklace mould, enchanted with Lvl-3 Enchant on the standard spellbook. Buy 200 more soul runes if you run out, for teleports and hourlies.
        • It doesn't have to be one block. These levels aren't needed until the Hallowed Sepulchre at the end of 2.2, so you can weave the fishing in between other steps as an AFK activity.
        Done whenyou are about 88 Fishing, with at least 1,700 offcuts stored.

        Coins: about 440,000 gp · Slots: about 8/28 for fishing · Requirements: Barbarian Training's fishing section; 48 Fishing, 15 Agility and 15 Strength to start Barbarian Fishing (salmon at 58/30/30, sturgeon at 70/45/45)

        Wiki referencesBarbarian FishingFish offcutsBarbarian Training

        The Fremennik Isles, the silvthrill rod, and Lunar Diplomacy to the altars

        Gate inThe Fremennik Trials; 20 Construction (boostable), 30 and 46 Crafting, 56 Woodcutting for The Fremennik Isles; the rod mould, a silver bar, a mithril bar and a cut sapphire; 60 Mining (not boostable), 61 Crafting, 65 Magic, 40 Defence, 49 Firemaking, 55 Woodcutting and 5 Herblore for Lunar Diplomacy; 30 Runecraft and the five blank runes.
        Carry inthe Fremennik Isles list, then the Lunar Diplomacy list with the talismans and tiara: about 25/28 at the Fire altar.
        ExitThe Fremennik Isles done with the Helm of Neitiznot (worn from 55 Defence); the silvthrill rod enchanted; the lunar staff made and the five Slug Menace runes empowered; lava runes made; about 430,000 gp.
        Carries forwardthe silvthrill rod (In Aid of the Myreque in Varlamore), the berserker helm until 55 Defence, 45 swamp tar.
        Deathbank checkthis block has fights where a death is unsafe. If Hespori or another retrieval service holds your items, collect them before you start: an unsafe death here deletes them.

        The Fremennik Isles

        RequirementsThe helm needs 55 Defence to wear and you are about 51 or 52 here, so keep the berserker helm on until the Warriors' Guild and green dragon blocks take you to 55, then sell it back to Skulgrimen.

        Use your Fremennik boots to get to Rellekka and do The Fremennik Isles, making a Neitiznot shield along the way for the diary. It starts with Mord Gunnars at the northernmost dock in Rellekka, who takes you to Jatizso and King Gjuki Sorvott IV. Put the XP on Attack, or Defence if you're already 60 Attack. The items are the Fremennik boots, a bronze nail, 6 mithril ore, 9 ropes, a knife, a rune axe, a hammer, a needle with 2 thread, melee gear, food and prayer potions. From the wiki: the ore is 8 tin at Mining 1, 7 coal at 2 to 54 or 6 mithril at 55 or more (ores can be noted), and you also need a raw tuna, which you can fish there with 35 Fishing or buy. The 9 ropes are 8 for the bridges and 1 for the shield, made from yak hair with 30 Crafting. The 8 split logs and the shield need 56 Woodcutting, and the yak-hide top and legs need 46 Crafting. Mawnis Burowgar asks for 8 ropes and then 8 split logs, and the wiki's Ironman note says you hand 8 items in at once, so have each set of eight on you together. Wear the Neitiznot shield as soon as it is made: it cuts the ranged trolls' max hit from 6 to 2, and the eastern route can be fatal without it. Turn on Protect from Missiles before you repair the eastern bridge, because the wiki warns that without it all the damage lands at once afterwards and can kill a low-level player, and a low-level player should not take the eastern bridge back after the Troll King, because powerful trolls wait there. Construction 20 (boostable) is only needed for the bridge repairs. The Ice Troll King is level 122: use melee or Magic (he has a 35% fire weakness) with Protect from Magic, not Ranged or Protect from Melee (he knocks you back while Protect from Melee is up). Kill the ten frenzied ice trolls first, past an unmarked dead-end cave entrance you should ignore, and stay at the edge of the battle so five or more trolls can't surround you; Bork Sigmundson supplies tuna, strength potions and prayer potions, one request per supply.

        Rewards1 quest point, 5,000 Construction, 5,000 Crafting and 10,000 Woodcutting XP, two lots of 10,000 combat XP (Attack, Strength, Defence or Hitpoints, and you may pick the same skill twice) and the Helm of Neitiznot (55 Defence); a replacement helm costs 50,000 coins from Mawnis.
        Done whenThe Fremennik Isles is complete and the Neitiznot shield is made.

        The silvthrill rod

        At the Neitiznot furnace, make the silvthrill rod from a silver bar, a mithril bar and a cut sapphire with the rod mould from In Aid of the Myreque, then swap to the standard spellbook and enchant it with Lvl-1 Enchant (a cosmic rune and a water rune). The wiki asks for 25 Crafting (boostable) to make the rod and 7 Magic for the enchant, and the enchant has to be cast, so don't count on a tablet. Smelt the mithril bar at your last Blast Furnace visit or here from mithril ore and coal. The silver bar is the fifth one from Al Kharid, kept for this; the cut sapphire is one of the ten uncut sapphires from the Tempor Tantrum chest, unnoted at a bank booth and cut. The rod waits in your inventory (one slot) until In Aid of the Myreque finishes in the Varlamore block, where you dip it in the well under Paterdomus with a rope to make the Rod of Ivandis. Making another needs a new silver bar, mithril bar and sapphire, so avoid risky fights until then.

        Done whenthe enchanted silvthrill rod is in your inventory.

        Lunar Diplomacy and the five altars

        RequirementsLunar Diplomacy needs 60 Mining to start, and it can't be boosted.

        The sandstone stop left you about 10,000 XP short, Enakhra's Lament took 7,000 of that and the ores of 2.1 a little more, so you arrive up to about 3,000 XP short (about 10,000 if you deferred Enakhra's Lament). Mine coal in the Rellekka mine, 50 XP a rock (about 60 for 3,000 XP), dropping it as you go, until you are 60 first. Then start Lunar Diplomacy and continue until you have to use your dramen staff on the elemental altars to make a lunar staff. While on the Lady Zay, collect at least 45 swamp tar. The items for this part are a tinderbox, a bullseye lantern (use the lantern lens on your sapphire lantern to swap the sapphire back out), melee gear, food, energy and prayer potions, a clean guam and marrentill, a pestle and mortar and the dramen staff. Lokar Searunner on Rellekka's westernmost dock starts the quest, and Brundt the Chieftain gives you the seal of passage. The Oneiromancer keeps one lunar staff until the clothing is made, so make any spare before you hand one in.

        Before the altars, get what the Fire and Air altar work needs, because nothing earlier in the guide hands it to you:

        • a mind, an earth and a water talisman: the Temple Supplies shop at the Temple of the Eye sells each for 10 abyssal pearls, which the Guardians of the Rift games for The Slug Menace's Runecraft have paid you;
        • a plain tiara: smelt a silver bar with a tiara mould at a furnace (23 Crafting; Dommik's Crafting Store in Al Kharid sells the mould for 100 gp);
        • a binding necklace: make an emerald necklace at a furnace (a gold bar, a cut emerald from a gem shop and your necklace mould) and cast Lvl-2 Enchant on it (27 Magic). It has 16 charges, one per altar click, and makes combination runes certain instead of a 50% chance;
        • water runes for the mist runes Swan Song needs, and the stack of earth runes for the lava runes.

        Now do the altars once for both Lunar Diplomacy and The Slug Menace:

        1. Take a tiara and a mind talisman to the Mind altar (the talisman opens the ruins, and the diary's mind tiara needs both). Empower the blank mind rune for The Slug Menace and make a mind tiara for the diary.
        2. Through the Abyss, visit the Air, Fire, Water and Earth altars in that order. At each, make the lunar staff part and empower the blank Slug Menace rune. With the binding necklace worn, make two sets of combination runes for Swan Song on the way: at the Air altar, use water runes on the altar with the water talisman and 10 pure essence for 10 mist runes; at the Fire altar, use earth runes on it with the earth talisman and 10 more essence for 10 lava runes. Each needs 6 or more Runecraft and uses up its talisman. The Abyss is entered through the Mage of Zamorak in the Wilderness north of Edgeville (the run there is in a PvP area, entering drains your Prayer to 0 and skulls you, and the outer ring is multicombat with aggressive monsters, though the Abyss itself is not PvP), so before you go, leave everything this trip doesn't need in a deathpile outside the Wilderness (the Edgeville Monastery one, as in Chapter 1) and take only the essence, pouches, talismans, the staff parts, the blank runes and a teleport.
        Done whenthe lunar staff is made, and the five Slug Menace runes are empowered.

        Coins: about 430,000 gp · Slots: about 25/28 at the Fire altar · Requirements: The Fremennik Trials, 20 Construction (boostable) for The Fremennik Isles, with 30 Crafting for the ropes, 46 Crafting for the yak-hide armour and 56 Woodcutting for the split logs and shield (40 Agility is only recommended); Lunar Diplomacy's quests and 61 Crafting, 40 Defence, 49 Firemaking, 5 Herblore, 65 Magic, 60 Mining and 55 Woodcutting, none boostable; 30 Runecraft for The Slug Menace runes

        Wiki referencesThe Fremennik IslesSilvthrill rodLunar DiplomacyThe Slug MenaceRecipe for Disaster/Freeing Evil Dave

        The Slug Menace, proselyte armour and the rest of Lunar Diplomacy

        Gate in41 Hunter for graahk; the empowered runes; the Lunar Diplomacy list, with the seal of passage worn.
        Carry inLunar Diplomacy's long item list: about 26/28 for the finish.
        ExitThe Slug Menace and Lunar Diplomacy done; the proselyte set bought; three graahk furs; the Lunar spellbook open; about 390,000 gp.
        Carries forwardthree graahk furs (the medium fur pouch), the proselyte set, the seal of passage.
        Deathbank checkthis block has fights where a death is unsafe. If Hespori or another retrieval service holds your items, collect them before you start: an unsafe death here deletes them.

        Graahk furs for the fur pouch

        Take fairy ring CKR, which lands south of Tai Bwo Wannai Village, and catch horned graahk (41 Hunter, with a teasing stick or hunter's spear, a knife and logs for the trap) until you have three graahk furs; they become the medium fur pouch later. Tatty graahk fur is a different item and the pouch recipe lists graahk fur, so keep catching until you have three intact ones.

        Done whenyou hold three intact graahk furs.

        Finish The Slug Menace

        Teleport to Ardougne (fairy ring BLR), continue The Slug Menace and kill the Slug Prince (level 62, melee only; its attacks drain Prayer by the damage dealt, so bring prayer potions). Savant teleports you to Falador to complete the quest.

        Rewards3,500 Crafting, Runecraft and Thieving XP and promotion to Proselyte: buy the full proselyte set from Sir Tiffy in Falador Park (wearing it needs 30 Defence and 20 Prayer, and a UIM can't take single pieces out of the armour case until the whole set is stored, so keep the set together), which the wiki prices at 25,000 coins as a pack against 30,000 separately.
        Done whenThe Slug Menace is complete and the proselyte set is bought.

        The chainbody and the bowl of hot water

        Buy an adamant chainbody at the chainmail shop and heat a bowl of water on the range on the east side of Falador.

        Done whenyou hold the adamant chainbody and the bowl of hot water.

        Complete Lunar Diplomacy

        Teleport to Rellekka with the Fremennik boots (or fairy ring AJR) and complete Lunar Diplomacy. The items are long: law, air, earth and water runes, the dramen staff, a teasing stick, a knife, logs or an axe, the seal of passage, coins, the bowl of water, a pickaxe, a hammer, a needle with 2 thread, 4 suqah hides, a tinderbox, a spade, potions and food, cosmic and astral runes, a staff of air, a chisel and the abyssal book. For the Dream World, enter through the brazier in the long western building with all nine lunar items and the lunar staff equipped, and use the soaked kindling on the lit brazier; progress is saved if you log out, but you need more kindling to return. If the fight with Me doesn't start, drop and pick up your axe and talk to the guide again. Wear the seal of passage the whole time, so it never takes a slot or gets dropped by mistake: without it Lunar Isle's NPCs send you back to Rellekka. Rimae Sirsalis tans the suqah hides for 100 coins each for the ceremonial clothes. The suqah are level 111; the wiki's safespot is the tree on the path to the Oneiromancer. Equip all the lunar items before the Dream World, or the kindling won't work, and expect no combat equipment there: the wiki says staves, armour and potions can't be used against Me (level 79). A replacement lunar staff costs 30,000 coins once the first one is handed in.

        Rewards2 quest points, 5,000 Magic and 5,000 Runecraft XP, the Lunar spellbook and Astral Altar, and 50 astral runes.
        Done whenLunar Diplomacy is complete.

        Coins: about 390,000 gp · Slots: about 26/28 for the Lunar finish · Requirements: as above

        Wiki referencesThe Slug MenaceProselyte armourLunar DiplomacySeal of passage

        What Lies Below, One Small Favour's middle chain and the end of Scorpion Catcher

        Gate inRune Mysteries and 35 Runecraft (not boostable) for What Lies Below; the last carried harralander for the Guthix rest; a bronze, an iron and a steel bar's ores for the weathervane; the scorpion cage with three scorpions.
        Carry inthe One Small Favour chain items: about 24/28.
        ExitWhat Lies Below started (outlaws done); One Small Favour at Petra; Scorpion Catcher complete; five pigeon cages.
        Carries forwardthe pigeon cages (Eagles' Peak), the folder (Rat Burgiss).

        Start What Lies Below

        Use the chronicle, run east and start What Lies Below: Rat Burgiss stands at the crossroads south of Varrock, wants Rune Mysteries and 35 Runecraft, and hands you an empty folder for five stolen papers. Use the chronicle again, run north-west and kill five outlaws for it, then run to Barbarian Village. They are level 32, and the wiki places them between Edgeville and the Grand Exchange; each drops a paper, and you use the papers on the folder.

        Done whenthe five outlaws are dead.

        One Small Favour as far as Petra

        RequirementsThe Guthix rest needs 18 Herblore.

        Continue One Small Favour from Barbarian Village: grab a cooked meat from the longhall, deal with Hammerspike in the westernmost cave of the Dwarven Mine, past the anvils (the wiki suggests asking "Have you always been a gangster?"; his gang of three, in their mid to high 40s, attacks when you talk to him again, so bring a weapon and armour), then Sanfew on the upper floor of the central house in Taverley, Captain Bleemadge on White Wolf Mountain (he takes the Guthix rest; a level 73 Big Wolf spawns nearby, so keep him between you and it, and use "Talk-to" if you haven't finished The Grand Tree), Arhein in the general store south of Catherby's bank, Phantuwti in the house with the water symbol north of Seers' Village's church (ask "Why can't you get a clear picture?", then "Which special Seers tools do you need?", then "What do you mean, 'special combination of items'?"; repair his weathervane with a hammer and a bronze, an iron and a steel bar; hammer the vane on his roof for three broken parts and smith them at an anvil, with one bar of each smelted at the Falador furnace on the way: bronze from copper and tin, iron from iron ore, steel from iron ore and two coal; the anvil is across the street, and if you lose the parts he sells them back for 1,000 coins), and Petra in the Goblin Cave, which is east of the Fishing Guild entrance: head north along its east wall and search the sculpture. Stop once you have inspected Petra in the rock. For the Guthix rest, carry a cup of hot water, a harralander, two guam and a marrentill; use your last carried harralander here. Open any crystal keys you happen to have on the way.

        Done whenyou have inspected Petra in the rock.

        Finish Scorpion Catcher

        Visit Thormac in the Sorcerer's Tower to complete Scorpion Catcher, then go to Wizard Cromperty in the north-east Ardougne marketplace house with the blue checkerboard floor for One Small Favour. Pick up five pigeon cages along the east wall outside the house south of East Ardougne's northern bank.

        Done whenScorpion Catcher is complete and you hold five pigeon cages.

        Coins: about 385,000 gp · Slots: about 24/28 for One Small Favour's chain · Requirements: Rune Mysteries and 35 Runecraft (not boostable) for What Lies Below; One Small Favour's quests and skills as before; the cage with all three scorpions for Scorpion Catcher

        Ardougne: the emerald lantern, Jimmy Dazzler and the start of Cold War

        Gate inthe emerald lantern and the lantern lens; 49 Firemaking; the cat; Ratcatchers' first segment done; 10 Hunter, 15 Thieving, 30 Agility, 30 Crafting and 34 Construction for Cold War.
        Carry inthe cat, 10 oak planks in the sack, steel nails, a hammer, a spade, the dramen staff and a butterfly net and jar.
        Exitthe Falador medium lantern task done; Jimmy Dazzler's segment finished; Cold War at the penguin suit; the cat back home; about 385,000 gp.
        Carries forwardthe bullseye lantern and both lenses.

        The emerald lens and Jimmy Dazzler

        Have Barnaby take you to Rimmington. Lunar Diplomacy left you with an emerald lantern. Use your lantern lens on it to take the emerald lens back out (the chemist doesn't supply a lens), then light the bullseye lantern in the chemist's house for the Falador medium diary (49 Firemaking), and return to Ardougne. Fetch the cat from your house first if it isn't with you: Ratcatchers continues with Jimmy Dazzler in the house west of the Flying Horse Inn. Jimmy's directions teleport you to the mansion gardens once read, so you need your cat (an overgrown cat won't do) and a free slot. The guards catch you if they see you, whichever way they face, but they ignore the cat; there are three checkpoints, so being seen sends you back to the last one, and rats already caught stay caught. Sneak through the three sections and catch six rats: one in the first room, two in the next before the ladder down (no guards after that), then three across that room and the kitchen to the north. If a rat spawns elsewhere, hop worlds; if the cat sticks on scenery, use Call follower. Then finish that segment with Jimmy.

        Done whenthe lantern task is done and Jimmy's segment is finished.

        Start Cold War

        Start Cold War with Larry at the south entrance of the Ardougne Zoo and continue until you have to make the penguin suit. The items include 10 oak planks and 10 steel nails (your plank sack takes the planks), a hammer, a spade, the dramen staff, and a butterfly net and jar for catching a snowy knight for the diary on the way. Any follower stops Larry's teleport, so if he won't take you, put the cat away first. Put the cat back in the menagerie afterwards; the next block is in the Brimhaven Dungeon.

        Done whenCold War waits at the penguin suit and the cat is home.

        Coins: about 385,000 gp · Slots: about 24/28 for One Small Favour's chain · Requirements: Rune Mysteries and 35 Runecraft (not boostable) for What Lies Below; One Small Favour's as before; Ratcatchers' first segment done; 10 Hunter, 15 Thieving, 30 Agility, 30 Crafting and 34 Construction for Cold War

        Wiki referencesWhat Lies BelowOne Small FavourScorpion CatcherRatcatchersCold War

        Tai Bwo Wannai Trio and the Brimhaven Dungeon

        Gate inJungle Potion and Druidic Ritual, 30 Cooking (not boostable), 15 Agility and 5 Fishing for Tai Bwo Wannai Trio; an iron or better spear and a 4-dose agility potion; a pickaxe for the Shilo Village gem rocks (the machete's gems); 47 Farming for the watermelon.
        Carry inEarth Blast runes, an axe, a small fishing net, coins, the anti-dragon shield, the Trio items and food: about 25/28 in the dungeon.
        ExitTai Bwo Wannai Trio done; the opal machete bought; the scarecrow placed in Morytania and the Morytania easy diary ready; about 370,000 gp.
        Carries forwardthe 2-dose agility potion (Making Friends with My Arm), the karambwan-poisoned spear.

        Rat Burgiss and the Karamja kit

        Return the folder to Rat Burgiss for What Lies Below, then teleport to Varrock and continue it until you have to use the wand on the Chaos altar. Rat sends you to Surok Magis in the Varrock Palace library for a wand and Sin'keth's diary; the altar absorbs 15 chaos runes into the wand, and the wiki lists a route through the Tunnel of Chaos (42 Mining and a bronze pickaxe from Anna Jones) that avoids the Wilderness. For Karamja, carry Earth Blast runes, an axe, a small fishing net (Harry's in Catherby sells one for 5 gp), coins, the anti-dragon shield, the Tai Bwo Wannai Trio items and food, and have Barnaby take you to Brimhaven. Keep the 2-dose agility potion for Making Friends with My Arm later.

        Done whenWhat Lies Below waits at the Chaos altar and you are in Brimhaven with the Karamja kit.

        Tai Bwo Wannai Trio and the Brimhaven Dungeon

        Catch the raw karambwanji on site rather than carrying them in: the wiki's quick guide puts the karambwanji spots north of fairy ring CKR, south of Tai Bwo Wannai, and Lubufu alone wants 20. They stack, so fish a few dozen into one slot with the small net. If you didn't make one earlier, buy a karamjan rum and a banana, slice the banana with a knife and add it to the rum before you leave the island, or the rum breaks; a whole banana makes a useless item. Talk to Lubufu to continue Tai Bwo Wannai Trio. A drop trick gets you two karambwan vessels: bait one with karambwanji and give it to Tiadeche for a raw karambwan, and keep the spare for Tinsay. Kill snakes for Rag and Bone Man II and go into the Brimhaven Dungeon (Saniboch at the entrance charges 875 coins a visit): chop the vines (an axe and 10 Woodcutting), take the stepping stones (12 Agility) and climb the stairs, killing fire giants for Rag and Bone Man II, and use Magic on a bronze dragon for the diary steps.

        Done whenthe dungeon diary steps are done.

        Finish Tai Bwo Wannai Trio

        RequirementsCairn Isle needs 15 Agility for the rock slide, and Tinsay is across the bridge beyond it.

        Then complete Tai Bwo Wannai Trio. Kill jogres for the diary and Rag and Bone Man II, catch a karambwan, explore Cairn Isle and trap a horned graahk on the way. Poison the spear with karambwan paste before you give it to Tamayu, and don't hand it over unpoisoned. Claim the rewards from all three brothers in their houses along the southern fence.

        Done whenTai Bwo Wannai Trio is complete and all three rewards are claimed.

        The machete and the gout tuber trade

        Afterwards, buy an opal machete (or better) from Safta Doc for the diary. The trading sticks come from Gabooty in exchange for semi-precious gems: he pays 60 for a cut opal, 90 for a cut jade and 120 for a cut red topaz, and a fifth of that for uncut ones. Safta Doc wants 300 sticks, three opals and a gout tuber for the opal machete, and the wiki lists a tuber at Gabooty for 240 sticks if you didn't keep one from Tai Bwo Wannai Cleanup, so plan on 540 sticks. A UIM can't note gems, so mine them now: take the cart from Brimhaven to Shilo Village (the ride is itself a medium Karamja diary task) and mine the gem rocks, which need 40 Mining and give one random gem each (a red topaz from them is another diary task), until you hold three opals and five red topaz, cutting them as they come (opal and topaz can be crushed, so mine a few extra). Five cut red topaz cover the 540 sticks, and Gabooty's stock of tubers is often empty, so check before you count on buying one.

        Done whenyou hold the opal machete.

        The scarecrow

        Make a scarecrow for the Morytania easy and Falador medium diaries (it needs 23 Farming to make, and a poisoned spear won't do): use an empty sack on a hay bale for a hay sack, add a bronze spear, then a watermelon (an ironman needs 47 Farming to grow one, which Chapter 1 gives you, so plant one in an allotment on a farm run). Place the scarecrow in the Falador farm flower patch, take it back with a spade and place it in the Morytania flower patch by Canifis; the herb patches don't count. With the scarecrow in the Morytania patch you have finished every Morytania easy task; collect the reward when convenient and put the lamp on Herblore.

        Done whenthe scarecrow stands in the Morytania flower patch.

        Coins: about 370,000 gp · Slots: about 25/28 in the dungeon · Requirements: Jungle Potion and Druidic Ritual, 30 Cooking (not boostable), 15 Agility and 5 Fishing (boostable) for Tai Bwo Wannai Trio (65 Fishing to catch the karambwan yourself), an iron or better spear and a 4-dose agility potion; an opal machete needs 300 trading sticks, three opals and a gout tuber; 47 Farming for the scarecrow's watermelon

        Wiki referencesTai Bwo Wannai TrioBrimhaven DungeonGabootyMacheteScarecrow

        Contact!, A Tail of Two Cats' chores and the Feldip Hills

        Gate inPrince Ali Rescue and Icthlarin's Little Helper; Fire Blast runes; the camulet; 30 Agility for the Agility Pyramid; 31 Hunter for spined larupia; the cat; 40 and 50 Cooking for the chocolate cake.
        Carry ina light source traps can't put out, Fire Blast runes and food; then the chore items with the cat: about 26/28.
        ExitContact! done (Keris locust kill for the desert hard diary); Bob's chores done; the landing strip repaired; four larupia furs and the small fur pouch; about 360,000 gp.
        Carries forwardthe Contact! lamp (Attack or Defence), the fur pouches.
        Deathbank checkthis block has fights where a death is unsafe. If Hespori or another retrieval service holds your items, collect them before you start: an unsafe death here deletes them.

        Making History, Contact! and the Sphinx

        Take the minigame teleport to Port Khazard and continue One Small Favour with Tindel Marchant. Teleport to the Ardougne market and talk to Erin the silver merchant for Making History; he keeps the north-west stall of the central market and won't talk to you if you have recently stolen from his stall, so don't thieve from it on this visit. Carry Fire Blast runes for the fight in Contact!, because fire spells do extra damage to the Giant Scarab, as the next step explains.

        RequirementsThe climb needs 30 Agility, though the first set of rock-climbing obstacles that leads to Simon needs none.

        Take a ring of dueling to Emir's Arena, walk into Al Kharid and talk to Osman, who stands outside the palace, for Contact!. You meet him again outside Sophanem's gates later in the quest. Use the camulet to Enakhra's temple (you have it from Enakhra's Lament by now) and go on to Sophanem. Talk to Simon Templeton, climb the Agility Pyramid and take the golden pyramid for the diary. A slip on an obstacle costs 4 to 10 Hitpoints and most slips also drop you a level of the pyramid, and the desert heat applies inside, so check your desert protection before you start. Collect the golden pyramid (the wiki calls it the pyramid top) before you go through the doorway at the summit: the doorway returns you to the base with no reward and no confirmation prompt, so walking through early costs you the whole climb. You no longer need to press space or click through the dialogue for the diary step, because the wiki says that stopped mattering in June 2022. Sell the Pyramid Plunder artefact and the golden pyramid to Simon. The pyramid top weighs 11 kg, so sell it as soon as you reach him.

        Done whenOsman has sent you on, the golden pyramid is sold and you are in Sophanem.

        The Giant Scarab

        RequirementsThe task also needs 50 Attack and a light source.

        Enter Sophanem and finish Contact!. The dungeon is entered by a ladder inside the temple and then a trapdoor, and the maze beyond is a multicombat area, so don't stop to fight anything in it. The dungeon needs a light source; the wiki strongly recommends one traps can't put out (Kandarin headgear, an abyssal lantern or a bruma torch), and a Sophanem guard sells a torch and tinderbox otherwise. Floor and wall traps in the maze take 10% of your current Hitpoints plus 10, and a scarab trap puts out an ordinary light and drops you to the bottom level, where level 98 and 106 locust riders and many scarab mages wait in a multicombat area: leave that level quickly or teleport out, and relight your light with the tinderbox once you are clear. The Giant Scarab is level 191 and hits up to 20 with melee and ranged, it can poison you even through antipoison, and it summons locust riders and scarab mages. Fire spells do 50% extra damage to it and Iban Blast works well against its low Magic; for Magic, the wiki says to hide in one of the cave's safespots with Protect from Missiles. If you fight it in melee, the wiki advises Protect from Melee, and a cannon can't be used in the instance. Pick up the Keris and kill a locust rider with it near the end; that is a Desert hard diary task, so you claim the diary much later. The Keris is lodged in the scarab after the fight, and Osman gives it back in Al Kharid if you leave without it. It only has to be equipped when the rider dies, so other weapons can do the damage, and the wiki puts one rider just up the ladder in the southern corridor.

        UIM riskThe fight is an instance: on a death your items go outside it, and anything left on its floor is lost.
        Rewards1 quest point, 7,000 Thieving XP and one combat lamp worth 2 × 7,000 XP: put it on Attack, or Defence if Attack is already 60.

        Bring the cat now. Speak with the Sphinx, south-east of the northern pyramid, to progress A Tail of Two Cats: he offers a long cutscene or a brief summary, and either works, but the summary removes the offer of a teleport to Burthorpe, which costs you nothing because you take a games necklace anyway. Then take a games necklace to Burthorpe and do Bob's chores in Unferth's house, in the east of the village just north of the general store: rake Unferth's patch in the yard north of the house and plant four potato seeds (don't wait for them to grow, but the garden chore only counts once they are fully grown, 15 to 35 minutes, which the next block's visit to Burthorpe allows for), make the bed, light the fire with logs and a tinderbox, use a whole chocolate cake and a bucket of milk on the southern table (use them, don't eat them, and a slice of cake does not work), and shave Unferth with shears. The shave animation runs until he is bald, so don't click away from it. The Culinaromancer's Chest (in the Lumbridge Castle cellar, a Lumbridge teleport away) sells the bucket of milk and a chocolate bar, but no cake: buy a cake tin, an egg, a pot of flour and a second bucket of milk there too, bake the cake on a range (40 Cooking) and add the chocolate bar (50 Cooking).

        Done whenContact! is complete and the locust rider is killed with the Keris.

        The Feldip Hills

        Ring of dueling to Castle Wars and continue One Small Favour until the gnome glider landing strip is repaired. Cut the gems from the landing lights; the eight lights take opal, jade, red topaz and sapphire. If you crush one, Gnormadium sells up to 2 spare gems of each type for 500 coins each, but only while the crushed gem is in your inventory, so keep the crushed pieces and buy only after a crush (at most 4,000 gp for all eight spares). The wiki mentions a drop trick to keep the cost down, which you can use here. Catch spined larupia in the Feldip Hunter area until you have four larupia furs (intact ones, for the same reason as the graahk), which is also a diary task, and kill wolves for Rag and Bone Man II. The larupia is caught with a spiked pit, so bring a teasing stick, a knife and logs of any type except redwood. Once provoked it hits back for up to 5 about every four ticks, and the chance of intact fur rather than tatty fur rises with your Hunter level, so some catches give tatty fur that you leave behind. When convenient, make the small fur pouch from the four larupia furs, then upgrade it to the medium fur pouch with the three graahk furs (50 Crafting, 40 XP), both with a needle and thread. The small pouch holds up to 14 furs and the medium pouch holds up to 21, and tatty furs can't be stored in it.

        Done whenthe landing strip is repaired and you hold four larupia furs.

        Coins: about 360,000 gp · Slots: about 26/28 for the chores, with the cat following · Requirements: Prince Ali Rescue and Icthlarin's Little Helper for Contact!; 30 Agility for the Agility Pyramid; 31 Hunter for spined larupia

        Wiki referencesContact!Agility PyramidA Tail of Two CatsCulinaromancer's ChestSpined larupia

        Varrock and Keldagrim: Hooknosed Jack, the penguin suit and Between a Rock...'s schematics

        Gate inthe Rantz step of One Small Favour done; The Giant Dwarf started; 34 Construction for the workbench; the golden cannonball and the dwarven lore book; the cat.
        Carry inthe Ratcatchers list (cheese, poison, milk, marrentill, unicorn horn dust, fish) and the catspeak amulet, worn, then the penguin suit materials: about 25/28.
        ExitHooknosed Jack's segment and Smokin' Joe done; the penguin suit made; three of the four schematics; about 355,000 gp.
        Carries forwardthe penguin suit (Cold War), the schematics (Between a Rock...).

        Burthorpe and Varrock with the cat

        After the Rantz step of One Small Favour, teleport to Burthorpe and progress A Tail of Two Cats (the potatoes should be grown): with the garden chore counted, Unferth insists on seeing a doctor, and that sends you to the Apothecary in Varrock. Then use the chronicle to the Champions' Guild and run to Varrock with the cat.

        Done whenyou are in Varrock with the cat.

        Ratcatchers: Hooknosed Jack and the King rat

        For Ratcatchers at Hooknosed Jack's warehouse in south-eastern Varrock, south of the pub, bring a cat with decent combat stats, since the King rat fight is the cat's. The wiki lists four cheese, rat poison (a vial, red spiders' eggs and a cleaned kwuarm, or have Jack make it), a bucket of milk, a cleaned marrentill and unicorn horn dust, plus fish to heal your cat. Buy the cheese and milk at the Culinaromancer's Chest before you come. Poison the four cheese and place them on the four rat holes (climb the ladder inside the warehouse to reach them), return to Jack, and make the cat antipoison at the Apothecary in west Varrock (marrentill, unicorn horn dust and milk). The Apothecary also gives you the A Tail of Two Cats item you need. Give Jack the antipoison, then fight the King rat upstairs: use your cat on the hole in the wall and heal it with fish through the hole (the wiki recommends 8 to 10 fish, used on the hole, not on the cat). The King rat has only 10 Hitpoints and hits for at most 1, which is why the retreat option plus a steady supply of fish is enough.

        UIM riskChoose "Be careful in there, cat!": the cat then retreats at 1 Hitpoint and you can restart the fight, while "Go get that nasty rat, don't hold back!" fights to the death and can cost you the cat.
        Done whenthe King rat is beaten and the cat is alive.

        The penguin suit and Smokin' Joe

        Make the penguin suit for Cold War after upgrading your workbench, using a steel bar for the clockwork (smelt it at the Blast Furnace, which is next). The suit is made at a clockmaker's bench (crafting table 3): the steel bar becomes a clockwork mechanism, then a toy, then the suit, and Crafting can be boosted for it. The items are two molten glass, a plank, silk and the steel bar. Take the Blast Furnace minigame teleport to Keldagrim. Do the Smokin' Joe part of Ratcatchers (pot of weeds, tinderbox, cat, catspeak amulet): light the pot and smoke the rat hole east of Joe, then use the smouldering pot on the hole again so your cat ambushes the rats. The amulet is only required while Dragon Slayer II is not complete, and if the cat vanishes you can log out and back in or use Call follower. Give the golden cannonball to Dondakan, who stands at the mine in south-west Keldagrim, and collect three of the four schematics for Between a Rock..., using the dwarven lore book for the third page. Dondakan gives you the first schematic, the Dwarven Engineer in western Keldagrim gives the second, and the lore book's last page holds the third. The fourth comes from Khorvak under White Wolf Mountain in the next block, so stop at three here.

        Done whenthe penguin suit is made, Smokin' Joe is done and you hold three schematics.

        Coins: about 355,000 gp · Slots: about 25/28 · Requirements: The Giant Dwarf started for Smokin' Joe; Cold War's workbench needs 34 Construction

        Wiki referencesRatcatchersCold WarBetween a Rock...

        Legends' Quest, One Small Favour to Horvik, What Lies Below and the end of A Tail of Two Cats

        Gate in107 quest points and Legends' Quest's quests; two gold bars; a pickaxe to wield for Slagilith; the Stronghold of Security's floors; the cat; 15 chaos runes and the wand; ten eagle feathers, a swamp tar, a yellow dye and 50 coins for Asyff.
        Carry inthe Cold War and One Small Favour items listed in the steps: about 25/28.
        ExitLegends' Quest started; Petra freed; What Lies Below and A Tail of Two Cats done; the skull sceptre imbued; the eagle disguises made; about 345,000 gp.
        Carries forwardthe Dagon'hai history book (Historian Minas), the eagle disguises (Eagles' Peak), the beacon ring.
        Deathbank checkthis block has fights where a death is unsafe. If Hespori or another retrieval service holds your items, collect them before you start: an unsafe death here deletes them.

        Start Legends' Quest

        Teleport to Burthorpe to progress A Tail of Two Cats (this is Unferth's cure: wear the doctor's hat with white robes (the desert shirt and robe the guide has you carry), take off your weapon and shield, and give him the vial of water as a miracle cure; princess robes and graceful outfit pieces do not work in place of the robes), then take the minigame teleport to Port Khazard and continue One Small Favour. Take fairy ring BLR to the Legends' Guild, talk to the Legends' Guards at the gate (they let you in only if you meet the requirements) and start Legends' Quest with Sir Radimus Erkle, and take five papyrus from the table in the guild during its start, and bring two gold bars for the gold bowls (smelt them from gold ore mined at Rimmington, 40 Mining). Grind a sweetcorn into a pot of cornflour if you have one (Recipe for Disaster uses it). Papyrus can break while you copy notes, which is why you take all five.

        Done whenLegends' Quest is started and you hold five papyrus.

        One Small Favour: Slagilith and Petra

        The One Small Favour steps here run through Tindel in Port Khazard, whose iron oxide Wizard Cromperty in Ardougne turns into the animate rock scroll. Tindel only parts with the oxide once he has his mattress stuffed with chompy bird feathers, which is why Rantz's work in the Feldip Hills came first. In the Goblin Cave near the Fishing Guild, follow the eastern wall to Petra's sculpture and read the scroll in front of it to summon Slagilith (level 92); if the game says there are enemies around when you read it, kill the nearby bats first. Wield your pickaxe: without a pickaxe equipped your damage is reduced by 67%, while with one combat spells do full damage, and if you melee with it, set it to crush. Protect from Melee nullifies its hits (up to 13), and it disappears 5 minutes after it is summoned, so have food and prayer ready before you read the scroll. Read the scroll again to free Petra and send her back to Seers' Village.

        Done whenPetra is freed.

        One Small Favour and Cold War, continued

        Continue One Small Favour, then Cold War until just after the raw cod and ring of charos (a) step, so that the intelligence is next. Larry only teleports you to the iceberg when no follower is with you, so leave the cat out of it. The raw cod or the ring decides how the penguins talk: with the ring the penguin gives you the secret phrase outright, and without it the penguin asks for a raw cod in exchange. The mission report and Crazy penguin outcome depends on whether Between a Rock... or Enlightened Journey is finished, permanently; you reach it with neither done. The items include the doctor's hat, a desert shirt and robe, a vial of water, law runes, a water staff, melee gear with your pickaxe, prayer potions, a hammer, the ring of charos (a), the Ardougne cloak and the penguin suit.

        Done whenCold War has reached the intelligence step.

        One Small Favour from Seers' Village to Horvik

        Continue One Small Favour all the way from Seers' Village to Horvik in Varrock:

        • under White Wolf Mountain, in the White Wolf Tunnel, talk to Khorvak for the fourth schematic of Between a Rock...; he asks for a dwarven stout, but you can refuse and still get the schematic;
        • in Barbarian Village, make a second pot lid for Swan Song: Tassie Slipcast teaches the craft, so use soft clay on the potter's wheel and fire the result in the pottery oven (25 Crafting);
        • in the Stronghold of Security, collect the skull sceptre, imbue it with fancy or fighting boots, and spend one charge teleporting for the diary. Its four pieces drop at roughly 3% each from the monsters of the four floors (minotaurs, flesh crawlers, catablepons and ankou), the two skull halves combine into a strange skull, the two sceptre parts into a runed sceptre, and the skull goes on the runed sceptre. Take the sceptre and the boots to Solztun near the Cradle of Life to imbue it, which needs a Jagex Account or an enabled Authenticator. An unimbued sceptre starts with 10 charges and destroys itself when they run out, so the imbue is what lets you spend the diary charge without losing the sceptre;
        • run north and take the Abyss to the Chaos altar with the wand and 15 chaos runes for What Lies Below, and bring an empty bowl, which Surok Magis needs with the infused wand. Surok says you need a chaos talisman or tiara to infuse the wand, but the wiki notes you don't when you reach the altar another way, and the Abyss is another way: use the wand on the altar and it takes the 15 runes. The Abyss is entered from the Wilderness, so leave the rest of your kit in the Edgeville Monastery deathpile first and go in with only these and a teleport. The Abyss also drains your Prayer points and skulls you, which is a second reason to carry nothing else;
        • at Horvik, finish his part of One Small Favour and complete What Lies Below: take the infused wand and the bowl back to Surok Magis in the Varrock Palace library for his letter to Rat Burgiss, who reveals he commands the palace's Secret Guard and sends you to Zaff for the beacon ring. Have your gear on for King Roald (level 47): when he is low, your character says it is a good time to summon Zaff, so operate the beacon ring then. The rewards are 1 quest point, 8,000 Runecraft and 2,000 Defence XP, the beacon ring and a shortcut to the Chaos Altar. Keep the Dagon'hai history book from the palace library: the quest's 5 kudos are claimed from Historian Minas at the Varrock Museum while you hold it.
        Done whenHorvik's part is done and What Lies Below is complete.

        Asyff's eagle disguises

        Reclaim the herbal tincture from the Apothecary instead of carrying it, and bring two soft clay, five pigeon cages and a dwarven stout from a table. Then go to the south-east corner of Varrock and ask Asyff in the Fancy Dress Shop about bird costumes: for the 10 eagle feathers, a swamp tar, a yellow dye and 50 coins he makes two eagle disguises for Eagles' Peak, each an eagle cape and a fake beak. You wear the whole disguise to walk past the guard eagle in the Eagles' Peak dungeon, which otherwise hits you for small damage. The wiki's UIM note for this quest is to take off your headgear and cape and have 6 free slots besides. Have the shop make the spottier cape too if your Hunter allows: two dashing kebbit furs (69 Hunter to catch, at the Piscatoris falconry area) and 800 coins, and it needs 66 Hunter, unboostable, to wear.

        Done whenyou hold two eagle disguises.

        Finish A Tail of Two Cats

        Hop worlds at the anvils south of Varrock West bank until you find Bob (the cat must still be with you; the wiki lists the Varrock anvil building by the Smithing tutor as one of the three places where Bob tends to get stuck) and, for 2 quest points, a doctor's or nurse hat, a mouse toy and two 2,500 XP lamps for skills at 30 or more, complete A Tail of Two Cats (lamp on Slayer).

        Done whenA Tail of Two Cats is complete.

        Coins: about 345,000 gp · Slots: about 25/28 · Requirements: Legends' Quest's quests and 107 quest points to start, and 50 Agility, Crafting, Smithing, Strength, Thieving and Woodcutting, 56 Magic, 52 Mining, 45 Herblore and 42 Prayer (all boostable) to finish; the Stronghold of Security's floors for the boots

        Wiki referencesLegends' QuestCold WarSkull sceptreWhat Lies BelowA Tail of Two Cats

        Recipe for Disaster in Misthalin, Cold War, Making History and the end of One Small Favour

        Gate inthe hellcat path through Evil Dave; the Lumbridge Guide items; a purple dye; the Cold War list.
        Carry inthe long list for the three quest finishes: about 27/28, so the cat stays at home.
        ExitOne Small Favour, Cold War and Making History done; Evil Dave freed; Awowogei, Sir Amik, the Lumbridge Guide and Skrach started; black gloves bought; about 340,000 gp.
        Carries forwardthe hellcat, the One Small Favour lamps (Slayer).

        Lumbridge: Cold War and Evil Dave

        Get to Lumbridge and continue Cold War. Start and complete Recipe for Disaster's Evil Dave subquest: inspect Evil Dave in the dining room, then take fairy ring DKR to his house in Edgeville, the one west of the bank where his mother Doris lives, with the cat and feed him spicy stew, making more at the Edgeville range if the spice combination needs them (the cat becomes a hellcat; it is the one item here you can't replace cheaply, so keep it out of fights). Evil Dave sits in the basement below a trapdoor, surrounded by hellrats that stole the spices, and your cat catches them: each catch gives one of four spice colours, and seven catches turn the cat into a hellcat. The stew needs all four spices at a dose of one to three each, found by testing one spice at a time: add one dose to a stew and use it on Evil Dave, which uses that stew up, so the method takes up to nine stews. Don't eat the final stew yourself, and finish all of his dialogue when you give it to him; he leaves part of it uneaten, and that stew goes back to Evil Dave in Lumbridge, where you started. Then start Awowogei, Sir Amik Varze (talk to the cook to learn the recipe; without that the Wise Old Man later won't offer the Evil Chicken option), the Lumbridge Guide and Skrach. Buy black gloves. Do not let Larry teleport you to the iceberg, because you'd be stuck there with no easy way back.

        Done whenEvil Dave is freed and the four subquests are started.

        One Small Favour in Misthalin

        Continue One Small Favour's Misthalin steps. In Draynor, talk to the Wise Old Man, in the large house across from the bank, for Sir Amik Varze and ask him about the Evil Chicken. Do this before Swan Song: once that quest is started, its dialogue stops you asking him about the Evil Chicken until you have finished its Repairing the Colony section. Then go to the Wizards' Tower and talk to Wizard Traiborn for the Lumbridge Guide (his quiz enchants the milk, egg and pot of flour; bring those and a cake tin from the Culinaromancer's Chest). In Port Sarim, kill seagulls for Rag and Bone Man II, then talk to The Face, who stands next to the fishing shop, and Felkrash for Ratcatchers until you need the snake charmer; pick The Face's third dialogue option to learn about him, and wear your catspeak amulet for the talks.

        Done whenFelkrash has sent you to the snake charmer.

        Complete One Small Favour

        Complete One Small Favour: charter to Brimhaven, take the cart to Shilo Village, then leave the city; lamps on Slayer. The quest does not count as complete until you have used both reward lamps (10,000 XP each, for skills at level 30 or higher), and if you drop one first, Yanni gives it back. The other rewards are 2 quest points, a steel key ring, the Guthix rest tea, pot lid crafting and the gnome glider route to the Feldip Hills.

        Done whenOne Small Favour is complete.

        Complete Cold War and Making History

        Complete Cold War and Making History, which teleports you around a bit. For Making History, Erin lends you an enchanted key that changes temperature as you walk: in the Castle Wars area, cross both northern bridges and stand about four steps north of the east end of the second bridge, and when the key starts steaming, dig up the chest with your spade and open it with the key for the journal. The wiki's Ironman note is that making your own unstrung sapphire amulet takes 24 Crafting, and a wrong answer in Dron's quiz in Rellekka ends the conversation, so start it again if that happens. If you don't have a spare purple dye, get one in Ardougne for Making History. For Cold War, the wiki's notes: the five feathers must be regular chicken feathers; the cowbells are only obtainable during the quest; crafting the bongos needs 30 Crafting unboosted, even though a boost works for starting the quest and making the penguin suit; and keep a slot free, because with a full inventory you don't get the third report and aren't told. The Icelords are level 51, and Noodle, the black market merchant at the iceberg, sells the ID you need for one swamp tar and five feathers. Larry won't teleport you from the zoo or the sheep pen during the plan stage, so take the boat east of Rellekka. At the end Pescaling Pax cages you with Icelords: kill up to three to escape, because teleporting out sends you back to the cage. The list for these three is the ring of charos (a), the sharpened axe, the penguin suit, a swamp tar, five feathers, the mission report, a mahogany plank, soft leather, the cowbell, food and potions, a spade, the Ectophial, the ghostspeak amulet, a sapphire amulet, the Fremennik boots, the necklace of passage, law runes, a water staff, the enchanted key, a ring of dueling, and air and earth runes.

        RewardsCold War's rewards are 1 quest point, 2,000 Crafting, 5,000 Agility and 1,500 Construction XP, and the Penguin Agility Course.
        Done whenCold War and Making History are complete.

        Coins: about 340,000 gp · Slots: about 27/28 for the three quest finishes, so leave the cat at home · Requirements: as for each quest

        Wiki referencesRecipe for DisasterOne Small FavourCold WarMaking History
