        The desert easy diary, Ratcatchers' end and Between a Rock... in the Wilderness

        Gate inKalphite Hive access by fairy ring; the hellcat; the ring of charos (a); for Between a Rock..., 30 Defence, 40 Mining and 50 Smithing, a pickaxe and no gold in your pack; 7,500 coins and three gold bars for the Wilderness helmet.
        Carry infor the Wilderness, only 7,500 coins, three gold bars, a slash weapon, a hammer, a pickaxe, melee gear, prayer potions and food; everything else in a deathpile outside.
        Exitthe desert easy diary claimed; Ratcatchers and Between a Rock... done; the Wilderness gold helmet smithed; about 340,000 gp; the quest's rune pickaxe replaces your adamant one.
        Carries forwardthe gold helmet (quest reward).
        Deathbank checkthis block has fights where a death is unsafe. If Hespori or another retrieval service holds your items, collect them before you start: an unsafe death here deletes them. In the Wilderness, a death to a player also hands most of what you carry to the killer.

        The desert easy diary

        Take fairy ring BIQ, go into the Kalphite Hive and collect five potato cacti for the diary (the wiki's tip is to drop and pick up a single cactus five times), then return to the Shantay Pass and collect the desert easy diary from Jarr (lamp on Slayer). Entering the hive is itself an easy task, and the wiki lists a rope for it. Claim the easy tier now: each tier has to be finished before you can claim the next one's rewards, and the Eagles' Peak block claims the medium.

        Done whenthe desert easy diary is claimed.

        Ratcatchers: the snake charmer and Port Sarim

        Fetch the cat and continue Ratcatchers with the snake charmer in Pollnivneach (Ali, who goes by Badir since The Feud, south of the bar). Talk to The Face first, or the charmer won't discuss the alternative; then use a coin on his money pot, and he gives you a music scroll and snake charm for 100 coins, or 50 with the ring of charos (a), which also skips the walking-away step. On any herb run that passes Port Sarim, stand on the docks (not beside the hole), compose the scroll's tune on the snake charm and play it, then finish Ratcatchers there by talking to Felkrash in the Port Sarim Rat Pits, and enter the rat pits for the diary. You can't play the tune without the scroll. Your cat is a hellcat by now, from Evil Dave; the wiki accepts any cat or hellcat, and only an overgrown cat is barred from the first two sections.

        Rewards2 quest points, 4,500 Thieving XP, a rat pole and the rat pits minigame teleports.
        Done whenRatcatchers is complete and the rat pits task is done.

        Complete Between a Rock...

        Then complete Between a Rock..., which finishes with the fight in Dondakan's rock in the Arzinian Mine. The fight is timed: you have about 8 minutes to mine and kill the avatar, and its form depends on your highest combat stat, so choose your gear and protection prayer to match. Mine 15 pieces of gold there (the wiki's minimum is 6), as the wiki highly recommends: it lowers the Arzinian Being from level 125 to 75. Use Prayer and bring food; if you are close to dying, removing the gold helmet teleports you out next to Dondakan. Keep a slot free before the last hit, because with a full inventory the reward helmet drops on the ground.

        UIM riskThe wiki warns that any gold ore or bars in your inventory disappear when you enter that mine, so smith the Wilderness gold helmet in the section below before it, or enter with no gold, and wear that gold helmet into the rock: the wiki requires a gold helmet equipped to be fired in.
        Rewards2 quest points, 5,000 XP each in Defence, Mining and Smithing, a rune pickaxe and a gold helmet.
        Done whenBetween a Rock... is complete.

        The gold helmet in the Wilderness

        Smithing a gold helmet in the Wilderness Resource Area counts for the Wilderness medium diary and needs only partial progress in the quest, but you can't have another gold helmet in your inventory or bank when you smith it: the wiki says it must be dropped first. The helmet you smith here is the one you wear into Dondakan's rock, so smith it first; the quest's reward helmet comes only after the fight. The entry fee is 7,500 coins (6,000 after the medium diary, 3,750 after the hard). Take only 7,500 coins into the Wilderness, three gold bars (smelted beforehand from Rimmington gold ore), a slash weapon, a hammer and a pickaxe (an adamant one is preferable to a rune one, and the diary accepts any pickaxe), melee gear, prayer potions and food. While you are in the Wilderness, restore a prayer point at the Demonic Ruins for the diary and cast low alchemy on any junk at the Fountain of Rune (the explorer's ring alchemy works on any spellbook). Both are easy Wilderness tasks, the alchemy needs 21 Magic, and the easy tier has to be finished before you can claim the medium rewards. Keep the rune pickaxe from the quest as your pickaxe from now on and drop the adamant one.

        UIM riskFor a UIM, a Wilderness death loses what you carry, so take exactly that list and nothing else; leave the rest in a deathpile outside the Wilderness, such as the Edgeville Monastery one, and come back to it inside the hour.
        Done whenthe Wilderness gold helmet is smithed and your pile is collected.

        Coins: about 340,000 gp · Slots: what you take into the Wilderness · Requirements: Between a Rock...'s 30 Defence, 40 Mining and 50 Smithing

        Wiki referencesDesert DiaryRatcatchersBetween a Rock...Wilderness Diary

        Motherlode Mine, the coal bag and gem bag, and Volcanic Mine

        Gate in30 Mining (57 for the upper level); 50 Mining and Bone Voyage (for Fossil Island) for Volcanic Mine; a team for Volcanic Mine; no celestial ring worn at MLM.
        Carry ina pickaxe and the hourly kit: about 6/28.
        Exitthe coal bag and gem bag; the prospector hat (and outfit if you want it); 72 Mining by Making Friends with My Arm; the Falador easy diary and the hard step done; about 340,000 gp.
        Carries forwardthe coal bag and gem bag (core kit), the prospector helmet (the Falador hard diary).

        Motherlode Mine

        Mine in the Motherlode Mine for the coal bag and gem bag, repairing the strut along the way for the diary. The north entrance, outside the Mining Guild's underground entrance, has no Mining requirement. The strut task is an easy Falador task and needs a hammer (the crates in the mine hold one) and any pickaxe: a broken strut stops pay-dirt reaching the sack, a repair serves everyone on that world, and it gives Smithing XP. Pay-dirt in the tray stays there if you log out or hop, and rockfalls that block passages deal 1 to 4 damage and clear with your pickaxe. Don't wear the celestial ring here: it is bugged and reduces the nuggets you get. For a UIM the two bags matter more than for an Ironman: the coal bag holds the Blast Furnace coal and the gem bag the uncut gems that would otherwise take slots. From the wiki: pay-dirt needs 30 Mining (not boostable) and gives 60 XP each; each pay-dirt has a 3.13% chance of a golden nugget, about 1 in 32; Prospector Percy sells the coal bag and the gem bag for 100 nuggets each and the prospector helmet, jacket, legs and boots for 40, 60, 50 and 30; the upper level needs 57 Mining (not boostable) and a one-time 100 nuggets, and its hopper another 50; and the sack holds 108 pay-dirt, or 189 after a 200-nugget upgrade; when it is full, mining and hopper deposits stop until you empty it. Buy the two bags first.

        Notes:

        • You can spread the mining over several steps, but you need 72 Mining (boostable) for Making Friends with My Arm in the next block, and MLM finished by the Sepulchre block.
        • If you don't want the bags, you can get to about 68–70 Mining another way (sandstone or blast mining), but you'll then be short of money.
        • A tick-manipulation method makes MLM about 40% faster in theory at 70 Mining. It needs an adamant pickaxe and works like 3-tick mining, but on a success you click the next ore again on the XP drop, so the cycle is 4 ticks on a success and 3 on a failure. Use the south-west corner, where several veins are close together.
        • After hourlies, you can run back from the Falador herb patch, or use the Arceuus Respawn Teleport to Falador.
        Done whenyou hold the coal bag and gem bag.

        Volcanic Mine

        Then do a low-level group Volcanic Mine for the prospector hat, and optionally the rest of the outfit (each entry costs 30 numulite, mined on Fossil Island, or 3,000 numulite once for permanent access); a Discord can help you find teams. The mine is in the north-east of the Fossil Island volcano. The helmet costs 26,000 points in Petrified Pete's Ore Shop (the jacket 39,000, the legs 34,000 and the boots 21,000). The mine kills: keep the gas chamber pressure around 40 to 60% or the mine becomes unstable, don't stand on hardened lava, which melts after 90 seconds, and leave with about 40 seconds left on the 10-minute timer, because the volcano erupts at the end and kills anyone still inside. Complete the Falador hard diary step, and the Falador easy diary (lamp on Slayer).

        Done whenthe prospector hat is bought and the Falador easy diary is claimed.

        Coins: about 340,000 gp · Slots: about 6/28 for mining · Requirements: 30 Mining for MLM; 50 Mining and Bone Voyage (for Fossil Island) for Volcanic Mine

        Wiki referencesMotherlode MineCoal bagGem bagVolcanic MineFalador Diary

        Enlightened Journey: the first trip to Entrana

        Chapter 1 moved this quest here because its first Entrana trip needs 25 unnoted items at once, and Entrana bans weapons and armour. The monks search you before you sail, and if you log in on Entrana carrying prohibited equipment a monk knocks you unconscious and you wake in Port Sarim. With Falador as your respawn that is another courtyard deathpile, as in 2.1's Entrana block, and the shopping is done before the death so that the hour is spent on the island. The second trip, for the balloon itself, opens the Recipe for Disaster block, by which time Auguste's willow has grown.

        Gate in20 quest points; 20 Firemaking, 30 Farming and 36 Crafting (all boostable); respawn in Falador; Prince Ali Rescue for the free toll gate.
        Carry inthe core kit with 16 free slots for the shopping; on Entrana, only the quest items and about 400 coins (26/28).
        ExitEnlightened Journey's first stage done; Auguste's sapling planted and protected; the pile collected; about 339,000 gp.
        Carries forwardnothing; the willow grows until the Recipe for Disaster block.

        The shopping

        1. Take a ring of dueling to Emir's Arena and walk to Al Kharid. Buy three papyrus at Ali's Discount Wares (10 gp each) and ten silk from the silk trader north of the palace (2 to 3 gp each). Opening Ali's basic stock with coins and a free slot can make him force-sell you a cheap item; drop it.
        2. Walk through the toll gate to Lumbridge. Buy shears at the general store (1 gp), shear a sheep at Fred the Farmer's, spin the wool at the castle spinning wheel, pick two onions in the field by Fred's farmhouse and drop the shears.
        3. Walk on to Port Sarim and buy three redberries at Wydin's Food Store, which stocks one at a time and restocks every minute. Walk back to Aggie in Draynor for a red dye (three redberries and 5 gp) and a yellow dye (two onions and 5 gp).
        Done whenyou hold three papyrus, ten silk, a ball of wool and the two dyes: 16 slots.

        Entrana with a Falador deathpile

        1. Teleport to Falador, take off everything in the castle courtyard and die to a White Knight. Pick up only the 16 quest items and about 400 coins: the death destroys the Chronicle again, and Diango sells it back for 300 gp.
        2. At Sarah's Farming shop on the farm south of Falador, buy nine empty sacks (1 gp each). Walk to Port Sarim and take the monks' boat to Entrana.
        3. Talk to Auguste, north of the Herblore shop, three times and agree to help (if you rely on boosts for the requirements, apply all of them before you talk to him). Use a papyrus on the ball of wool, steal a candle from the church and use it on the balloon structure, and talk to him again. Don't light the origami balloon during his test, or you have to gather the items again.
        4. Fill one sack with ten potatoes in the house east of him and give him the papyrus and the sack. Fill the other eight sacks with sand just south of him, and pick up the bowl upstairs in the same house. Give him the yellow and red dyes, the ten silk, the bowl and the eight sandbags. He gives you a basket of apples and Auguste's sapling.
        5. Take the boat back to Port Sarim, walk to Falador and buy the Chronicle back from Perdu upstairs in the general store (500 gp; its charges come back with it), then collect the rest of your pile.
        6. Plant the sapling in the Falador Park tree patch (a spade, and a rake if it has weeds, from the tool leprechaun) and give Heskel, the gardener there, the basket of apples so that she looks after it. The wiki says it needs at least four hours to grow and about another hour for its branches. If the patch holds one of your own trees, use any free tree patch and its gardener instead.
        Done whenAuguste has the first stage's items, and the sapling is planted and protected.

        Coins: about 339,000 gp · Slots: the core kit plus 16 for the shopping; about 26/28 on Entrana with the sacks and coins · Requirements: 20 quest points, 20 Firemaking, 30 Farming and 36 Crafting (boostable) for Enlightened Journey

        Wiki referencesEnlightened JourneyEnlightened Journey/Quick guidePapyrusSilk traderRed dyeYellow dyeEmpty sackEntrana

        Swan Song, Eagles' Peak, Making Friends with My Arm and the Weiss salts

        Gate in100 quest points, 66 Magic, 62 Cooking, 62 Fishing, 45 Smithing, 42 Firemaking and 40 Crafting for Swan Song; 66 Firemaking (not boostable), 72 Mining, 68 Agility (both boostable) and 35 Construction for Making Friends with My Arm; 29 Farming, at least 60% Tai Bwo Wannai favour, eight buckets of supercompost (seven, plus one spare) and three of ugthanki dung for My Arm's Big Adventure; the eagle disguises; 27 Hunter (boostable) for Eagles' Peak; a rope; 35 Agility and 45 Strength for the eyries.
        Carry in10 mist, 10 lava and 5 blood runes, 5 iron bars, logs, a hammer, a tinderbox and the airtight pot: about 24/28 for Swan Song.
        ExitEagles' Peak, Swan Song, My Arm's Big Adventure and Making Friends with My Arm done; the eyrie diary tasks done; the Weiss salts and the basalt teleports made; about 330,000 gp.
        Carries forwardthe salts (eternal braziers in Chapter 3), the basalt teleports, the Weiss herb patch.
        Deathbank checkthis block has fights where a death is unsafe. If Hespori or another retrieval service holds your items, collect them before you start: an unsafe death here deletes them.

        Start Swan Song and finish Eagles' Peak

        Take fairy ring AKQ to the Piscatoris Fishing Colony and start Swan Song with Herman Caranos, then continue Eagles' Peak at the Ardougne Zoo until you only need to report back to Charlie. Charlie stands just south of the scorpion cage. For Eagles' Peak, the eagle door needs three feathers: bronze (lift the net trap by taking the feather, then operate all four winches), silver (inspect the empty pedestal, which needs 27 Hunter, and follow the rocks to a level 13 kebbit) and gold (the lever and bird-feeder puzzle with six odd bird seeds, which has a reset lever outside the chamber if you get stuck). Then Nickolaus teaches you ferret catching outside the dungeon: don't trigger the box-trap retrieval cutscene, which stops you catching the ferret, and if you release it by accident, the box trap he gives you catches another. Visit the Wise Old Man in Draynor Village, who asks for the 10 lava, 10 mist and 5 blood runes you carry and sends you back to the colony.

        Done whenSwan Song is started and Eagles' Peak waits only on Charlie.

        Eagles' Peak and the eagle rides

        Report to Charlie to complete Eagles' Peak (2 quest points, 2,500 Hunter XP, a box trap, the use of box traps and the eagle transport system), then take fairy ring AKQ to Eagles' Peak (the necklace of passage only reaches the desert eyrie near Uzer) and take several eagle rides back and forth (bring a rope), finishing in the desert, for the Fremennik, Desert and Western Provinces medium diaries. Each diary task is arriving at that eyrie from another one. The wiki's requirements: the polar eyrie by Rellekka needs 35 Agility to climb; the desert eyrie at Uzer needs 45 Strength (boostable) to push the boulder; and the jungle eyrie in the Feldip Hills needs its young vine grown with a teasing stick, thatching spar or garden cane, which takes about 40 minutes, so plant the stick early and do that ride when it has grown. Run to the Shantay Pass, collect the desert medium diary from Jarr and put the lamp on Slayer (it is worth 7,500 XP in a skill at level 40 or higher).

        Done whenEagles' Peak is complete and the eyrie tasks and desert medium diary are done.

        Complete Swan Song

        RequirementsSwan Song needs 100 quest points, and its boss is the level 170 Sea Troll Queen.

        Take fairy ring AKQ to the Piscatoris hunter area. Run to Piscatoris and complete Swan Song, bringing 10 mist, 10 lava and 5 blood runes (the mist and lava runes come from the Air and Fire altars in 2.2; the wiki's Ironman note adds that combination runes can be bought from the Rewards Guardian in the Mage Training Arena), 5 iron bars, logs, a hammer, a tinderbox and the airtight pot. The repairs can be done in any order: for Arnold Lydspor, catch and cook five fresh monkfish (catch extras, because some burn, and three trolls of levels 65, 87 and 101 attack while you fish and must be killed); for Franklin Caranos, fuel the metal press in the furnace building with logs, light it with the tinderbox and use the five iron bars on it for iron sheets, which go on the broken west wall (the wiki warns against using the hammer on the wall). The seven bones Malignius Mortifer wants can be picked up from the eight level 79 sea trolls of the first battle, and he also wants a pot and pot lid, which is what the airtight pot is. Malignius stands north-west of Port Sarim's magic shop. He refuses to help if you carry Saradomin god equipment (the cape doesn't count), and a UIM can't bank it, so don't bring it. He teleports you to the colony without warning when you finish talking, so be ready first. The wiki advises Protect from Magic with melee armour, fighting in melee range: she uses a Prayer-draining attack if you use an overhead prayer from range, and it calls the ranged and magic safespots inadvisable. Her magic hits up to about 37.

        UIM riskThe fight is an instance, so on a death your items go outside it, and anything left on its floor is lost.
        Rewards2 quest points, 15,000 Magic, 10,000 Prayer and 50,000 Fishing XP and 25,000 coins.
        Done whenSwan Song is complete.

        My Arm's Big Adventure

        Chapter 1 moved this quest here, because it needs seven unnoted buckets of supercompost carried to the Troll Stronghold on top of a kit. The wiki's requirements are Eadgar's Ruse, Druidic Ritual, Troll Stronghold, Death Plateau, The Feud and Jungle Potion (all done in Chapter 1), 29 Farming, 10 Woodcutting and at least 60% Tai Bwo Wannai favour, which the quest takes from your total; Chapter 1 left you on 100%, and the Karamja medium task for it is already done.

        Prepare two things first:

        • Supercompost. Fill a compost bin with 15 compostable items and, once it has rotted, add a compost potion for supercompost, as in Chapter 1's Hosidius bin. The bin gives 15 buckets and the tool leprechaun stores them. The wiki says a bottomless compost bucket won't work, and the eighth bucket stands in for plant cure when you give My Arm the last tool. Take eight out when you leave: seven for the soil patch and one for My Arm's own patch.
        • Ugthanki dung. Three buckets, from the camels in Pollnivneach (magic carpet from the Shantay Pass). The wiki's quick guide gives two ways to get it: the camulet from Enakhra's Lament, or red hot sauce from Pollnivneach fed to a camel (the sauce goes on the trough at Ali the Camel Man's, and with the camulet you ask a camel in Al Kharid for dung). Bring four empty buckets: three for the dung and one for the cooking pot on Death Plateau.

        You carry the eight supercompost, the three dung, the empty bucket, a spade, a rake and a seed dibber from the leprechaun, food and Trollheim teleport runes: about 16 slots on top of the kit, falling to six after the soil patch.

        1. Cast Trollheim Teleport. In the Troll Stronghold kitchen, talk to Burntmeat and agree to help, then to My Arm east of him. Don't pick up the stolen items from the floor during Burntmeat's cutscene, or the cutscene stops.
        2. Go to Death Plateau and, with one free slot, use the bucket on the cooking pot in the middle for a goutweedy lump. Thrower trolls are there, so pray against missiles and melee, as the wiki advises.
        3. Back in the Stronghold, talk to My Arm until he takes you to his patch, read the book he gives you and talk to him again. Use the three buckets of ugthanki dung and seven buckets of supercompost on the soil patch and talk to him. Then talk to Captain Barnaby in East Ardougne and tell him My Arm would like to go to Karamja.
        4. Meet My Arm in Tai Bwo Wannai. With a free slot, talk to Murcaily by the hardwood grove about My Arm's favour; after the cutscene you hold the hardy gout tubers. The tuber goes into the last inventory slot, and if that slot is occupied the game sorts your inventory, so keep it empty and expect the order to change. Talk to My Arm and take the boat back to Ardougne.
        5. On the Troll Stronghold roof (the ladder in the west room of the upper level, where the Troll Generals are), give My Arm the rake (if it breaks, combine the head and handle and return it), the last supercompost, the tubers and the seed dibber, in that order. Then talk to him to start the fight: a level 75 Baby Roc and a level 172 Giant Roc in an instance. The wiki says unprotected items go to a grave if you die, which for a UIM is everything you carry, so treat it as a no-death fight. Its attacks can be dodged by stepping back and forth, but not its stun, and the wiki recommends Magic against the Giant Roc, whose defence is lowest against it.
        6. Give My Arm the spade (if you forgot it, climb down the ladder and back up so the leprechaun reappears), talk to Burntmeat in the kitchen and then to My Arm on the roof to complete My Arm's Big Adventure: 1 quest point, 10,000 Herblore and 5,000 Farming XP, a disease-free herb patch on the roof and the rocks shortcut outside the Stronghold. Drop the 29 burnt meat.
        Done whenMy Arm's Big Adventure is complete.

        Making Friends with My Arm

        RequirementsMaking Friends with My Arm: 66 Firemaking, which can't be boosted; Chapter 1 leaves you on 85 or more. The cave exit needs 72 Mining.

        Then start and complete Making Friends with My Arm, starting with Burntmeat in the Troll Stronghold kitchen. Wolfbone blocks the start without the Firemaking, and Larry's boat is reached by fairy ring DKS and a run west. Bring a saw, a bolt of cloth, five mahogany planks from the plank sack, cadava berries and Trollheim teleports (or climbing boots), and an agility potion to boost if you need it. A karambwan-poisoned rune spear from Tai Bwo Wannai Trio works; the wiki recommends Ranged with Protect from Missiles against Mother, so bring a ranged setup as well. The rockslides need 68 Agility and a failed climb deals up to 15 damage, so bring food if your Agility is low. On the Weiss surface Boulder refuses you entry, so sneak through the fence gap east of him, and the trolls' thrown rocks eject you when they hit, so use safespots and run diagonally. You can't teleport out of the fight until the Fire of Domination is put out, and without dousing it your attacks won't hit, so fill a bucket at the barrel of water and use it on the fire (the wiki lists a bucket of water for the quest). This is where good herb runs start.

        Rewards2 quest points, 10,000 Construction, 40,000 Firemaking, 50,000 Mining and 50,000 Agility XP, access to the salt mines and the Weiss herb patch, which needs the Fire of Nourishment built first.
        Done whenMaking Friends with My Arm is complete.

        The Weiss salts

        RequirementsThe Fire of Nourishment needs 35 Construction and 66 Firemaking.

        In Weiss, mine Te, Efh and Urt salts and basalt, which needs Making Friends with My Arm done and 72 Mining; a rock gives 2 to 4 salts at 72 to 80 Mining and 3 to 5 at 81 to 89, so expect many rocks. The minimum is two Eternal Braziers, the Fire of Nourishment (for the Weiss herb patch) and 100 each of stony and icy basalt teleports: 750 Te, 900 Efh, 950 Urt and 200 basalt. Make the teleports inside the mine, so the basalt never needs carrying out. Also mine for the Fire of Eternal Light, the Fire of Dehumidification and the Fire of Unseasonal Warmth: 1,200 Te, 1,350 Efh, 1,400 Urt and 200 basalt in total. Salts and basalt teleports stack, so all of this is a few slots. The Fire of Nourishment also needs 2 mahogany planks (from the plank sack), 2 steel bars, a hammer, a saw and a tinderbox; the wiki's Mining salts page lists its salt cost as 50 Te, 100 Efh and 150 Urt, an Eternal Brazier as 250 of each salt, and each icy basalt teleport as 1 basalt, 1 Te and 3 Efh (stony: 1 basalt, 1 Te and 3 Urt), which is where the totals above come from. The braziers themselves need 78 Sailing and 72 Construction, on top of the salts they take 4 ironwood planks, 16 rune nails, 6 runite bars and 6 cupronickel bars each, so they wait for Chapter 3.

        Done whenyou hold the salts and the basalt teleports are made.

        Coins: about 330,000 gp · Slots: about 24/28 for Swan Song · Requirements: 100 quest points, One Small Favour (done earlier in 2.2), Garden of Tranquillity and Creature of Fenkenstrain (both done in Chapter 1), 66 Magic, 62 Cooking, 62 Fishing, 45 Smithing, 42 Firemaking and 40 Crafting for Swan Song; 66 Firemaking (not boostable), 72 Mining, 68 Agility, 35 Construction, Swan Song, My Arm's Big Adventure, Eadgar's Ruse, The Feud, Romeo & Juliet and Cold War for Making Friends with My Arm; 29 Farming, 10 Woodcutting, 60% Tai Bwo Wannai favour, Druidic Ritual, Troll Stronghold, Death Plateau and Jungle Potion for My Arm's Big Adventure

        Wiki referencesSwan SongEagles' PeakMy Arm's Big Adventure/Quick guideMaking Friends with My ArmWeissBasalt

        Barbarian Fishing to 99

        Gate in72 Mining; the Barbarian Fishing spot; a knife and a teak log.
        Carry inthe barbarian rod, feathers and 2-dose potions: about 8/28.
        Exit99 Fishing; 70 Cooking for Awowogei; the super defence potion for the Fremennik hard diary.
        Carries forwardnothing new; keep the hourlies going.

        Fish to 99

        Back to the Mount Quidamortem spot, this time to 99 Fishing (the heavy rod needs 48 Fishing, 15 Agility and 15 Strength, and every catch also gives Strength, Agility and Cooking XP, which is how this method feeds the 68 Agility and 70 Cooking you need), with Barblore as much as possible and cut-eating the fish for Cooking (you still need 70 for Awowogei). Notes:

        • If you aren't aiming to max, you can skip this and train Agility to 72 manually at the Hallowed Sepulchre instead.
        • At 66 Herblore, make a super defence potion in the Fremennik Province for the Fremennik hard diary (the wiki says it has to be done in or near Rellekka, with a cadantine potion (unf) and white berries).
        • Keep up the hourlies: farming contracts, hardwood and fruit tree runs, herb runs (and a potion round whenever the sack is full), and optionally seaweed (you never need more than 12,500). You can stop raising cats, but you are catching cat food the whole time.
        • Optionally, play Tempoross (a mass world, or a solo method) for the fish barrel, then come back to Barbarian Fishing.

        The items are a knife, a teak log, the barbarian rod, feathers and 2-dose potions. If you lose the rod, the wiki says another is under Otto's bed in his grotto, and a knife gutting the fish gives roe or caviar and sometimes fish offcuts. Mining should be 72 by now, as the Motherlode block said.

        Done whenyou are 99 Fishing and 70 Cooking.

        Coins: about 330,000 gp · Slots: about 8/28 · Requirements: as before

        Wiki referencesBarbarian FishingFremennik DiaryFish barrel

        Recipe for Disaster to the barrows gloves

        Gate in175 quest points and every Recipe for Disaster subquest; 70 Cooking; 48 Agility; Legends' Quest's skills; the ice gloves; Auguste's willow grown, Lost City and 36 Woodcutting for this block's Entrana trip (Enlightened Journey's balloon and the dramen branch).
        Carry inthe jungle and subquest items (about 26/28 in the jungle), then the Culinaromancer kit.
        ExitEnlightened Journey, Legends' Quest and Recipe for Disaster done; barrows gloves bought; the Karamja easy and medium diaries claimed; about 200,000 gp.
        Carries forwardthe barrows gloves.
        Deathbank checkthis block has fights where a death is unsafe. If Hespori or another retrieval service holds your items, collect them before you start: an unsafe death here deletes them.

        Entrana again: the balloon and the dramen branch

        Auguste's willow has had far more than its four hours by now. Cut its twelve branches with secateurs from the tool leprechaun: the wiki says you take six at a time and wait half an hour for the other six, so cut six, do a herb run and come back. Axes can't be taken to Entrana, so the logs are chopped beforehand. Chop ten normal logs and keep the tinderbox. Then use the Falador courtyard deathpile again: die to a White Knight and pick up only the branches, the logs, the tinderbox, 300 coins and the runes for one Falador Teleport (3 air, 1 water and 1 law; runes are allowed on Entrana).

        1. Take the monks' boat from Port Sarim. Talk to the cave monk and go down into the Entrana Dungeon, kill zombies until one drops a bronze axe, and cut a branch from the dramen tree (36 Woodcutting) for Sir Amik Varze's cinnamon. Drop the axe. The wiki says there is no way to climb back up to Entrana from the dungeon: the exits are a teleport or the magic door to level 32 Wilderness, which you skip. Cast Falador Teleport, leave the pile in the courtyard (its hour keeps running, so don't linger), walk to Port Sarim and take the monks' boat again with the same items. If the axe drop took long and the pile's hour is nearly up, pick the pile up and repeat the death before you go back.
        2. Use the twelve willow branches on the balloon frame on the platform by Auguste and tell him you're ready. Fly the balloon: the wiki's quick guide gives the three screens move by move (the sandbag, the logs, relaxing and the red and brown ropes), and a crash costs ten more logs and another try. The balloon must avoid every obstacle, including birds, trees, houses and clouds, and on the final screen you land one space above the landing pad with no gap: flying past it blows the balloon away, and landing in the pad's space smashes it. After a failed flight you gather the logs and tinderbox again but don't rebuild the balloon. The wiki calls this the Taverley route; after the landing, talk to Auguste to complete Enlightened Journey: 1 quest point, 2,000 Crafting, 3,000 Farming, 1,500 Woodcutting and 4,000 Firemaking XP, the bomber jacket and cap (drop them if you don't want them) and the balloon network. A route is unlocked by visiting Entrana and completing the ballooning there with its log type (10 oak, willow or yew logs, or 3 magic logs, with 30, 40, 50 and 60 Firemaking). Each successful route flight gives 2,000 Firemaking XP. Chapter 3's Monkey Madness II needs the Grand Tree route unlocked before the quest can start, so that one waits for Chapter 3, where you will have the magic logs.
        3. Walk from Taverley to Falador for your pile, then buy the Chronicle back from Perdu upstairs in the Falador general store (500 gp; its charges come back with it).
        Done whenEnlightened Journey is complete and you hold the dramen branch.

        The remaining subquests

        Swap to the standard spellbook and finish Recipe for Disaster in this order:

        1. Ring of dueling to Emir's Arena, walk to Al Kharid and take the gnome glider to the Feldip Hills. Talk to Rantz for Skrach until he asks to meet you in Karamja.
        2. Ardougne teleport, the boat to Brimhaven and the cart to Shilo Village. Resume Legends' Quest up to its final step, when you have the totem pole. The wiki's warnings for the jungle: jungle wolves are aggressive, tormented warriors and oomlie birds only attack if provoked, so don't use the bullroarer near them, and a rune axe or better is needed on the yommi tree, with 45 Herblore for the seeds and 50 Woodcutting for the totem. Death wings in the Viyeldi caves are level 83, and Protect from Melee negates their damage. A UIM can drop charcoal and papyrus after completing the notes three times, the sketch after making a gold bowl, and the binding book after freeing Ungadulu; if you lose the binding book, you redo the gem puzzle. In the Kharazi Jungle: chop a teak and a mahogany log; chop a palm tree for a palm leaf five times (drop and pick up); kill an oomlie, wrap it in a palm leaf, cook it and eat it; pick up a vanilla pod for Sir Amik Varze; kill a deathwing in the Viyeldi caves. The oomlie wrap, the palm leaf and the deathwing are not on the Karamja easy or medium list in step 6, so treat them as hard-diary tasks and skip them if they slow you down. North-west of Tai Bwo Wannai, light a fire on the coast for Skrach, then take the boat back to Rantz and prepare a cooked jubbly.
        3. Go to the Legends' Guild by fairy ring BLR. Complete Legends' Quest and put the lamps on Herblore. Don't talk to or attack Ungadulu during the fight, because talking damages you and attacking knocks you out of the octogram. Nezikchened drains about 90% of your Prayer at the start, his Fire Blast goes through Protect from Magic, and he resets and heals if you are too slow, and a death during that fight means re-obtaining every quest item. Take the fairy ring to Zanaris (BKS) and go into the Evil Chicken's Lair for Sir Amik Varze until you have the brulee, with the items listed after this list.
        4. Fairy ring CLR to Ape Atoll and do Awowogei up to and including the cooked stuffed snake.
        5. Teleport to Lumbridge and complete Recipe for Disaster, including the Culinaromancer fight. Lamp on Herblore. Buy barrows gloves from the Culinaromancer's Chest.
        6. When convenient, go to Brimhaven, earn one Agility Arena ticket and claim the Karamja easy and medium diaries (lamps on Slayer).
        Done whenLegends' Quest and every subquest but the final fight are done.

        The Culinaromancer fight

        Go into the Culinaromancer fight with your ice gloves (without them you cannot wield a weapon against Flambeed), restore potions for Karamel's stat drain, water, air, earth and fire runes for the Gelatinnoth Mother, and plenty of food. Prayer does not work in the dimension, and white portals in it lead back to Lumbridge. The bosses come in this order: Agrith-Na-Na (level 146), Flambeed (149), Karamel (136), Dessourt (121) and the Gelatinnoth Mother (130), then the Culinaromancer himself (75). Karamel's drain can stop you casting your highest spell, so keep a lower one ready, and the Mother's hits can reach 40 combined, which is dangerous at low Hitpoints. The bosses come one after another, but you can leave and restock between them without losing your place, so do that instead of pushing on low. The reward lamp is worth 20,000 XP in a skill above level 50, and with a full inventory it drops at your feet to collect on returning to Lumbridge Castle.

        UIM riskA death there leaves a deathpile outside the instance and anything dropped on the floor is lost, so a UIM's whole inventory is on the line.
        UIM riskAris claims your items are lost forever on a death in the dimension, but the wiki says that is false.
        Done whenyou know the fight's rules and your kit is ready.

        Sir Amik Varze's brulee

        Sir Amik Varze's brulee. The wiki's item list for this subquest is a bucket of milk, a pot of cream, a pot of cornflour (grind your sweetcorn at a windmill into a pot if you haven't yet), the vanilla pod from step 2, cinnamon, a dramen or lunar staff for the fairy rings, an anti-dragon shield and a raw chicken (the plain one, not the green Morytania kind). Combine the food in this order: the bucket of milk on the pot of cream, the cornflour, the vanilla pod, the Evil Chicken's egg and the cinnamon, and a game message says the brulee just needs flambeing. If the Wise Old Man doesn't offer the Evil Chicken question, you skipped the cook or Swan Song isn't past Repairing the Colony. Buy the milk and cream at the Culinaromancer's Chest (Hudo and Heckel Funch in the Tree Gnome Stronghold also sell cream, or churn a bucket of milk at a dairy churn with 21 Cooking). Cinnamon is a dramen branch crushed with a pestle and mortar: use the one you cut in the Entrana Dungeon at the start of this block. In Zanaris, wear the anti-dragon shield, use the raw chicken on the Evil Chicken shrine, kill a black dragon (level 227) in its instance, which drops a dragon token (there is a safespot near the entrance), then run north past the dragons into the cave and kill the Evil Chicken (level 159), picking up the egg it drops; if you die to the black dragon while carrying the egg, it respawns in the nest. Walk straight out afterwards: if you die after the Evil Chicken but before leaving the lair, everything you dropped is gone, and the brulee never shows up in a deathpile. Then rub the dragon token, wish to K'klik for the brulee to be flambeed and take it to Sir Amik in Lumbridge Castle; wear your ice gloves for that, because without them the flambe can hit for up to 50. A following cat can vanish during this, and logging out and back in or Call follower brings it back.

        Done whenthe Evil Chicken is dead and Sir Amik has his brulee.

        Check before the final fight

        Before the final fight, check that every subquest is done: Another Cook's Quest, Freeing the Mountain Dwarf, Freeing the Goblin Generals (it needs Goblin Diplomacy), Freeing Pirate Pete, the Lumbridge Guide, Evil Dave, Skrach, Sir Amik Varze and Awowogei. Chapter 1 completed the Mountain Dwarf and Goblin Generals subquests. Free the council members from the Culinaromancer's spell and the cutscene triggers, after which he retreats through a portal into the dimension, and you need to be able to beat every enemy without Prayer.

        Done whenRecipe for Disaster is complete and the barrows gloves are bought.

        Coins: about 200,000 gp after the barrows gloves · Slots: about 26/28 in the jungle · Requirements: 175 quest points, Desert Treasure I, The Dig Site, Temple of Ikov, The Tourist Trap, Troll Stronghold, Death Plateau, Priest in Peril, Waterfall Quest, Horror from the Deep, Alfred Grimhand's Barcrawl and every Recipe for Disaster subquest for the final fight (70 Cooking for Awowogei, 48 Agility); Legends' Quest's skills (45 Herblore, 42 Prayer, 56 Magic, 52 Mining, 50 in the others)

        Wiki referencesEnlightened Journey/Quick guideRecipe for DisasterDefeating the CulinaromancerFreeing Sir Amik VarzeDramen branchPot of creamLegends' QuestBarrows glovesKaramja Diary

        The Warriors' Guild, the dragon defender and Nightmare Zone

        Gate in130 combined Attack and Strength; a full black armour set (about 7,700 gp); 60 Defence to wield the dragon defender (it has no Attack requirement).
        Carry inthe black set, food bought per trip and a defence potion: about 20/28.
        Exitthe dragon defender; 60 Attack and as much Defence as the grind gave; berserker ring and salve amulet imbued if not already; 200,000 extra NMZ points spent on redirection scrolls; about 190,000 gp.
        Carries forwardthe black armour set (King's Ransom), the redirection scrolls.
        Deathbank checkthis block has fights where a death is unsafe. If Hespori or another retrieval service holds your items, collect them before you start: an unsafe death here deletes them.

        Get into the Warriors' Guild

        If you can't enter the Warriors' Guild yet (130 combined Attack and Strength), train them on Slayer tasks until you can; aim for 60 Attack and 70 Strength. The guild is in the west of Burthorpe, behind the statues of Ghommal and Sloane, and the guard at the door checks your levels. The check uses your real levels, so a potion or other boost won't get you in early, and 60 and 70 add up to exactly 130 with nothing to spare.

        Done whenyour Attack and Strength total 130.

        Tokens and defenders

        Kill cyclopes in the guild for the dragon defender. Learn three places first: the animation room is on the ground floor beside the entrance hall, the cyclops room is on the top floor (the second floor), and the basement is down a ladder just west of the guild's back door. The cyclops room needs 100 warrior guild tokens in your inventory to enter, spends 10 on entry and takes 10 more every minute, and tokens come only from the animation room: put a full set of black armour (helm, platebody and platelegs) on a magical animator and kill the animated armour for 20 tokens. You must fight it in melee, it ignores poison and venom, and it despawns after five minutes, so stay on it. If it despawns or kills you, Shanomi gives the armour back. Black armour comes back in full when the animated armour dies, so one set does the whole grind. Buy it at Valaine's Shop of Champions (helm and platelegs, on the first floor of the Champions' Guild) and Horvik's Armour Shop (platebody, in central Varrock), about 7,700 gp in all, and keep it for King's Ransom (or leave it with Shanomi, who holds one helm, platebody and platelegs up to rune, and collect it before King's Ransom). Each shop holds one piece at a time and restocks slowly (ten minutes for the helm, half an hour for the platebody, over three hours for the platelegs), so never sell or drop a piece. Defenders drop in order only (bronze, iron, steel, black, mithril, adamant, rune), about 1 in 50 on the top floor each. Leave and re-enter the room after each defender drops, or the next tier won't come, and keep the best defender on you, because without one only bronze drops and a UIM has no bank to leave it in. The basement needs the rune defender, shown to Lorelai once (she then lets you in for good), and drops the dragon defender at about 1 in 100 from the level 106 cyclopes. Attack and Defence should both be at least 60; train Attack to 60 first, then Defence. You don't need 60 Defence to receive the dragon defender, only to wield it, so a drop is never wasted. Train Defence as high as it goes during the defender grind, and if you don't reach 60 Defence, finish it on Slayer or sand crabs.

        If it doesn't dropthe dragon defender is about 1 in 100 a kill, so roughly one player in ten has none after 230 basement kills. Carry on with the rune defender, which the green dragon setup works with, and finish the grind in a later Warriors' Guild session.
        Done whenyou hold the dragon defender (or the rune defender, with the dragon one left for later).

        Food for the grind

        Food: cheese potatoes (or 15-healing premade gnome food) in bulk are too much for a UIM to hold, so buy potatoes with cheese per trip at the guild's food shop (9 gp each, they heal 16, and it stocks ten at a time). Lidio runs it on the ground floor, and its stock refills every minute, so if ten isn't enough for a trip you can wait a minute and buy again. Buy a defence potion.

        Done whenyou have a defence potion and know the food routine.

        Nightmare Zone and the imbues

        If you haven't imbued the berserker ring and the salve amulet, do it now at the PvP Arena or Nightmare Zone. Both are safe for a UIM. The PvP Arena is Emir's Arena, where a scroll of imbuing costs 200 arena reward points and turns the berserker ring into the imbued version. At Nightmare Zone the ring costs 650,000 points and the salve amulet 800,000, and each is halved once the Hard Combat Achievements reward is unlocked, so those points come on top of the 200,000 extra ones below. Nightmare Zone is north-west of Yanille bank: you talk to Dominic Onion, pay the dream fee from his coffer, drink the dream potion on the plinth and accept. There are four NMZ approaches: AFK easy rumble (long AFK, almost no points), AFK hard rumble with a rock cake and absorptions (about 8-minute AFK, medium points), hybrid hard rumble (good points), and active "points" NMZ (not AFK, very high points). For the active method it disables only the Corrupt Lizardman, Elvarg, Dad, Glod, the Recipe for Disaster bosses, the Giant Roc, Barrelchest, the Elven traitor, Essyllt and the Corsair Traitor. Collect 200,000 extra points and spend them all on redirection scrolls. Each scroll costs 775 points, so that buys 258 of them (199,950 points), and ironmen can buy them from the resources tab of the reward shop. A scroll redirects one house tablet to one destination (Rimmington, Taverley, Pollnivneach, Hosidius, Rellekka, Aldarin, Brimhaven, Yanille, Trollheim or Prifddinas, each with its own Construction or quest requirement), and reverting a redirected tablet doesn't give the scroll back, so choose before you apply one. Disable Desert Treasure (and Family Crest if you want a more AFK session). From now on you have redirected house tablets for herb runs, tree runs and general travel; come back for more when you run out.

        UIM riskIf you die inside, the dream ends and you keep your items, though anything you dropped on the ground is lost.
        Done whenboth items are imbued and the redirection scrolls are bought.

        Coins: about 190,000 gp · Slots: about 20/28 · Requirements: 130 combined Attack and Strength (or 99 in one) for the Warriors' Guild; a full black armour set (about 7,700 gp) for the guild's animation room

        Wiki referencesWarriors' GuildDragon defenderNightmare ZoneRedirection scroll

        Kourend: A Kingdom Divided, the Shayzien outfit and the Barrows run

        Gate in53 Hunter for the chinchompa task (or boost); 20 Defence for Shayzien armour; A Kingdom Divided's quests with 54 Agility, 52 Thieving, 52 Woodcutting, 50 Herblore, 42 Mining, 38 Crafting and 35 Magic (none boostable); Priest in Peril.
        Carry inmelee gear, cheese potatoes, potions and the quest items; a full Barrows inventory: about 25/28.
        Exitthe Kourend & Kebos medium diary; the tier 5 Shayzien set; A Kingdom Divided done; His Faithful Servants done; about 180,000 gp.
        Carries forwardthe Shayzien set, the crypt map.
        Deathbank checkthis block has fights where a death is unsafe. If Hespori or another retrieval service holds your items, collect them before you start: an unsafe death here deletes them.

        The herb sack

        Your house in Hosidius is next to the Tithe Farm, so buy the herb sack here as soon as you are 58 Herblore, which can't be boosted (lamps have gone on Herblore since 2.1): talk to Farmer Gricoller and spend the 250 points Chapter 1 kept in reserve. If you aren't 58 yet (the lamps alone leave you near 57 until Varlamore), use the leprechaun's noted herbs and buy it the first time you pass at 58. The sack holds only grimy, unnoted herbs of the normal kinds (30 of each), so from then on put your farm-run herbs straight into it. Cleaned herbs, noted herbs and coral can't go in, so it saves you slots only for the grimy ones. Optional later: a pristine spider silk (Sarachnis, 1/50) upgrades it to the silklined herb sack, which holds 100 of each herb.

        Done whenyou hold the herb sack, or know to buy it the first time you pass at 58 Herblore.

        The Kourend & Kebos medium diary

        In Kourend, tackle organised crime for intelligence along the way and hand it in to Captain Ginea for the diary. Speak to Captain Ginea first: she tells you about a gang meeting, and intelligence drops from the gangsters (about one in five) and always from the gang boss, so kill the boss as well as the gangsters, with combat gear on. Catch a chinchompa in the Kourend Woodland for the diary; it needs 53 Hunter, Eagles' Peak and a box trap, so boost with a hunter potion or trapper's tipple if you are close, and otherwise leave the diary until the blue crabs have trained Hunter.

        Done whenthe organised crime and chinchompa tasks are done or set aside.

        The Shayzien combat ring

        Collect the tier 5 Shayzien outfit from the combat ring (20 Defence). The ring is in the Shayzien Encampment, and you start each duel by talking to the soldier of the tier you want. If you lose a piece before the next tier's set is complete, you have to beat the previous tier again, so wear each finished set while you earn the next. Pieces in your house still count towards a set, but don't put loose pieces in the armour case yet: the wiki says a UIM can't take individual pieces back out until the full set is stored. The soldiers are level 39, 48, 58, 70 and 99, and the last hits up to 10 against 5 for the first. Collect the Kourend & Kebos medium diary from Elise, southeast of the King Rada I statue in the Kourend Castle courtyard, lamps on Slayer. Its rewards include Rada's blessing 2, which the green dragon setup wears later, and an antique lamp worth 7,500 XP.

        UIM riskDeaths there are unsafe, so treat every fight as a no-death fight: if one goes badly, run out of the combat area, which ends the duel.
        UIM riskEach tier's soldier drops one piece per defeat, so you beat each tier five times, and you must finish a tier's set before challenging the next; duplicates don't drop until a set is complete, so every win until then is a new piece.
        Done whenyou wear the tier 5 Shayzien set and the medium diary is claimed.

        A Kingdom Divided

        Do A Kingdom Divided on the standard spellbook, using melee for the fights; lamps on Slayer. Don't grab a skull near the Shayzien graveyard dungeon entrance or buy redberries at Kenelme's Wares in Piscarilius: the wiki's item list for the quest names neither, so skip both unless a later step asks for them. The items are melee gear, cheese potatoes, stat-boosting and prayer potions, a 3-dose defence potion, volcanic sulphur, a molten glass, a dark essence block, an axe and Fire Bolt runes. Start by talking to Martin Holt at the entrance to Kourend Castle in Kingstown. Most ways of getting stuck are small. In the Rose cutscene, clicking outside the chat box ends it and sends you back to the shack, where you inspect the crates again to resume. In the Shayzien prison, speak to Commander Fullore before Martin or he won't talk to you. Talk to all five leaders before you start any of their tasks, and after Lady Lovakengj go back to Fullore and ask for help with her. The Judge of Yama is melee only and immune to magic and ranged: pass through the gaps in the fire waves on the four-wide pier, or tank them with 70 or more Hitpoints. Xamphur is level 239, immune to magic and mostly uses magic attacks: stay off the Marks of Darkness and kill the Phantom Hands quickly. The quest gives two antique lamps worth 10,000 XP each (one if you claimed the Architectural Alliance lamp before 10 January 2024) and the Book of the Dead, and offers Kourend Castle as a respawn point: keep Falador, as the Knight Waves step explains.

        Done whenA Kingdom Divided is complete.

        The Barrows run

        Take the minigame teleport to Mort'ton and do a full Barrows run of all six brothers; the mounds are a short walk east of Mort'ton, and the teleport has a 20-minute cooldown, so plan the way home. The His Faithful Servants miniquest asks for exactly that run (kill all six, then open the chest for the strange icon, with at least six free slots). The icon never drops to the floor, so if your inventory is full at the chest you have to kill all six again. Talk to the Strange Old Man in the Barrows before you go in to start it and again afterwards to finish it; the wiki lists only Priest in Peril as a requirement, and the reward is a dusty lamp (20,000 Prayer XP) and a crypt map. Starting it first matters, because the mounds won't let you dig into the crypts until you have. Bring a spade as well as combat gear. Desert Treasure II in Chapter 3 needs it done. Get these combat achievements on that run: Defence? What Defence? (one brother killed with Magic only), Can't Touch Me (Dharok, Verac, Torag and Guthan killed without a melee hit), Pray for Success (no damage from any brother, which Verac's one-in-four chance to hit through Protect from Melee can spoil) and optionally Just Like That (Karil killed by special attack damage alone). Spec Karil with the dragon dagger (reset on a failure), use Magic on the four melee brothers and melee Ahrim. Use Protect from Magic on Ahrim, Protect from Missiles on Karil and Protect from Melee on the rest. One crypt leads to the tunnels and the chest, and which one is random: if you find it early, leave and kill the others first, because a brother who spawns and is left despawns and comes back at full health. After you loot, the tunnels collapse and the doors on the way out can summon brothers you didn't kill, who don't count toward anything, so don't fight them. For a UIM the run is a full inventory in the crypts, so treat it as a no-death trip.

        Done whenHis Faithful Servants is complete.

        Coins: about 180,000 gp · Slots: about 25/28 for Barrows · Requirements: A Kingdom Divided's quests and 54 Agility, 52 Thieving, 52 Woodcutting, 50 Herblore, 42 Mining, 38 Crafting and 35 Magic (none boostable); Priest in Peril for His Faithful Servants

        Wiki referencesA Kingdom DividedShayzien armourHis Faithful ServantsBarrowsKourend & Kebos Diary

        Green dragons, King's Ransom and the Knight Waves

        Gate inHoly Grail, Murder Mystery and One Small Favour; 65 Defence and 45 Magic (not boostable) for King's Ransom; a piece of granite (mined in this block), the black set and a guard disguise; 76 Magic (boost from 73) for Greater thralls.
        Carry inthe green dragon setup in the fourth step and food; for the Wilderness method, nothing else (the rest in a deathpile).
        ExitKing's Ransom and the Knight Waves done; 70 Defence and about 70 Prayer (Piety); 1,300 green dragons; the King Black Dragon Lair task; a Pest Control novice and intermediate game; about 70,000 gp at the low point.
        Carries forwardgreen dragonhides a trip at a time (the next block), Piety.
        Deathbank checkthis block has fights where a death is unsafe. If Hespori or another retrieval service holds your items, collect them before you start: an unsafe death here deletes them. In the Wilderness, a death to a player also hands most of what you carry to the killer.

        Green dragons to 65 Defence

        Kill green dragons with defensive melee and thralls until 65 Defence, handling the bones in one of the two ways under "Choose how to handle the bones" below; read that before your first trip. The Wilderness green dragons are level 79 and aggressive. They hit up to 8 in melee, and without a dragonfire shield or an antifire potion their breath can hit for up to 50, so eat before you drop low. They always drop dragon bones and a green dragonhide. Player killers target green dragon hunters often, which is why the planned death at the Monastery below matters.

        Done whenyou are 65 Defence.

        King's Ransom

        RequirementsKing's Ransom: 65 Defence and 45 Magic, neither boostable.

        Then do King's Ransom (XP on Herblore). Besides the quests in the Gate in line, the wiki also lists Black Knights' Fortress, Merlin's Crystal, Rune Mysteries, Shilo Village, Jungle Potion and Druidic Ritual, so check your quest log before you carry anything over. Start by talking to Gossip outside Sinclair Mansion, north-west of Camelot Castle. First use the camulet's teleport to Enakhra's temple and mine one piece of granite of any size in the Desert Quarry just south-west of it (45 Mining; without the camulet, take the carpet from the Shantay Pass), then bring it, the black armour set you bought for the Warriors' Guild (the quest wants a full black helm, platebody and platelegs; trimmed and elite versions work, heraldic ones don't) and a bronze med helm and an iron chainbody for the guard disguise (24 gp for the helm at Peksa's Helmet Shop in Barbarian Village, 210 gp for the chainbody at Horvik's in Varrock); the animate rock scroll is handed to you during the quest. When the scroll arrives, click through all of Cromperty's dialogue, and if you cut it short, talk to him again. In the Holy Grail riddle, the trap boxes deal 5 damage, drain a random skill and send you to the Ardougne sewers: pick the round purple box, second from the right. Pushing the wall in the Black Knights' Fortress works only in the full black set. The quest gives 1 quest point, 33,000 Defence XP, 5,000 Magic XP and an antique lamp worth 5,000 XP, which goes on Herblore.

        Done whenKing's Ransom is complete.

        The Knight Waves

        Afterwards do the Knight Waves Training Grounds. The easiest way through the waves is to fight with food and potions and let the knights kill you when you run low: the minigame is safe, you continue from the knight that killed you, and a UIM keeps everything. The room is at the top of Camelot Castle, up the ladder in the south-western tower, and a squire outside the door explains the rules. Only melee works inside, because Prayer, Ranged and Magic are all disabled, so bring restore potions rather than super restores. There are eight knights, from Sir Bedivere at level 110 to Sir Lancelot at level 127, and the last one hits up to 23. When you die you respawn outside the room with everything you carried, and walking back in puts you against the knight that killed you.

        Rewards20,000 XP each in Attack, Strength, Defence and Hitpoints, and the prayers: Chivalry, and Piety once you have 70 Prayer and 70 Defence.

        The Knight Waves offer Camelot as a spawn point and A Kingdom Divided offers Kourend Castle; decline both.

        UIM riskKeep Falador as your respawn point: the Falador Castle deathpile is your planned-death route, and Hespori's storage is lost on any unsafe death, so it doesn't replace the pile for fights.
        Done whenthe Knight Waves are complete.

        Green dragons for Prayer

        Return to the green dragons and kill 1,300 in total, defensive melee to 70 Defence and accurate after that. The setup is the Helm of Neitiznot, dragon scimitar, the anti-dragon shield (not the dragon defender: without the shield or an antifire potion their breath hits for up to 50; Oziach in Edgeville sells another if you lost yours), amulet of strength, barrows gloves, proselyte top and bottom, berserker ring (i), Ardougne cloak, climbing boots, Rada's blessing 2, and runes and a book for Resurrect Superior thralls (Greater at 76 Magic, boostable from 73 with a wizard's mind bomb). Take a games necklace to the Corporeal Beast, run east to the dragons (1,250 and 1,500 total-level worlds are usually empty) and, when you need to restore, walk back down to Wilderness level 20 or lower before you take the ring of dueling to Ferox (it does not work above level 20, and the Corporeal Beast's lair itself is at level 21). Use any runes you have for thralls and buy more if they run out, but don't let the stack drop below about 70,000 gp. Along the way, kill one mammoth and one ent, chopping yew logs from the ent for the diary. These are Wilderness Diary tasks: the mammoth (south-east of Ferox) is an easy one, and the yew logs are a medium one that needs 61 Woodcutting, not boostable, and a mithril axe. Ents stand north and east of the Chaos Temple, near the elder chaos druids. The Lesser Fanatic, east of the Edgeville jailhouse, hands out each tier's reward, and you must claim the tiers in order.

        Done whenyou have killed 1,300 green dragons and are 70 Defence.

        Choose how to handle the bones

        The UIM difference is the loot, and neither of the usual tools works for you. A UIM's looting bag can't be emptied: what goes in only comes out when you die or destroy the bag in the Wilderness (destroying it anywhere else, the Ferox Enclave included, deletes the contents, as does destroying it within the first two tiles beyond the Wilderness Ditch), so you can't offer bones from it. And a UIM gets no gravestone, so gravestone and suicide methods at the Chaos Altar don't apply. Choose one of these instead.

        UIM riskIf a player kills you the bag is lost to them, and they take everything in it except food and potions.

        The Wilderness with a deathpile, as in Chapter 1 (faster). Start each session with a planned death at the Edgeville Monastery, so that everything you don't need waits in the pile; a pile only exists after a death and lasts an hour. Take only the setup above, a ring of dueling, food and your thrall runes, and leave everything else in the pile, including untradeables you'd rather not lose: a death to a player turns most of what you carry into coins for them. Kill dragons until your free slots are full of bones, walk to the Chaos Temple, the church west of the Lava Maze at Wilderness level 38, and offer them (every bone has a 50% chance not to be used up, so each bone averages about seven times its normal Prayer XP). The temple is multi-combat and player killers often target people there, so offer the whole load quickly and leave. A ring of dueling only works up to Wilderness level 20, so run south to level 20 or lower before you take it to Ferox, then go back. The pile lasts an hour of logged-in time and a second death doesn't reset it, so keep each session well inside the hour, including the walk back. Every third or fourth trip, fill the slots with hides instead of bones and take them to Canifis for the next block's crafting.

        Varlamore's calcified rocks (no Wilderness). The HCIM option works for a UIM: mine calcified rocks for bone shards, which stack, turn them into blessed bone shards as the wiki describes and offer them at the libation bowl. The XP scales with your Mining level. Take Defence to 70 on defensive-style Slayer tasks or crabs instead of the dragons. It costs nothing and risks nothing, but gives no hides, so the next block's Crafting has to come from glassblowing.

        Either way, reach 60 Prayer first. You reach about 70 Prayer after 1,300 kills, which with 70 Defence unlocks Piety.

        Done whenyou are 70 Prayer with Piety unlocked.

        The King Black Dragon Lair and Pest Control

        After one inventory, run into the King Black Dragon Lair for the diary and leave by ring of dueling to Ferox. The lair is beneath the Lava Maze, and entering it is an easy Wilderness Diary task with no requirement, so claim that tier from the Lesser Fanatic along with the others. Take the Pest Control minigame teleport (the teleport has a 20-minute cooldown) and do one novice and one intermediate game. The novice boat needs combat level 40 and the intermediate 70. You may also be close to finishing the easy Combat Achievements by now, which gives a 5,000 XP lamp (Herblore, probably).

        Done whenthe lair task and two Pest Control games are done.

        Coins: about 70,000 gp at the low point · Slots: setup plus a full inventory of bones or hides · Requirements: Holy Grail, Murder Mystery, One Small Favour, 65 Defence and 45 Magic (neither boostable) for King's Ransom, plus a full black armour set and a guard disguise; King's Ransom for the Knight Waves; 70 Defence and 70 Prayer and King's Ransom's training for Piety

        Wiki referencesGreen dragonKing's RansomKnight Waves Training GroundsChaos Temple (church)Level 30 teleportLooting bagThralls

        The fossil cases, and green dragonhide for a UIM

        Gate inenough fossils and Curse of the Empty Lord for the cases (otherwise later); 57 to 63 Crafting for the hide items; 45 and 50 Fletching for maple bows.
        Carry inan inventory of hides, a needle and thread, and the bow kit: about 20/28.
        Exitthe fossil cases if ready; the hides crafted and alched; 60 Fletching on the way; about 270,000 gp after an hour of blackjacking.
        Carries forwardheadless arrows.

        The fossil cases

        If you have enough fossils for three display cases and have finished Curse of the Empty Lord, teleport to Varrock, pray at the palace altar with Smite active, build the three fossil display cases and talk to the information clerk (XP), Historian Minas (XP lamp) and Orlando Smith (diary). Historian Minas is on the first floor of the Varrock Museum. Collect the Varrock hard diary if you have its 153 kudos (the Varlamore block lists where the rest come from), lamp on Herblore. The kudos is one task, done by speaking to Orlando Smith. Praying with Smite at the palace altar is another, which this step already has you do, and the spottier cape needs the two dashing kebbit furs from the Eagles' Peak step (69 Hunter for an ironman). Toby, behind the Varrock General Store, hands out the reward once the earlier tiers are claimed, and the lamp is worth 15,000 XP at level 50 or higher. If you can't fill two cases yet, leave this for the Sailing block.

        Done whenthe cases are built, or set aside for the Sailing block.

        Green dragonhide for a UIM

        The green dragonhides look like the money here, but a UIM can't bank 1,300 of them, so tanning and crafting them all into bodies to alch doesn't work. What you can do:

        • Keep the Crafting. Bring an inventory of hides back every third or fourth dragon trip, tan them at Sbott in Canifis (Ectophial) and make the best item your level allows: vambraces at 57, chaps at 60, the shield at 62 (it also needs a maple shield and 15 steel nails) and bodies at 63. Sbott's shop is on the north side of Canifis, close to the CKS fairy ring, and charges 45 gp per dragonhide, a little over double most tanners, which you pay for the convenience. Every hide gives the same XP. Alch them as you go. If you took the calcified-rock option you have no hides, so skip this. Lunar Diplomacy's Tan Leather spell (78 Magic) would save the Canifis trip, but it also needs the Fremennik hard diary, which you only finish in 2.3, so it is not available yet.
        • Accept less money. The hides won't make much. The purchases before the Hallowed Sepulchre add up to a few hundred thousand, and the Sepulchre itself pays far more than the hides, so blackjack in Pollnivneach for the gap rather than grinding hides. You hold about 70,000 gp here, and the blocks up to the sloop need about 150,000 gp (Pellem's cape 20,000 gp, the next Sailing step about 30,000 gp, the accumulator upgrade and Pellem's boots about 20,000 gp, and up to 75,000 gp of oak planks), so blackjack for an hour (about 200,000 gp) before you leave Varrock.

        The rest of this block:

        • cut maple logs into arrow shafts, making one into a maple shortbow (u) and stringing it in Seers' Village bank for the diary (50 Fletching, bring a bow string);
        • make a superantipoison there;
        • buy feathers to reach 60 Fletching, and make the shafts into headless arrows, ideally while AFK underwater for seaweed spores.
        Done whenthe hides are crafted and alched, and you hold about 270,000 gp after blackjacking.

        Coins: about 270,000 gp after an hour of blackjacking (about 70,000 gp before it) · Slots: about 20/28 · Requirements: 57 Crafting for green d'hide vambraces, 60 for chaps, 62 for the shield, 63 for bodies; 45 Fletching for maple shortbows

        Wiki referencesGreen d'hide bodyTan LeatherFur pouchesVarrock Diary

        Varlamore, the Myreque finish and Sins of the Father

        Gate in51 Slayer; 53 Thieving, a ring of visibility and a ghostspeak amulet for Curse of the Empty Lord; Children of the Sun and Eagles' Peak (done in 2.2) for At First Light; the enchanted silvthrill rod and a rope; Sins of the Father's 62 Woodcutting, 60 Fletching, 56 Crafting, 52 Agility, 50 Attack, 50 Slayer and 49 Magic (none boostable); about 100,000 gp for air runes.
        Carry inthe quest items a part at a time and Efaritay's aid: about 24/28 in Varlamore.
        ExitAt First Light, Perilous Moons, The Heart of Darkness, Shadows of Custodia, In Aid of the Myreque, Darkness of Hallowvale, A Taste of Hope and Sins of the Father done; the mixed hide cape; 58 Slayer; about 250,000 gp.
        Carries forwarda few trapper's tipple, the Rod of Ivandis.
        Deathbank checkthis block has fights where a death is unsafe. If Hespori or another retrieval service holds your items, collect them before you start: an unsafe death here deletes them.

        Curse of the Empty Lord and the book

        You should be 51 Slayer or higher. Finish Curse of the Empty Lord if you haven't (it needs 53 Thieving, not boostable, a ring of visibility and a ghostspeak amulet or Morytania legs 2; the lamp comes from Historian Minas afterwards, not from the quest). If you haven't started it, Valdez, the ghost outside Glarial's Tomb near the Fishing Guild, begins it. Take the amulet if you can, because Morytania legs don't work in the first of the quest's three sequences. In Varrock, grab the Dagon'hai history book from the palace library if you don't have it, and pray at the palace altar with Smite active. Varlamore has been open since Children of the Sun in Chapter 1: take fairy ring AJP to Avium Savannah and walk into Civitas illa Fortis.

        • Do At First Light (46 Hunter, 30 Herblore, 27 Construction, none boostable; Guildmaster Apatura at the Hunter Guild starts it), then kill a jaguar for its fur, because the quest itself gives none. For the quest you need two jerboa tails from the embertailed jerboas north-west of Locus Oasis, or a box trap to catch them. Jaguars are level 67 and aggressive, hit up to 6, always drop one fur and roam the Avium Savannah and the land north of the Tlati Rainforest. A fur pouch won't hold jaguar fur, so it costs you a slot until Pellem takes it. Hunter Rumours aren't efficient now, but you can do them any time after this quest for the rewards.
        • At the Hunter Guild, collect 27 trapper's tipple in The Burrow (or buy them from Huntmaster Gilman in dialogue). Each tipple adds 2 Hunter but drains 2 Attack and 1 Strength, so drink one just before you need it. The cargo hold doesn't take them and each is a slot, so take only the few the next Hunter session needs and come back for more. Pay Pellem, in the south-east corner of the guild, 20,000 gp and the jaguar fur to make a mixed hide cape; he supplies the mixed hide base. The mixed hide boots wait until you have a sunlight antelope fur (72 Hunter, boostable; see the chinchompa step), when Pellem makes them for 17,500 gp. Wearing either needs 60 Ranged and 50 Defence, and making them yourself needs 68 or 69 Crafting, which you don't have yet.
        • Start and complete Perilous Moons (the dragon scimitar is fine for every fight, including ice; use moss lizards and moonlight potions for supplies) and The Heart of Darkness. Perilous Moons starts with Zuma or Attala at the Cam Torum entrance on Ralos' Rise and needs five free slots. Protection prayers don't reduce the Moons' damage, so stand in the highlighted circle during their normal attacks, and if you log out during Blue Moon's weapon freeze your weapon stays unequipped until you re-enter the fight; the wiki's UIM guide uses this as storage for one weapon, kept even through an unsafe death. The Heart of Darkness starts with Prince Itzla Arkan at the Teomat and needs 30 gp for a room at the Windbreaker, which has no basement. In the icons puzzle, note the murals before you search any urn, because early searches spawn frost nagua you can't safespot, and touch the statues in the right order or rocks fall on you. At the end, travel to Tal Teklan and buy four willow longbows at Arcuani's Archery Supplies for Shadows of Custodia. Do Scrambled! if you haven't.
        • Travel to Auburnvale, do the Vale Totems miniquest, and start and complete Shadows of Custodia. It starts at the notice board in the Auburn Pub and needs 54 Slayer, 45 Fishing, 41 Construction and 36 Hunter, so check your Slayer after the two quests before it. Shas and Etz won't talk to you until you give the four longbows to Captain Ariadna and reinforce the scratched wall with four maple logs cut from the town's maple trees and a hammer (one spawns in the furnace building north of the town; the general store also sells one). Only Antos can be rescued. The last fight is three level 93 creatures in a multi-combat side chamber reached by running east inside the cave: use Protect from Melee, and standing still slows the bleed they cause.
        Done whenCurse of the Empty Lord is done.

        Potions to 60 Herblore

        Sins of the Father's tome gives 15,000 XP a use in any skill at level 60 or higher, six uses, and this guide puts every use on Herblore (A Taste of Hope's tome is only 2,500 XP, three times, at level 35 or higher, so it never needs the 60). By now the lamps and quests leave you at about 58 Herblore, about 40,000 XP short of 60, so make that much in potions before you finish Sins of the Father: about 400 super attacks or about 320 super strengths from your herb runs, made at Catherby in rounds of 9. If you reach the end of the quest below 60, keep the tome (one slot) and spend it the moment you reach 60, because putting it on another skill would leave about 90,000 XP more to make later.

        Done whenyou are 60 Herblore before you finish Sins of the Father, or the tome waits in your inventory for it.

        Coins: about 3,000 gp · Slots: about 18/28 during the rounds · Requirements: 45 Herblore for super attack, 55 for super strength

        The Rod of Ivandis and the Myreque quests

        Then take a rope and the enchanted silvthrill rod, take fairy ring CKS and go to Paterdomus. Dip the rod in the well to make the Rod of Ivandis and complete In Aid of the Myreque. The rod has 10 charges and turns to dust when they run out. It is untradeable, the wiki says Veliaf has no replacement if you lose it, and A Taste of Hope needs it later. The rest of In Aid of the Myreque (Veliaf, the Burgh de Rott repairs and Ivan's escort) was done in 2.1, so the well is all that is left. Do Darkness of Hallowvale, A Taste of Hope and Sins of the Father, all lamps on Herblore. Sins of the Father starts with Veliaf outside the church in Slepe. Wear Efaritay's aid for the Vanstrom Klause fight in Sins of the Father; one ring should be enough, and the second is the spare in case you die or run out of charges. Fight him in melee with the Blisterwood flail and turn off auto-retaliate.

        UIM riskA Taste of Hope starts with Garth outside the Theatre of Blood entrance in Ver Sinhaza and ends with a tome that gives 2,500 XP in each of three skills; the Ranis Drakan fight has a deathbank at the chest north-west of the notice board, and an unsafe death deletes what is in it.
        UIM riskThe fight has a deathbank at Veliaf, and an unsafe death deletes its contents.
        Done whenSins of the Father is complete.

        Lamps and loose ends

        The lamp plan: Slayer until 58 for cave horrors (about 30,000 quest XP is still to come), then Herblore. This also unlocks Yarnio's Baked Goods, a better garden pie shop than Romily Weaklax. You can now finish the Varrock hard diary (information clerk, Historian Minas for the Curse of the Empty Lord lamp, Orlando Smith). It needs 153 kudos. Chapter 1 left you at about 105 (the quiz's 28, the five display finds' 50 and Historian Minas's quests); since then, tell Minas about Making History, A Tail of Two Cats, What Lies Below (with the Dagon'hai history book), In Aid of the Myreque and the Observatory if you haven't yet (5 each) and Curse of the Empty Lord (10), which makes about 140. The rest come from the Fossil Island fossil displays (2 kudos for each display filled with its five fossils, so seven displays cover it) and the first eight salvaging relics, handed to Curator Haig Halen (1 each). Defender of Varrock's 5 come later. At any point, kill a brine rat for the Fremennik medium diary (lamp on Herblore).

        Done whenthe lamps are spent and the Varrock hard and Fremennik medium diaries are claimed.

        Coins: about 250,000 gp · Slots: about 24/28 in Varlamore · Requirements: Children of the Sun, Eagles' Peak and 46 Hunter, 30 Herblore and 27 Construction (not boostable) for At First Light, and the listed quests for Perilous Moons, The Heart of Darkness and Shadows of Custodia; the Myreque quests in order; Sins of the Father's 62 Woodcutting, 60 Fletching, 56 Crafting, 52 Agility, 50 Attack, 50 Slayer and 49 Magic (none boostable), plus 1,950 gp for a vyrewatch outfit, a ruby and a chisel

        Wiki referencesAt First LightPerilous MoonsThe Heart of DarknessRod of ivandisSins of the Father

        Sailing to 50, Troubled Tortugans and blue crabs to 61 Hunter

        Gate in51 Slayer, 48 Construction, 45 Sailing, 45 Hunter, 40 Woodcutting and 34 Crafting for Troubled Tortugans; the fish offcuts; a lockpick for the tarnished chest (Martin Thwait's Lost and Found in the Rogues' Den, 20 gp, if you dropped the Dorgesh-Kaan one); 42 Sailing to dock at Chinchompa Island.
        Carry inan axe and 4 free slots for the quest; then food, teleports, a lockpick, a pickaxe and the camp key (about 15/28), with fish and cannonballs in the cargo hold.
        ExitTroubled Tortugans done; 50+ Sailing; Ex-Captain Siad recruited; the salvaging station schematic read; the lead bars made and the hooks and keel built; 61 Hunter; coral farming started; about 220,000 gp.
        Carries forwardthe medallion fragments, 50 pilar fragments.
        Deathbank checkthis block has fights where a death is unsafe. If Hespori or another retrieval service holds your items, collect them before you start: an unsafe death here deletes them.

        Jobless Jim

        Take the Pest Control minigame teleport and the boat to Port Sarim and recruit Jobless Jim; he can man the ship and trim the sails, for half the trimming XP but easier sailing. He is in the Rusty Anchor Inn and needs 40 Sailing to hire.

        Done whenJobless Jim is recruited.

        Troubled Tortugans

        RequirementsTroubled Tortugans: 51 Slayer, 48 Construction, 45 Sailing, 45 Hunter, 40 Woodcutting and 34 Crafting to start, an axe (a better one than the bronze axe found in the quest, for the 10 logs), a hammer and saw (both obtainable during it) and at least 4 free slots.

        Charter to the Pandemonium, take your skiff to the start of Troubled Tortugans and complete it. The start is the injured tortugan Floopa on a remote island north-west of the Great Conch.
