The wiki also asks for the Pandemonium quest. The level 95 gryphon only uses melee: stand next to the wall with Protect from Melee. For the level 235 Shellbane gryphon, keep the tortugan shield equipped the whole fight and use Protect from Missiles near the shore. Its slam knocks you back and deals heavy damage if your worn weight is under 40 kg (heavy armour avoids it), so stay out of its reach. If you are struggling, hide behind the palm trees and let the tortugans bring its health down to 20. If you die in the fight, a ship moored at the Little Pearl counts as lost and must be recovered from a shipwright. On the Great Conch, collect the spyglass and the strange apple tree charter.

        Rewards1 quest point, 10,000 Sailing and 8,000 Slayer XP and access to the Great Conch.
        Done whenTroubled Tortugans is complete.

        Courier tasks to 50 Sailing

        RequirementsThe Summer Shore needs 45 Sailing and Troubled Tortugans, and Ruins of Unkah tasks join at 48.

        Do port courier tasks to the Summer Shore until 50 Sailing, finishing the Unquiet Ocean charting on the way (the Sea of Shells after 47). Courier tasks come from notice boards: you collect cargo at the pickup port's ledger table with both hands free, put it in your cargo hold and deposit it at the destination's ledger table. The ocean completion bonus (6,000 XP) can take you to 50; aim for 51–53 to need fewer boosts while salvaging. Drink the bottle that sends you home (to your house, with your ship to Port Sarim) only when convenient; it helps set up a courier task. At the Onyx Crest, dock and dig for the small key for the lost schematics, and kill pirates for a medallion fragment.

        Done whenyou are 50 Sailing or more.

        Ex-Captain Siad, Chinchompa Island and Port Roberts

        Requirements54 Thieving, which can be boosted, and a lockpick lifts the success chance at 54 from about 32% to about 42%.

        At 50, ring of dueling and run south to the desert mining camp, run out and recruit Ex-Captain Siad (don't forget the camp key). He is on the first floor of a building in the camp, and hiring him needs 50 Sailing (boostable) and The Tourist Trap. You can get the key again after the quest if you lost it. Teleport to Port Piscarilius with the book of the dead that A Kingdom Divided gave you (Commander Fullore, on the ground floor of Kourend Castle, replaces a lost one; without it, a house teleport to Hosidius and a walk north), recover the skiff and sail south to Chinchompa Island. The island is south-east of Hosidius. Chart the charred remains of a table and open the lockbox on its northern end for the salvaging station schematic; with the small key from the Pandemonium it gives the schematic, and without the key it only points you back to where the key is. Dock at Port Roberts: buy lead ore (80, plus 22 more for the steel keel and the salvaging hooks, which 2.1 left for here) in loads you can carry, about 26 ore at a time, and smelt each load into bars at the next furnace you pass; build the hooks and keel from them as soon as you can, because the cargo hold won't take ore or bars. Steal from the silver stall for a medallion fragment. With a lockpick, charter to Aldarin, sail to the Shimmering Atoll (49 Sailing to moor) and pickpocket the tarnished chest for 50 pilar fragments. A failed attempt can teleport you a short way off, so walk back and try again.

        Done whenSiad is recruited, the schematic is read and the hooks and keel are built.

        Blue crabs to 61 Hunter

        Take the minigame teleport to Pest Control or Port Khazard, charter to the Great Conch and catch blue crabs until 61 Hunter (60 is fine). Blue crabs only need 48 Hunter, so you are well inside the range; each is a trap on the Great Conch and gives 136 Hunter XP. Use the fish offcuts you kept, boosting with trapper's tipple for the first two levels, and grind the crabs into crab paste with a pestle and mortar as you go; the paste is what the super hunter potions use. It's a good chance to fletch or alch. Grow pilar coral too, and from 67 Herblore make super hunter potions and keep boosting for all future Hunter; boosting also unlocks an extra trap. If you didn't keep enough offcuts, buy sardines in Rellekka and cut them with a knife, or at higher Sailing cut them at a chum station on your ship.

        Done whenyou are 61 Hunter.

        Coral farming

        RequirementsPillar coral needs 52 Farming.

        Coral farming starts here, so add coral to your farm runs: charter to the Summer Shore, take the small boat to the north-east side of the island and dive. The fairy ring CJQ on the Great Conch is the other way in, and the nurseries sit off the eastern dock. You can dive only with a diving apparatus and a fishbowl helmet, or with a medallion of the deep. Each nursery takes one frag, no compost, and the coral grows over four 40-minute stages. Harvested coral can't go in the herb sack. On your first visit, search the coral on the west side for a medallion fragment. Paying for protection isn't worth it, though the gardener Chet will protect both nurseries if you change your mind.

        Done whenthe coral is planted and the medallion fragment found.

        Coins: about 220,000 gp · Slots: inventory, plus fish and cannonballs in the cargo hold · Requirements: Troubled Tortugans' Sailing and quests; 61 Hunter for the target; 67 Herblore for super hunter potions

        Wiki referencesTroubled TortugansSailing trainingBlue crabCoral

        Chinchompas, Ranged to 62 and the large fur pouch

        Gate inEagles' Peak and 53 Hunter (63 for red); Monkey Madness I for the tunnels; 45 and 55 Ranged; 72 Hunter (boostable) for the sunlight antelope; 65 Crafting (boostable) for the large fur pouch.
        Carry inan axe, a knife-log and the chinchompa stacks: about 18/28.
        Exit62 Ranged; the accumulator; the kyatt and antelope furs; the large fur pouch; the mixed hide boots ordered; about 200,000 gp.
        Carries forwardthe antelope fur (Pellem's boots).

        Chinchompas and Ranged

        Catch about 1,500 grey chinchompas at Soul Wars (take an axe for the nearby teaks in case you ruin your knife-log) and about 1,500 red chinchompas between Kastori and Tal Teklan. Grey chinchompas come from box traps and need 53 Hunter and Eagles' Peak; the catch rate is about 146 in 255 at 53 and climbs with every level, so boosts matter. Chinchompas stack, so they're one slot each for a UIM. Use any boosts you have left for catch rate. Train Ranged in the Monkey Madness I tunnels with short fuse to 62: grey chins from 45 (their wield level), red from 55 to 62. Stay within three squares of what you hit, because short fuse is fully accurate only at that range and drops to 75% at four to six squares. Wear the salve amulet (ei). If you can make ranging potions, teleport for an inventory of Zamorak wines and make them all, repotting often.

        Done whenyou are 62 Ranged.

        Alternatives and Ranged upgrades

        Alternatives: AFK the Ranged at crabs instead (70 at most); or after 50 Ranged do A Night at the Theatre for four Ranged lamps (80,000 XP), probably with someone carrying you. At 50 Ranged, upgrade your Ava's attractor to an accumulator through Ava (the wiki lists 75 steel arrows plus 999 gp for the upgrade) or buy it from Perdu. Ava is in Draynor Manor, and the accumulator needs 50 Ranged, not boostable. It stops working while you wear a metal body. If you lose it, Ava sells a replacement for the same arrows and coins. At 60 Ranged, wear the mixed hide cape and, once you have them, the boots. Optionally do the Fight Caves for a fire cape now, though waiting for Chapter 3 is better.

        Done whenthe accumulator is upgraded and the cape is worn.

        The large fur pouch

        When convenient, catch three sabre-toothed kyatts north of fairy ring DKS for their furs and the diary, and one sunlight antelope east of fairy ring AJP for its fur (72 Hunter, boostable), which Pellem needs for the mixed hide boots. Kyatts need 55 Hunter and a pitfall trap: a teasing stick, a knife and logs (not redwood or arctic pine). Tatty fur is the common drop and doesn't count, so you need three regular furs. With the kyatt furs you can upgrade to the large fur pouch (65 Crafting, boostable, with a needle and thread), which holds 28 furs instead of 21.

        Done whenyou hold the large fur pouch and the antelope fur.

        Coins: about 200,000 gp at the end (you arrive with about 220,000 gp and spend about 20,000 gp on the accumulator upgrade and Pellem's boots) · Slots: about 18/28 · Requirements: Eagles' Peak and 53 Hunter for grey chinchompas, 63 for red; Monkey Madness I for the tunnels; 45 Ranged to wield grey chinchompas and 55 for red; 72 Hunter for the sunlight antelope; 65 Crafting (boostable) and 3 kyatt furs for the large fur pouch

        Wiki referencesChinchompaRed chinchompaAva's assemblerSabre-toothed kyatt

        Smelting, nails and planks for the Sepulchre

        Gate in50 Smithing for mithril bars, 54 for nails; 34 Herblore for agility potions; up to 75,000 gp for oak planks.
        Carry inore and the coal bag, then the plank sack and log basket: about 10/28.
        Exit975 mithril nails; 28 oak planks in the sack and the means to refill it; 12 four-dose agility potions; about 125,000 gp.
        Carries forwardthe nails and planks (the Sepulchre), the agility potions (floor 4 boosts).

        Smelting and nails

        Use the wiki's hybrid ore UIM smelting method here, which suits you as it is. Smelt your mithril ore from the Motherlode Mine, your lead ore and your gold ore. At the Varrock west anvils, make 65 mithril bars into 975 mithril nails (each bar gives 15); nails stack. A UIM can't bank the rest of the mithril bars in case you need more nails later, so smelt those later from ore if the nails run short.

        Done whenyou hold 975 mithril nails.

        Planks for the bridges

        The Sepulchre bridges use oak planks with mithril nails, and you need 300 oak planks. A bridge takes two planks and five nails and needs 56 Construction. A UIM can't bank them. The plank sack holds 28, which is several sessions of bridges, so refill it between sessions at the Auburnvale sawmill with oak logs from the log basket. Planks you buy at the sawmill go straight into the sack. Sawmill operators don't take noted logs, so there is no noted-plank shortcut. The sawmill fee is 250 gp for each oak plank (wiki Oak plank), so 300 planks cost up to 75,000 gp.

        Done whenthe plank sack holds 28 oak planks.

        Agility potions

        Make 16 three-dose agility potions and decant them into 12 four-dose potions, for the floor 4 boosts. The cargo hold doesn't take potions, so carry them; make them just before the Sepulchre if the slots matter, or decant them noted at Bob Barter at the Grand Exchange (one slot a type) and unnote them at a bank booth before you go in.

        Done whenyou hold 12 four-dose agility potions.

        Coins: about 125,000 gp (after up to 75,000 gp of oak planks) · Slots: about 10/28 · Requirements: 50 Smithing for mithril bars, 54 for mithril nails; 34 Herblore for agility potions

        Wiki referencesUltimate Ironman GuideMithril nailsPhialsAgility potion

        The sloop and salvaging to 55 Sailing

        Gate in200,000 gp for the sloop (blackjack an hour first); the salvaging station schematic read (Troubled Tortugans block); 42 Sailing and 34 Construction for the station; Ex-Captain Siad; 50+ Sailing for salvaging.
        Carry infour empty beer glasses, high alchemy runes, the log basket and the seed box.
        Exitthe sloop with two kegs, the salvaging station and two mithril hooks; 55 Sailing; hemp and cotton seeds planted; about 125,000 gp plus alchs.
        Carries forwardhemp (bolts of canvas in 2.3), the 25 kraken ink stout in the keg.

        Buy and fit the sloop

        RequirementsThe sloop needs 50 Sailing and has five crew slots, so Jim and Siad both fit.

        The sloop costs 200,000 gp at a shipwright (wiki Sloop) and you hold about 125,000 gp, so blackjack in Pollnivneach for an hour (about 200,000 gp) before you charter. Charter to the Pandemonium and buy the 25 kraken ink stout from Chartin' Charles McAtless, then charter to Port Khazard and buy a sloop. Build two kegs (fill one with whirlpool surprise and one with kraken ink stout), a salvaging station and two mithril salvaging hooks. The station needs the salvaging station schematic, which you read from the Chinchompa Island lockbox in the Troubled Tortugans block. The station itself is 4 teak planks and 16 steel nails with a saw and a hammer, at 42 Sailing and 34 Construction (372 Construction XP), and assign Ex-Captain Siad to the boat.

        Done whenthe sloop has its kegs, station and hooks, with Siad aboard.

        Sail to the shipwrecks

        Sail the sloop to the large shipwrecks far south, north of the Isle of Serpents; don't detour for charting with the slow sloop. Large wrecks need 53 Sailing, so at 50 you are boosting. Bring four empty beer glasses, high alchemy runes on the standard spellbook, the log basket and the seed box, and optionally a way to 1.5-tick the salvaging.

        Done whenyou are at the shipwrecks with the salvaging kit.

        Salvage to 55 Sailing

        Salvage to 55 Sailing, boosting where needed (it is worth boosting even at 53) and alching diamond rings, except one, which becomes the ring of life for The Curse of Arrav two blocks on. The goal is hemp and cotton seeds. Plant the hemp as soon as you can, with Attas and ultracompost, for at least 40 hemp in total, about six seeds. Hemp goes in a hops patch at 37 Farming, three seeds to a patch, and takes 80 minutes to grow, so six seeds fill two patches. If you don't get enough, either postpone the ship upgrades until after the albatrosses in Chapter 3 or kill them earlier for the seeds.

        Done whenyou are 55 Sailing and the hemp is planted.

        Coins: about 125,000 gp after the sloop's 200,000 gp and an hour of blackjacking before it, plus alchs · Slots: the sloop's storage and your inventory · Requirements: the sloop's Sailing level and 50+ Sailing for salvaging

        Wiki referencesSloopSalvagingHemp seed

        The Hallowed Sepulchre to 84 Agility

        Gate inSins of the Father (Darkmeyer); 77 Agility for floor 4, boostable from 74; the nails, planks and agility potions; 62 Ranged and a mithril grapple before the floor 3 grapples.
        Carry inagility potions for the floor 4 boost, the plank sack with oak planks, mithril nails, a saw (the crystal saw works) and a hammer (the hallowed hammer once you buy it), the herb sack, arrow shafts, feathers and arrowheads for headless arrows, and the rune bolts you will alch: about 19/28.
        Exit84 Agility and about 2.1 million gp (you start near 125,000 gp); the hallowed hammer and focus.
        Carries forwardheadless arrows (one stack), hallowed marks.

        Do, in this order:

        1. Take the runs from Darkmeyer (Sins of the Father done) and enter floor 4 with 77 Agility, boosted if you need it.
        2. Loot the floor 4 bridge and the floor 4 enchantment obstacle, each on one side only, and the floor 3 enchantment obstacle on one side. Leave the floor 3 grapples until you hold a crossbow and a mithril grapple. Skip floors 1 and 2.
        3. Fletch headless arrows and alch bolts while you move, and keep up your hourlies.
        4. Spend marks in this order: hammer, focus, instances, ring, grapple.

        This is the big grind and the biggest money-maker. It is UIM-friendly: the runs start and end in Darkmeyer, the loot is mostly coins, runes and stackables, and you carry very little. Read the whole block before starting. The entrance is on the north-east side of Darkmeyer. Drakan's medallion from A Taste of Hope teleports you to the town, and a single-use hallowed crystal shard takes you to the lobby. Floor 1 gives you two minutes and each lower floor adds two, a hallowed token adds one, and spare time carries over. The clock stops after the last obstacle of a floor, and you get the XP when you take the stairs or leave by an obelisk. Once time is up you can finish the floor for XP but can't loot coffins or go on. A trap sends you back to just before it, and costs time and a little health unless you own the hallowed ring. A failed coffin challenge poisons you.

        UIM riskIf you die, the Mysterious Stranger in the lobby keeps your items, but anything you dropped on the floor is lost, and an unsafe death elsewhere before you collect them deletes them.

        Floors and coffins. You do floor 4 runs (77 Agility to enter) until 84 Agility, boosting for floor 4 at 74 with an agility potion (or 72 with a summer pie, which you may not have; use pies at 82–83 if you do, for earlier floor 5). Floors 2, 3 and 5 need 62, 72 and 87 Agility, and all can be boosted. Loot these:

        • the floor 4 bridge, on one side, so half your runs. Buy the hallowed hammer first, so you never break nails building it. Use oak planks with mithril nails until you can use teak with adamant;
        • the floor 4 enchantment obstacle, on one side, almost always worth looting. With the hallowed focus (100 marks) you never fail and it uses your lowest enchant; without it, use Lvl-5 Enchant until you have the focus, then Lvl-3. The XP is the same for every level;
        • the floor 3 enchantment obstacle, on one side, which works the same way;
        • the floor 3 grapples, on both sides, once you have 62 Ranged, a crossbow and a mithril grapple, which the next block has you make. Until then leave them: failing costs a lot of time. The hallowed grapple (100 marks) never fails inside the crypt but still needs the crossbow, so it is optional and not recommended;
        • not floors 1 and 2, unless you want the collection log.

        Hallowed shop order. Hammer, then focus, then instances, then the ring, then the grapple. The hammer, focus and grapple cost 100 marks each and the ring 250, and a private instance is 200 marks from the Mysterious Stranger. The hammer removes the chance that a bridge repair fails and bends your nails. Buy the hallowed symbol only if you already have vampyre dust; collecting them isn't efficient. Keep a few hallowed tokens for a bad run. After the useful items, spend marks on hallowed crystal shards (teleports) or save them.

        Multiskilling. Do fletchpalchre: fletch headless arrows and high-alch while moving. Bring the plank sack (and the herb sack, if you have already bought it), and keep up hourlies. Blowing glass inside the Sepulchre is even more efficient; most of the time goes on alching either way. Alch all your rune bolts for Magic XP. For a change of pace, AFK daeyalt essence for later Runecraft, or the high-effort "dae-alch" method.

        You can split this grind or stop below 84; the levels-per-loot depend on how much Barbarian Fishing you did, and crab's updated Sepulchre calculator helps with planning (it works from 72, excludes floor 1 bridges, and by default doesn't sell law and soul runes).

        Done whenyou are 84 Agility with about 2.1 million gp.

        Coins: rising by about 2 million, from about 125,000 gp to about 2.1 million gp · Slots: about 19/28 with planks in the sack · Requirements: Sins of the Father for Darkmeyer; 52 Agility for floor 1, 77 (boostable) for floor 4

        Wiki referencesHallowed SepulchreHallowed hammerHallowed focusDarkmeyer

        Blast mining, UIM gold to 74 Smithing, grapples and the medium diaries

        This splits into an essential Smithing part and an optional Mining part for later Sepulchre runs. It costs about 2.8 million gp and you arrive with about 2.1 million, so you cannot pay for all of it. Spend down to about 300,000 gp, the required part first, and leave the optional blast mining and adamant nails (dynamite and bars are most of the bill) until after the next Sepulchre block, when the stack has recovered. Do not blackjack for this block. The required part is the one everything else leans on: the mithril grapples open The Curse of Arrav, The Path of Glouphrie and a Falador diary step here, and they are also what the floor 3 pillars of the Sepulchre were waiting for, because a grapple never breaks inside the Sepulchre but has about a 1 in 25 chance of breaking on every use anywhere else. The optional part can wait because adamant nails only pay off on the teak bridges of floor 5, which the next block reaches.

        Gate inabout 2.1 million gp, spent down to about 300,000; 43 Mining and the Lovakengj blast mine; 59 Fletching and 59 Smithing for grapples; the quests' requirements.
        Carry inthe Blast Furnace setup: about 10/28.
        Exitabout 15,000 XP short of 74 Smithing; five mithril grapples; a mithril 2h sword for Devious Minds; Defender of Varrock, The Curse of Arrav and The Path of Glouphrie done; the Ardougne, Kandarin, Lumbridge & Draynor medium diaries and the Falador grapple step done; about 300,000 gp.
        Carries forwardthe mithril 2h sword (Devious Minds in Chapter 3), the grapples, the adamant nails if made.
        • Optional, recommended: blast mining to 380 adamantite ore in total, buying dynamite from the shop as needed. A 22-ore loop gives about 390–400 dynamite an hour. The blast mine is in the north of Lovakengj, and the wiki lists a Xeric's talisman, Kharedst's memoirs and the Lovakengj minecart station as ways in, each followed by a short run north, so use whichever you already own. You need a chisel and a tinderbox (the pickaxe isn't used), and Thirus, beside the mine, sells the dynamite. Light each charge and step at least two tiles away at once, or it hits you for about 15 damage, and put the blasted ore into the ore sack promptly, because loose ore disintegrates after three minutes.
        • Required: the wiki's UIM gold Blast Furnace method to 70 Smithing; the foreman lets you use the furnace free from 60. Wear the ring of charos (a) until 60 for the lower fee: below 60 the furnace costs 2,500 gp per 10 minutes of use, and the ring halves that through the foreman's dialogue, while the waiver at 60 can't be boosted. The bar dispenser holds 28 bars, and the furnace refuses more ore until you have collected them, so empty it before each new load. Make a mithril 2h sword for Devious Minds and smith about 5 mithril bars into grapples. Turn the rest of your mithril bars into mithril platebodies and alch them. Hybrid-smelt your adamantite into bars, buying coal as needed. Use UIM gold to train to 15,000 XP short of 74 Smithing.
        • Defender of Varrock: start and complete it. Elias White in the Jolly Boar Inn starts it, and the wiki asks for 55 Smithing and 52 Hunter before he will talk to you. In Camdozaal, make imbued barronite from a chaos core (dropped by chaos golems on the east side) and a barronite deposit (mined from the rocks on the west side; a barronite shard is a different item and doesn't work). The last part is the descendant puzzle in Varrock Palace: search the scrolls in the library's north-east corner before you read the census, or you have to destroy the list and read them in the other order. The free 5,000 XP lamp after the quest at Historian Minas goes on Slayer if you aren't 58 yet, Herblore otherwise.
        • Optional, recommended: make 5,700 adamant nails at the Varrock west anvils, keeping one adamantite bar; they stack. You can stop at 70–73 and boost for the nails. Don't do arteglass to 84 Thieving here; you are already 85 from blackjacking. A floor 5 bridge takes 2 planks and 5 nails, which is why this nail stock and the teak plank stock in the next block are sized together, and teak planks with adamant nails give 450 Construction XP a bridge against 300 for oak and mithril.
        • Required: make five mithril grapples and complete the Ardougne, Kandarin and Lumbridge & Draynor medium diaries (lamps on Slayer until 58, then Herblore), and the grapple step of the Falador medium diary. Each grapple is made in three steps: smith a grapple tip from a mithril bar at an anvil (59 Smithing), attach it to a mithril bolt (59 Fletching), then use that on a rope, which needs only 1 Fletching. The Falador step means grappling up the north wall of Falador and jumping off it, with a crossbow and the grapple both equipped (11 Agility, 37 Strength and 19 Ranged). You claim the Falador medium reward later, from Sir Rebral in the White Knights' Castle courtyard, in the Grim Tales block. Five is enough for the two quests and the diary step with a few spare for the 1 in 25 breakage.
        • Required, any time from here: with a grapple you can do The Curse of Arrav and The Path of Glouphrie. Do them now. The Curse of Arrav starts with Elias White at Uzer Oasis (fairy ring DLQ, then run east toward the coast) and needs 64 Mining, 62 Ranged, 62 Thieving, 61 Agility, 58 Strength and 37 Slayer. Bring three dwellberries (McGrubor's Wood, fairy ring ALS), a pickaxe, any crossbow except the love crossbow, a mithril grapple, insulated boots, a ring of life and your fighting gear. Chapter 1 had you drop its ring of life, so make another: keep one of the diamond rings from the salvaging block above instead of alching it, and cast Lvl-4 Enchant on it (57 Magic, a cosmic rune and 10 earth runes). Buy the insulated boots for 200 gp at any Slayer Equipment shop, such as the one in Canifis or Edgeville (they need 37 Slayer to wear), and pick the three dwellberries on the way; drop the boots once the quest is done. The quest has you use the berries and then the ring on the canopic jar, in that order, so you don't wear it. The golem guard (level 141) can't be safespotted: when the screen shakes and it draws its arms in, step one tile away from its three fast hits. In the vault of Zemouregal's base, equip the crossbow and grapple and use the grapple on the metal bars above the fire traps instead of walking across, which the wiki says costs about 14 damage and wakes you up far away. Arrav (level 339) throws his axe every third attack, so move behind him as it winds up. Bring an emergency teleport, since Zemouregal blocks the vault exit, and antivenom or a serpentine helm for the venom pools. The wiki's death notes talk about graves, which a UIM doesn't have: a death to the golem guard leaves your items outside the mastaba and a death to Arrav in the caves past the zombies, so read both as a deathpile on the 60-minute clock. The Path of Glouphrie starts with King Bolren in the Tree Gnome Village and needs 60 Strength, 56 Slayer, 56 Thieving, 47 Ranged and 45 Agility, none boostable. Bring a crossbow and grapple, combat gear, food and 10 to 14 free slots for the crystalline disks; anything on the floor vanishes after the cutscene in Yewnock's room. The three warped terrorbirds use both melee and ranged attacks, so stand where they can't melee you, pray Ranged and fight them one at a time at the door or around the pillars, with the crystal chime in hand since you can't hurt them without it.
        Done whenthe required parts are done and you hold about 300,000 gp.

        Coins: about 300,000 gp at the end (you arrive with about 2.1 million and stop spending there) · Slots: about 10/28 for the Blast Furnace · Requirements: 43 Mining and the Lovakengj blast mine for blast mining; 59 Fletching and 59 Smithing for mithril grapples; the quests' requirements

        Wiki referencesBlast mineBlast FurnaceMithril grappleDefender of VarrockThe Curse of ArravThe Path of Glouphrie

        The Sepulchre to 98 Agility, and teak planks

        Gate in84 Agility; 87 Agility (boostable) for floor 5; 84 Thieving or 92 Agility (boostable) for the grand coffin; a 300,000 gp margin over each teak refill.
        Carry inas for floor 4, with teak planks and adamant nails in place of oak and mithril.
        Exit98 Agility; about 37.3 million gp; about a million headless arrows; the rune stacks from the loot.
        Carries forwardthe headless arrows (broad arrows in Chapter 3), the runes, a ring of endurance if it dropped.

        Floor 5 and the grand coffin

        RequirementsA bridge needs a saw (the crystal saw works), a hammer, 2 planks, 5 nails and 56 Construction.

        Optional, and a very long grind; if you aren't aiming to max, reduce or split it, though you will then have fewer prayer potions for later Slayer. From 84 you can boost for floor 5 with agility potions (make them with the rest of your toad's legs as needed) or from 82 with summer pies. Floor 5 always has an enchantment obstacle, a grapple, a bridge and the grand coffin, and every one is worth looting. The timer starts at 2 minutes on floor 1 and gains 2 more for each floor you descend, which leaves about ten minutes on floor 5 if you waste none, and time you save carries over. If it runs out you can finish the floor for XP but can no longer loot or descend, so on a bad run you can leave by an obelisk. Wear a Saradomin item (anything from the hallowed shop counts) or the obelisks won't restore your run energy, and wear no other god's item, which drains your run energy there. An enchantment frame uses the highest Enchant spell you can cast and breaks after two failures, unless you hold the hallowed focus, which stops failures and makes you cast the lowest spell you have runes for. Since 12 August 2026 the Grand Hallowed Coffin needs 84 Thieving or 92 Agility, both boostable (the wiki's intro still shows the older 66 Thieving with 92 Agility; the changelog is the newer source). Each grand coffin gives 200 Thieving XP and 8 to 10 hallowed marks. Floors 3 and 4 stay as before; the floor 3 enchant is the best of the optional loots if you want a little more. Keep doing fletchpalchre all the way.

        Done whenyou are doing floor 5 runs.

        Teak planks without Miscellania

        The bridges need about 2,400 teak planks, and Managing Miscellania isn't available to a UIM, so make them yourself: between Sepulchre sessions, chop teak at Avium Savannah with the log basket open, as in the Mahogany Homes block, and turn the logs into planks at the Auburnvale sawmill with the log basket and plank sack (two inventories a trip), refilling the plank sack between sessions as with the oak. Two planks go into each bridge, so 2,400 planks is about 1,200 bridges, with the adamant nails from the previous block as the matching stock. It costs Woodcutting time and the sawmill's 500 gp for every teak plank, about 1.2 million gp for 2,400. Pay that from the Sepulchre loot as you go: refill the sack (28 planks, 14,000 gp) only when the stack covers it with a margin of about 300,000 gp, and if the stack runs short, do another session of floor 4 runs on the cheaper oak planks and mithril nails before the next teak refill. Blackjack only as a last resort.

        Done whenyour teak refills run between sessions.

        Fletching supplies

        Buy arrow shafts (or cut maples) and feathers in smaller batches, for a total of one million headless arrows through fletchpalchre. Keep alching rune bolts. Arrows stack, so a million of them cost one slot, and Chapter 3 turns them into broad arrows.

        Done whenheadless arrows are fletched as you go.

        What the grind gives you

        The average loot and XP for 84–98 is about 38.2 million gp (before fletching, rune and plank costs); 757,000 Thieving, 511,000 Construction, about 600,000 Fletching and 3.2 million Magic XP; and large stacks of cosmic, death, law, blood and soul runes, 1,700 monkfish, prayer potions, sanfew serums, ranarr weeds and seeds, about 107 strange lockpicks and a 98% chance of at least one ring of endurance. For a UIM the stackables are easy to hold, but the potions, monkfish and weeds aren't: drink or use them as they come, or decant them noted at Bob Barter at the Grand Exchange and unnote them at a bank booth when you need them. The cargo hold takes cooked fish such as the monkfish, but not potions or weeds. A ring of endurance is worth keeping: it needs 70 Agility to wear and holds up to 1,000 stamina potion doses, which takes those potions out of your slots. You will earn far more hallowed teleport crystals than you can use, so save some marks or spend them on loot sacks.

        Done whenyou are 98 Agility, or stopped where you chose.

        Coins: about 37.3 million gp at the end: about 300,000 gp at the start of the block, plus about 38.2 million gp of Sepulchre loot, less about 1.2 million gp of sawmill fees for the teak planks (fletching and rune costs are not counted) · Slots: as before · Requirements: 87 Agility (boostable) for floor 5

        Wiki referencesHallowed SepulchreManaging MiscellaniaAuburnvale

        Checkpoint at the end of 2.2. Compare yourself with this before you start 2.3; the figures are the plan's, not guarantees.

        • Coins: about 37.3 million gp if you took the Sepulchre to 98 Agility; less if you stopped earlier. Before the next Sepulchre session, keep a margin of about 300,000 gp over the teak refill.
        • Skills the section aimed at: Agility 98 (84 if you stopped after floor 4), Fishing 99, Smithing 74, Ranged 62, Hunter 61 or more, Sailing 55 or more (salvaging).
        • Done: Lunar Diplomacy, The Slug Menace, What Lies Below, Contact!, Tai Bwo Wannai Trio, Legends' Quest, Cold War, One Small Favour, A Tail of Two Cats, Ratcatchers, Swan Song, Recipe for Disaster to the Barrows gloves, His Faithful Servants, A Kingdom Divided, Sins of the Father and Troubled Tortugans.
        • Not yet: the costume room upgrades (2.3).

        2.3: More quests, and one of them is Song of the Elves

        You start 2.3 with the Sepulchre money; with the Sepulchre taken to 98 you start with about 37.3 million gp, and your total depends on how far you took it. Money stops being a UIM constraint here. Storage is still one: the costume room you upgrade first in this section is where a UIM's quest sets, capes and clue items finally live, but it only takes costumes, armour and mage sets, capes, holiday items and clue rewards, so the elf quests' ingredients are carried, not stored.

        The costume room, the Kandarin hard diary and karambwans

        Gate in87 Magic for Catherby tablets; 70 Fletching and the Kandarin hard diary's list (the granite body task already done in 2.1, 75 Smithing via a dwarven stout); the Construction levels for each piece with tea and a crystal saw (a deathpile death destroys the saw, so ask Brimstail for a new seed first if yours is gone); 83 Farming for the spirit tree; 750,000 gp for the shield right half.
        Carry inone piece's materials at a time; about one slot for the shield right half afterwards.
        Exitthe costume room upgraded and every set moved into it; the Kandarin hard diary; the shield right half; about 35.05 million gp.
        Carries forwardthe shield right half (the Ardougne hard diary in Chapter 3).

        Catherby tablets and the Kandarin hard diary

        RequirementsThe adamant spear also needs Barbarian Smithing and 75 Smithing, so if you are on 74 a dwarven stout gives the missing level; lamp on Slayer until 58, then Herblore. The water orb needs 56 Magic, an unpowered orb, 30 water and 3 cosmic runes, and a dusty key or 70 Agility, while the shadow hound wants 53 Thieving and Desert Treasure I started.

        When convenient, make Catherby Teleport tablets for your herb runs at the Lunar lectern in the westernmost building on Lunar Isle (87 Magic, on the Lunar spellbook, with 3 law, 10 water and 3 astral runes and a soft clay for each tablet). If you reached 70 Fletching at the Sepulchre (or are close enough to boost), teleport to Seers' Village and work through the Kandarin hard diary. It is not one sweep: its 11 tasks are spread over Seers' Village (the agility lap, a yew longbow from scratch, the courthouse with Piety on, burning maple logs with a bow, Fancy Stone for your house from the Seers' estate agent, who charges 25,000 gp and needs 50 Construction; finish his dialogue or the task won't count), Otto's Grotto (a leaping sturgeon and an adamant spear), the Shadow dungeon (a shadow hound, with your ring of visibility), a water orb, a mithril dragon and buying and wearing a granite body from Barbarian Assault, which should have ticked when you did it in 2.1 (check the journal; if not, Commander Connad sells another for 95,000 gp), so read the wiki's list first. A few tasks have traps. The yew longbow must be chopped, cut and strung with no other task completing in between, or the wiki says the progress resets, and the granite body works the same way across its buying and wearing. The mithril dragon needs dragonfire protection, which the wiki recommends for the water orb too. Claim the rewards at the Wedge outside Camelot's entrance, which gives a 15,000 XP lamp for any skill from level 50.

        Done whenthe Kandarin hard diary is claimed.

        Upgrade the costume room

        Chapter 1 built the costume room, an oak armour case and an oak magic wardrobe, and 2.1 added the oak treasure chest, toy box and cape rack and the teak wardrobe and fancy dress box. Upgrade and add these, boosting where needed (each tier is built from the one before it, so build the teak cape rack and the carved teak wardrobe on the way):

        • a marble lectern (77 Construction; it replaces your mahogany eagle lectern);
        • a spirit tree (75 Construction and 83 Farming, built in a superior garden; the crystal saw doesn't count for it);
        • a teak armour case (64), upgrading the oak one;
        • a mahogany cape rack (72);
        • a mahogany magic wardrobe (78);
        • a teak treasure chest (66);
        • optionally one or two portal rooms (2 limestone bricks and 9 teak planks a room, plus 100 times each teleport's runes).

        The total is 1 marble block, 1 magic stone, 1 gold leaf, 1 spirit sapling, 15 teak planks and 8 mahogany planks, and you may need to convert a few more logs. This is the most important block for a UIM in Chapter 2. The armour case, cape rack, magic wardrobe, fancy dress box, toy box and treasure chest are costume room storage, which a UIM can use like a small bank for the item sets they accept. Two rules matter: each item can be stored only once, and you can't take a piece of a set back out until the whole set has been deposited. Move every quest armour set, cape and clue item you have been carrying or keeping in deathpiles into them now. They hold only costumes, armour and mage sets, capes, holiday items and clue rewards, so quest items, food and the elf quests' ingredients stay on you. The sizes decide what fits: the teak armour case holds 50 sets (the oak one 25), the teak fancy dress box four costume types, the mahogany magic wardrobe 35 mage sets and the teak treasure chest only beginner, easy and medium clue rewards, so carry harder clue rewards until you can build better. At the 73 Construction you finish the Sepulchre with, the marble lectern needs a 4-level boost and the magic wardrobe a 5-level one: the crystal saw gives +3 and a cup of tea from your kitchen shelves makes up the rest, but the saw doesn't work on the spirit tree, which needs 2 levels from tea alone.

        Done whenevery piece is built and your sets are stored.

        Karambwans, if you have a fish barrel

        If you happen to have a fish barrel (nothing earlier in this chapter earns one), you can move the house to Brimhaven (and back afterwards) and buy 2,000 to 10,000 raw karambwans with the barrel method, for food and for 1-tick karambwan Cooking XP. For a UIM, raw karambwans are good food stock, but the wiki doesn't say whether raw fish survives a deathpile, so keep them on you or in the barrel, which keeps its fish when it drops, but you can only carry a barrel and an inventory, so buy them per trip and cook them as you go. Without the barrel, skip this.

        Done whenthe karambwans are bought, or skipped without a barrel.

        The shield right half

        With the Sepulchre money behind you, also buy a shield right half for 750,000 gp from the Legends' Guild Shop of Useful Items (Legends' Quest is done; the Myths' Guild Armoury sells it too, and the wiki lists no other source). It is one slot until Chapter 3, where the Ardougne hard diary smiths it with a shield left half into a dragon square shield (60 Smithing).

        Done whenyou hold the shield right half.

        Coins: about 37.3 million gp on arrival, about 35.05 million gp after the shield right half (750,000 gp) and the costume room (about 1.5 million gp) · Slots: costumes and sets move into the costume room; one slot for the shield right half · Requirements: the Construction levels listed for each piece (the crystal saw and tea boost all but the spirit tree, which also needs 83 Farming); 70 Fletching, 60 Woodcutting, 70 Fishing, 70 Prayer and the other skills for the Kandarin hard diary; 87 Magic for Catherby tablets

        Wiki referencesCostume roomUltimate Ironman GuideKandarin DiaryFish barrelMarble lecternLunar lecternSpirit tree (Construction)Shield right half

        Mage Arena I and II, and the Wilderness diary tasks

        Gate in60 Magic for Mage Arena I and 75 for Mage Arena II (not boostable); 160,000 gp for two of the god staves (the Chamber guardian gives you the third free); air, fire and blood runes and a slash weapon; 52 Agility, 55 Mining with a pickaxe, and 50 Slayer for the Wilderness tasks.
        Carry inonly the fight's gear; everything else in a deathpile outside the Wilderness.
        Exitboth Mage Arena miniquests done with the god capes; the listed Wilderness tasks done; about 34.89 million gp.
        Carries forwardone god staff only if you plan the Wilderness hard diary.
        Deathbank checkthis block has fights where a death is unsafe. If Hespori or another retrieval service holds your items, collect them before you start: an unsafe death here deletes them. In the Wilderness, a death to a player also hands most of what you carry to the killer.

        Mage Arena I

        RequirementsMage Arena I needs 60 Magic and Mage Arena II 75 Magic, neither boostable.

        Do the Mage Arena I miniquest, then Mage Arena II. Kolodion waits in a cave room where other players can't attack you. The wiki gives two ways in: a lever in the Edgeville ruins (or in the small building by the West Ardougne gate) that drops you in the Deserted Keep, from where you run north, cut a web and walk west to the arena's northern entrance, or the broken-down house north-west of the arena, where you cut two webs and pull the lever on the wall. Bring a slash weapon for the webs and the runes for the fight. The arena itself is not safe, so take only what the fight needs. Kolodion has five forms, and every form after the first uses magic even at melee range, so Protect from Magic stops all of it; the last is a level 112 black demon. If a form gets the better of you, run out through the north lever and he picks up in the form you left. Mage Arena I ends with you choosing a god by praying at its statue; Zamorak is slightly more useful for God Wars Dungeon protection. After the kill, step into the Sparkling pool, walk past the staff merchant into the room with the three statues, pray at the one you chose, then go back south to the Chamber guardian. The Chamber guardian gives you the staff of the god you chose for free; buy the other two from him (80,000 gp each). You can pray again later for more capes.

        Done whenMage Arena I is complete and you hold three god staves (one free, two bought).

        Mage Arena II

        Mage Arena II needs 100 casts each of Saradomin Strike, Claws of Guthix and Flames of Zamorak inside the arena, so you need all three staves (or a staff of the dead, staff of light or staff of balance), air, fire and blood runes and a slash weapon for the webs. Kolodion sends you after three followers, and each can only be hurt by its own god's spell: Porazdir (level 235) by Flames of Zamorak, Justiciar Zachariah (level 348) by Saradomin Strike and Derwen (level 235) by Claws of Guthix. Their spawn points rotate about every 45 minutes of time spent in the Wilderness, and if a spot is taken, hop worlds or wait. Porazdir's energy ball hits through protection prayers, Zachariah's blue wave lands on your tile so step off it, and Derwen's orbs heal him until you destroy them. If you die, you have to kill that follower again.

        UIM riskYou find them with an enchanted symbol that works like hot and cold, shaking when a follower is near; each use can cost you up to 16 hitpoints, so bring food.
        Done whenMage Arena II is complete.

        Wilderness diary tasks on the way

        RequirementsThe muddy chest needs a knife or slash weapon, or 82 Agility via the stepping stones north of the maze, and the bloodveld safespot only works while you wear one Saradomin and one Zamorak item.

        Along the way or afterwards: mine a mithril ore at the Pirates' Hideout mine (55 Mining and a pickaxe), do a lap of the Wilderness Agility Course (52 Agility), kill chaos dwarves for a muddy key and open the muddy chest in the middle of the Lava Maze, kill an ankou at the Forgotten Cemetery, and kill a bloodveld in the Wilderness God Wars Dungeon (60 Agility or Strength to enter, 50 Slayer and god protection items; the south-west corner is a safespot). Keep one god staff: Chapter 3's Wilderness hard diary, at 68 Slayer, needs you to hit another player with a god spell. High-alch the other two afterwards. You can kill the Mage Arena II bosses again for extra capes. The rewards for these tasks are claimed in the Edgeville block later in this section.

        Go in with only the gear the fights need, and keep everything else in a deathpile outside the Wilderness (the costume room only takes sets and costumes, not your working kit).

        UIM riskAll of this is in the Wilderness, where a death gives your items to whoever is there.
        Done whenthe listed Wilderness tasks are done and the staves alched (one kept if you plan the hard diary).

        Coins: about 34.89 million gp after the two bought staves (160,000 gp) · Slots: what you take in · Requirements: 60 Magic (not boostable) for Mage Arena I; 75 Magic (not boostable), Mage Arena I and 100 casts of each god spell in the arena for Mage Arena II; 52 Agility for the Wilderness Agility Course, 55 Mining and a pickaxe for the mithril ore, 50 Slayer for the bloodveld

        Wiki referencesMage Arena IMage Arena IIWilderness Diary

        The General's Shadow, Dream Mentor, chompies and Sailing to 67

        Gate inCurse of the Empty Lord, Fight Arena and a started Desert Treasure I; Lunar Diplomacy, Eadgar's Ruse and 85 combat for Dream Mentor; 70 Mining and 66 Herblore for the diary; 30 Ranged and Fletching for ogre bows; the hemp; 55 Sailing (not boostable) for the Jubbly Jive.
        Carry inthe ghostly outfit and 40 coins until The General's Shadow ends; the Dream Mentor food in sacks; one ship build's materials at a time (up to 28/28).
        ExitDream Mentor done; the Fremennik hard diary claimed; the Western Provinces easy and medium diaries; the herb sack; the skiff's mahogany mast, canvas sails and mithril helm; wind catchers on both ships; 67 Sailing.
        Carries forwardthe ghostly outfit and severed-leg path (The General's Shadow), the 20 bolts of canvas left over.
        Deathbank checkthis block has fights where a death is unsafe. If Hespori or another retrieval service holds your items, collect them before you start: an unsafe death here deletes them.

        The General's Shadow and Dream Mentor

        The General's Shadow needs an outfit you wear to speak to General Khazard and to every scout: a ghostspeak amulet (the Morytania legs don't work for this quest), your ring of visibility and the full ghostly robes from Curse of the Empty Lord. You can't pray in the Bouncer fight at the end, and the Sin Seer wants 40 coins, so carry the outfit and the coins until the quest is done. Teleport to Rellekka and start The General's Shadow with Khazard's ghost, who wanders the woods south-east of Rellekka between the house portal and the swaying tree (he isn't on the minimap and has a wide range, so search the whole path). Teleport to Seers' Village and talk to the Sin Seer (she is upstairs in the house with the water source), then go back to the general in Rellekka. He then sends you to four scouts, who can be met in any order while you wear the full outfit: the guide meets the Falador one during the Fremennik step below, the Stronghold and Tai Bwo Wannai ones in the scouts block, and the desert one after Fairytale II.

        Done whenthe Sin Seer is visited and the general has his report.

        Dream Mentor

        Before Dream Mentor, take the spirit tree to the Grand Tree and buy 7 potatoes, 7 cabbages and 6 onions at Grand Tree Groceries (it stocks ten of each for a few coins): the quest needs 20 pieces of food of three kinds, 6 of one and 7 of each of the others. Sacks of cabbages, potatoes and onions work, so buy them in three sacks (3 slots instead of 20), but take each piece out before you use it, or the sack counts as one. A death also loses the Seal of Passage, and Brundt the Chieftain gives a replacement. You face four bosses in a row without prayers: the Inadequacy (level 343), the Everlasting (223), the Untouchable (274) and the Illusive (108). All four have low Magic defence, so magic is the easiest way through. Ignore the Doubts the Inadequacy summons. The Everlasting can be safespotted directly west of the lectern on the north side, and the Untouchable with magic or a halberd, but don't get pinned against the arena wall. The Illusive burrows and reappears after two of your hits, so chase it. Bring the Seal of Passage from Lunar Diplomacy, goutweed, an astral rune, a tinderbox, a hammer, and a pestle and mortar as well (the Oneiromancer gives you the dream vial to fill with water); you need 85 combat. Go to Lunar Isle and do Dream Mentor (dreamy lamp on Ranged).

        UIM riskThe Dream World here is dangerous, unlike Lunar Diplomacy's: a death counts as a normal death, no teleport works there (not even the ring of life) and the lectern is the only exit.
        Rewards2 quest points, 15,000 Hitpoints and 10,000 Magic XP, 7 Lunar spells and a dreamy lamp (15,000 XP in a combat skill other than Attack or Prayer).
        Done whenDream Mentor is complete.

        The Fremennik hard diary

        Swap to the Lunar spellbook, teleport to Waterbirth, visit Jatizso and mine five adamantite ores for two diary steps (70 Mining, with The Fremennik Isles started). Back in Rellekka, make a super defence potion (66 Herblore, a cadantine potion (unf) and white berries, which you can grow in a bush patch at 59 Farming). Before you claim the Fremennik hard diary from Thorodin south of Rellekka (lamp on Herblore), make sure its other tasks are done: cast Trollheim Teleport (61 Magic, standard spellbook) and steal from the Keldagrim gem stall (75 Thieving); the kyatt, the Neitiznot shield, the Blast Furnace foreman and the Miscellania support came earlier. Swap to the Arceuus spellbook, teleport to Draynor Manor and run west to the Falador scout for The General's Shadow. Put the amulet, ring and robes on before you speak to the scout, since the ghost outfit has to stay on for all four.

        Done whenthe Fremennik hard diary is claimed.

        Chompies

        Make a composite ogre bow (achey tree logs, wolf bones, a bow string and a knife; 30 Fletching) and 150 mithril brutal arrows, take ogre bellows from the chest in Rantz's cave if you no longer have them, kill 125 chompies (the Western Provinces medium hat; the easy one needs 30), and talk to Rantz for chompy bird hats. Collect the Western Provinces easy and medium diaries when convenient, lamps on Herblore. Besides the tasks you passed on the way, they still need a swamp toad from the pen north-west of the Grand Tree, Brimstail's teleport to the Essence Mine, iron ore at the Piscatoris mine and gold ore under the Grand Tree (40 Mining).

        If you haven't bought the herb sack yet (the Kourend block in 2.2 buys it at 58 Herblore), buy it now: your Herblore is past 58 (the super defence potion above needed 66), so teleport to your house in Hosidius and talk to Farmer Gricoller at the Tithe Farm next door with the 250 points Chapter 1 kept in reserve. The sack holds only grimy, unnoted herbs of the normal kinds (30 of each), so from here on put your farm-run herbs straight into it and take them out to make potions.

        Done whenyou have 125 chompy kills and the Western Provinces easy and medium diaries.

        Sailing to 67

        Do this in trips: a bolt of canvas is made from 2 hemp yarn at a loom (Auburnvale has one), it can be noted but it doesn't stack, and the ship parts below also need steel and lead bars that take a slot each. Make 20 bolts of canvas from your hemp, keep them noted and unnote only the few you are about to use (a bank booth unnotes as many as fit, so keep only that many slots free, or pay Phials in Rimmington 5 gp a bolt for an exact number). A skiff's mahogany mast and canvas sails (52 Sailing, 45 Construction) take 5 bolts, 40 mithril nails and 10 mahogany logs; the sloop's take 10 bolts, 60 nails and 15 logs. Build the mahogany mast, canvas sails and mithril helm on your skiff, and the inoculation station on your sloop (40 Sailing, 37 Construction: 8 teak planks, 32 steel nails and 6 relicym's balm(4), the potions you left at Zogre Flesh Eaters).

        Done when20 bolts of canvas are made and the skiff and sloop upgrades are built.

        The Jubbly Jive

        Do the Jubbly Jive barracuda trial (55 Sailing, not boostable) up to shark rank. Gurtob runs it from Backwater. It takes the skiff, which needs at least a mithril helm and an inoculation station (the one you built at Port Khazard), and no crew aboard; Ros can dismiss them. The first clear of each rank unlocks the next. The trial gives 1,700, 3,000 and 6,200 Sailing XP at swordfish, shark and marlin, and beating the time limits of 2:03, 3:00 and 5:21 adds a one-time 5,000, 7,500 and 10,000 XP. Beating the shark time of 3:00 also gives the captured wind mote that the wind catchers below need, so aim for it on that rank.

        Done whenthe barracuda trial is done up to shark rank.

        Wind catchers and Sailing to 67

        Buy the 20,000 air runes the two wind catchers need now (10,000 each): they stack in one slot, but Aubury's shop holds only 5,000 at 4 gp each, so buy them over several shops or worlds, about 100,000 gp. Build a wind catcher on both ships (53 Sailing, 47 Construction; for each ship 4 teak planks, 16 steel nails, 8 steel bars, 4 lead bars, 10,000 air runes and a captured wind mote), then return to the Jubbly Jive and train to 67 Sailing on marlin rank with the wiki's method (the trial takes skiffs only and its 55 Sailing can't be boosted; clearing the shark rank for the first time unlocks marlin). Return to the Ardent Ocean meteorologists for their charting tasks when convenient. The alternatives (Rellekka courier tasks, salvaging) also work.

        At 67 Sailing you can dock at Deepfin Point, the best ironman source of blue dragon scales (Durrik's Goods, the general store in the mine, stocks five at 125 gp each and restocks every two minutes, according to the wiki).

        Done whenboth wind catchers are built and you are 67 Sailing.

        Coins: about 34.79 million gp after the 20,000 air runes (about 100,000 gp) · Slots: up to 28/28 on a ship-building trip (bolts of canvas, bars and the outfit), so split it into trips; about 20/28 otherwise · Requirements: Curse of the Empty Lord, Fight Arena and a started Desert Treasure I for The General's Shadow; Lunar Diplomacy, Eadgar's Ruse and 85 combat for Dream Mentor; Big Chompy Bird Hunting, 30 Ranged and 30 Fletching for ogre bows; 70 Mining for the Jatizso ore, 66 Herblore for the super defence; the Sailing levels listed for each upgrade; 67 Sailing for Deepfin Point

        Wiki referencesThe General's ShadowDream MentorChompy bird huntingBarracuda TrialsDeepfin Point

        The General's Shadow scouts, Fairytale II, Land of the Goblins and Rag and Bone Man II

        Gate in40 Thieving, 49 Farming and 57 Herblore for Fairytale II; 38 Agility, 40 Fishing, 45 Thieving and 48 Herblore (none boostable) for Land of the Goblins; 32 and 40 Slayer, a fishing explosive and a mirror shield for the last bones.
        Carry inthe ghostly outfit and the Land of the Goblins list: about 22/28.
        ExitThe General's Shadow, Fairytale II, Land of the Goblins and Rag and Bone Man II done; about six goblin mail.
        Carries forwardthe goblin mail, flippers if they dropped.
        Deathbank checkthis block has fights where a death is unsafe. If Hespori or another retrieval service holds your items, collect them before you start: an unsafe death here deletes them.

        The Stronghold and Tai Bwo Wannai scouts

        Teleport to Varrock and take the spirit tree to the Grand Tree. Run south and meet the scout outside the gate for The General's Shadow; the Gnomish scout stands at the entrance of the Tree Gnome Stronghold. Get to Brimhaven (use a Brimhaven house tablet; the Ardougne boat works too) and run to the scout just east of Tai Bwo Wannai, south of the teak and mahogany trees (fairy ring CKR also gets you there). Keep the ghostly outfit on for each one.

        Done whenthe Falador, Stronghold and Tai Bwo Wannai scouts are met; the desert scout follows Fairytale II (the Falador scout stands between Falador's east wall and the Draynor Manor west fence).

        Fairytale II and the desert scout

        Take the fairy ring to Zanaris and complete Fairytale II – Cure a Queen (40 Thieving, which can't be boosted, 49 Farming and 57 Herblore; Martin the Master Gardener in Draynor Village starts it if you haven't; XP lamp on Slayer). Talk to Martin again after about five and a half minutes of logged-in time, and don't pickpocket him, which locks you out for ten minutes. In Zanaris, take and study Nuff's certificate and read the Rune Temple sign at the Cosmic Altar ruins; landing among penguins means you lack the certificate. The ring sequence to the Queen's hideout is AIR, DLR, DJQ, AJS, and if the ring's default click is the last destination, right-click it and choose Configure. Pickpocket the Fairy Godfather for the secateurs from the side or behind with neither henchman facing you, since a failure throws you out of Zanaris. The star flower on the cosmic plane (ring CKP) takes about two minutes to grow, and the Goraks on the gorak plane (ring DIR) are very accurate and can drain stats; protection prayers don't work on them but Piety does, and they come one at a time. Give the secateurs to Fairy Nuff before you crush the claw or make the essence, or you will have to collect the ingredients again. Take fairy ring BIQ to the desert scout, just south of Shantay Pass. Teleport to Rellekka and give General Khazard the message; he gives you a severed leg and sends you to the cave near where the fish-men gather.

        Done whenFairytale II is complete and the general has sent you to the cave.

        Land of the Goblins and the Bouncer fight

        RequirementsLand of the Goblins: 38 Agility, 40 Fishing, 45 Thieving and 48 Herblore, none of them boostable.

        Teleport to Lumbridge, go into the Dorgeshuun mines and speak to Grubfoot to start Land of the Goblins. The wiki also says a UIM must unequip everything for this quest on top of carrying the items, so take the ghostly outfit off until you reach the Bouncer fight. Bring a light source, a toadflax potion (unf), yellow, blue, orange and purple dye (black dye is made from mushrooms during the quest, so carry a vial and a pestle and mortar), a raw slimy eel, a plain fishing rod (the oily one doesn't work) and 5 coins. The six keys for the crypt door vanish once used. Then finish The General's Shadow (the Bouncer fight is in the goblin cave south-east of the Fishing Guild: go north, take the first passage east to a larger cavern and enter the crack in its south wall. Keep the ghostly outfit on and don't pray. The ghost of Bouncer is level 160 and hits up to 15, can be safespotted in the south-east corner, and at 10 Hitpoints you throw the severed leg automatically. Don't drop anything there: the wiki says items dropped in the fight area are lost for good when he dies. The rewards are the shadow sword, which the ghost that appears afterwards returns if you lose it, and 2,000 Slayer XP), then complete Land of the Goblins. The travel is awkward, and a crate directly east inside the temple, past the guards, has unlimited goblin mail; take about six, and if you lose one, the crates by the temple exit have more.

        UIM riskIn the temple, wearing anything but goblin mail, fighting or standing in direct sunlight turns you back into a human, and wearing your dyed goblin mail with a full inventory destroys it, so don't.
        Done whenThe General's Shadow and Land of the Goblins are complete.

        Finish Rag and Bone Man II

        Finish Rag and Bone Man II with the last kills. Each listed monster has a 1 in 4 chance to drop its bone, so expect a few kills each, and the wiki lets you hand bones in in batches, so you need not hold them all at once:

        • ogres (Feldip Hills, fairy ring AKS), which drop ogre ribs rather than a bone;
        • mogres (fairy ring AIQ, the only spot), with Skippy and the Mogres done in full, 32 Slayer and a fishing explosive (sea mogres don't drop the bone); stay for flippers for seaweed runs;
        • basilisks (fairy ring AJR, the only spot), with 40 Slayer and a mirror shield (20 Defence); their attacks can lower your stats, so a restore potion helps;
        • trolls, if you didn't get one while questing.

        Then complete the quest.

        Done whenRag and Bone Man II is complete.

        Coins: about 34.79 million gp · Slots: about 22/28 with the outfit · Requirements: Curse of the Empty Lord, Fight Arena and a started Desert Treasure I for The General's Shadow; 40 Thieving, 49 Farming and 57 Herblore for Fairytale II; Another Slice of H.A.M. and Death to the Dorgeshuun, 38 Agility, 40 Fishing, 45 Thieving and 48 Herblore (none boostable) for Land of the Goblins; 40 Slayer (boostable) for Rag and Bone Man II

        Wiki referencesFairytale II – Cure a QueenLand of the GoblinsRag and Bone Man IISkippy and the Mogres

        The Wilderness easy and medium diaries, Grim Tales, and the Mos Le'Harmless quests

        Gate inthe Wilderness easy and medium tasks' levels; Witch's House and Grim Tales' boostable levels; 42 Slayer (not boostable) for Rum Deal; 42 Agility, 45 Crafting, 50 Smithing and 40 Ranged for Cabin Fever; 40 Cooking for Trouble Brewing; 16 Crafting, 30 Construction and 50 Prayer for The Great Brain Robbery; ten furs from Baraek.
        Carry inonly each Wilderness task's kit; 11 free slots for Cabin Fever; an empty inventory for Trouble Brewing (a Falador deathpile); about 26/28 for The Great Brain Robbery.
        Exitthe Wilderness easy and medium, Falador medium and Morytania medium diaries; Grim Tales, Rum Deal, Cabin Fever and The Great Brain Robbery done; a Trouble Brewing game played; about 34.80 million gp.
        Carries forwardthe holy wrench; watermelons growing on Harmony Island (Morytania hard).
        Deathbank checkthis block has fights where a death is unsafe. If Hespori or another retrieval service holds your items, collect them before you start: an unsafe death here deletes them. In the Wilderness, a death to a player also hands most of what you carry to the killer.

        The Wilderness easy and medium diaries

        Go to the Edgeville Dungeon and into the Wilderness. Collect five red spiders' eggs (you can drop and pick up the same one five times), cross the handrail and kill an earth warrior. The easy tier also wants iron ore mined in the Wilderness (15 Mining) and a team cape equipped there (put it on inside; entering with it on doesn't count), and the medium tier an Earth Orb charged (60 Magic, an unpowered orb, 30 earth runes and 3 cosmic runes). Have the Mage of Zamorak teleport you to the Abyss if you haven't (an easy task; the one-time Abyss teleport during Temple of the Eye doesn't count), and if you have lost the small essence pouch from Enter the Abyss, the Dark Mage there replaces it for free; Chapter 3's colossal pouch starts from it, and the medium, large and giant pouches come later as Guardians of the Rift loot. Then teleport out. Back in Edgeville, talk to the Emblem Trader north of the bank (a medium task), then collect the Wilderness easy and medium rewards from the Lesser Fanatic east of the jailhouse, near the bridge and outside the Wilderness (lamps on Herblore); claim the easy tier first, because each tier waits for the one before. Take only what those tasks need into the Wilderness.

        Done whenboth Wilderness diaries are claimed.

        Grim Tales

        RequirementsGrim Tales needs Witch's House and 45 Farming, 52 Herblore, 58 Thieving, 59 Agility and 71 Woodcutting, all boostable.

        Do Grim Tales, using the tool leprechaun to take out your watering can and seed dibber mid-quest. Sylas in Taverley, just south of the house portal, starts it. The items are two vials of water, two tarromins, leather gloves, a rune axe, food, the Iban's staff with death and fire runes, water runes, an air staff, law runes, a ring of dueling and potions. Wear the leather gloves at the electrified gate, which otherwise hits for 17. In the tower the princess blocks the door, so go round the back and climb the crumbling east wall (58 Thieving). Grimgnash takes a fifth of your current Hitpoints, rounded up, for each wrong answer in his story, so read the options. Keep your second shrinking potion, which you need after the fight to shrink the beanstalk before chopping it down, and leave any follower behind, since it can't enter Glod's instance. Glod is a cloud giant who grows from level 118 to 138 shortly after the fight starts, and his melee hits up to 24: use Protect from Melee and cast or range from behind a wall, but expect his quake to drain your Prayer and switch protection prayers off. Afterwards, wear the dwarven helmet in the Dwarven Mines under Falador (a Falador hard task, so it is banked for later), then alch it. Collect the Falador medium diary (lamp on Herblore).

        Rewards1 quest point and 60,000 Woodcutting, 25,000 Agility, 25,000 Thieving, 15,000 Herblore, 10,000 Farming and 5,000 Hitpoints XP, plus the dwarven helmet.
        Done whenGrim Tales is complete and the Falador medium diary is claimed.

        Rum Deal

        RequirementsRum Deal: 42 Slayer (not boostable) to start, plus 42 Crafting, 40 Farming, 47 Prayer and 50 Fishing (boostable).

        Do Rum Deal (leave with the Ectophial), killing a fever spider for the diary. Pirate Pete on the dock north of Port Phasmatys starts it. If you lose the blindweed, Captain Braindeath hands over what he stored earlier. The pressure lever only works once all five sea creatures are in the barrel. Davey only blesses the wrench if you have at least 47 Prayer points available, and the blessing doesn't drain them. The level 150 evil spirit's attacks are negated by Protect from Melee, or safespot it with Ranged, Magic or a halberd. Fever spiders give disease on hit even through Protect from Melee, so wear Slayer gloves or use a safespot. Finish Braindeath's dialogue before you use the carcass, or the spider won't drop it.

        Rewards2 quest points, 7,000 XP each in Fishing, Prayer and Farming, and the holy wrench, which adds to prayer potions while it is in your inventory.
        Done whenRum Deal is complete.

        Cabin Fever

        Do Cabin Fever, which needs 11 free slots and 42 Agility, 45 Crafting, 50 Smithing and 40 Ranged (none boostable), and talk to Bill Teach after it for 10,000 gp. Bill is in The Green Ghost inn in Port Phasmatys, and you board the easternmost ship, The Adventurous. The slots are for what you collect aboard, and on arrival at Mos Le'Harmless everything from the ships is taken back except a hammer, tinderbox, rope and swamp paste. On the enemy ship level 57 pirates attack, a missed rope jump empties your run energy, and leaving that ship loses the plunder you carry; only 10 plunder can be stored. Clean and ram the cannon before every load, because skipping a step blows it up for about 6 damage and breaks it, and clicking Empty-Out Cannon does not actually clean it. Don't return to the ships after the quest, since that can reset them and stop Bill talking to you.

        Done whenCabin Fever is complete and Bill Teach has paid you.

        Trouble Brewing with a planned death

        Requirementsan empty inventory, no follower and an empty head slot, and a UIM can't use the chest by Honest Jimmy, so make it a planned death: take everything off in the Falador Castle courtyard and die to a White Knight, leave everything in the pile and use the Trouble Brewing minigame teleport, which needs 40 Cooking and Cabin Fever (just done) and no coins, so there is no charter fare and no change to drop.

        Then play one game of Trouble Brewing for the Morytania medium diary (40 Cooking). Be on the standard spellbook for this errand, so that afterwards the free Home Teleport puts you in Lumbridge, a short walk from Falador: you swapped to Lunar for the Waterbirth step, so before the planned death pray at the Astral Altar on Lunar Isle, or talk to Tyss at the Dark Altar (fairy ring CIS), to switch back. The game needs at least one opposing player, so play on a busy world. Play one game with the watering-buckets-only strategy, which gives enough points to buy "the stuff" for future brewing, then Home Teleport to Lumbridge, walk to Falador and collect your pile within the hour. The diary only needs you to finish a game, not win it. A game lasts 20 minutes with a 3-minute gap before the next, and your pile's 60 minutes only run while you are logged in, so join a game as it starts (joining at the 10-minute mark also halves your pieces of eight).

        Done whenone game is played and the pile is collected.

        The Great Brain Robbery

        Do The Great Brain Robbery (lamp on Herblore; 16 Crafting, 30 Construction and 50 Prayer, none boostable, plus Recipe for Disaster's Pirate Pete and Another Cook's Quest): this is where the ten furs from Baraek go (bear or grey wolf fur; furs from Hunter creatures don't work), so buy them in Varrock just before, along with 18 planks. Brother Tranquility in Mos Le'Harmless starts it. Bring a holy symbol (Animal Magnetism used up the one from 2.1's Family Crest block, so cast a new one from a silver bar with a holy mould and string it with a ball of wool), a normal hammer (not the Imcando one), about 100 nails (the quest uses 38, bronze or iron nails can bend, and running out while building the crate means starting that part again) and underwater gear (the Medallion of the Deep works), and be ready for the Barrelchest, who disables prayer. Leave any follower behind, and don't blow the wolf whistle while wearing the fishbowl helmet. Wear your ring of charos (a) when you talk to Rufus in Canifis, and if you lose it, pickpocket Fenkenstrain for another. Once Rufus has shown you the cutscene, make the ten wooden cats at a crafting table in your house (16 Crafting, one plank and one fur each). The Barrelchest (level 190) is slow and inaccurate, but its hits often pass 20; stand at the corner of the first pew or at the entrance to safespot it, not behind a pew, or his anchor hits for up to 25. Plant watermelon seeds in the Harmony Island allotment on the way: harvesting one there is a Morytania hard task (the easy diary's scarecrow was placed in 2.2), and the quest also unlocks the Harmony Island teleport on the Arceuus spellbook to bring you back. Complete the Morytania medium diary, lamp on Herblore.

        Done whenThe Great Brain Robbery is complete and the Morytania medium diary is claimed.

        Coins: about 34.79 million gp, plus 10,000 gp from Bill Teach (about 34.80 million gp afterwards) · Slots: empty for Trouble Brewing, 11 free for Cabin Fever, about 26/28 for The Great Brain Robbery · Requirements: Witch's House, 45 Farming, 52 Herblore, 58 Thieving, 59 Agility and 71 Woodcutting (boostable) for Grim Tales; Zogre Flesh Eaters, Big Chompy Bird Hunting, 42 Slayer and Rum Deal's quests for Rum Deal; Rum Deal and the skills above for Cabin Fever; Cabin Fever and 40 Cooking for Trouble Brewing; Creature of Fenkenstrain, Cabin Fever, Rum Deal, Recipe for Disaster's Pirate Pete and Another Cook's Quest for The Great Brain Robbery

        Wiki referencesWilderness DiaryGrim TalesRum DealCabin FeverTrouble BrewingThe Great Brain RobberyMorytania DiaryEssence pouch

        The elf quests and Song of the Elves

        Gate in70 in Agility, Construction, Farming, Herblore, Hunter, Mining, Smithing and Woodcutting (none boostable); a runite bar; 60 Ranged and 50 Thieving for Mourning's End Part I; Thorgel's 47 items in two trips.
        Carry ineach part's list only, the rest in a Falador deathpile; for Seren, phoenix necklaces, Guthix rests and the fight's setup.
        ExitSong of the Elves done and Prifddinas open; 68 Crafting or more (Chapter 3 takes it to 70); about 34.80 million gp.
        Carries forwardthe death talisman.
        Deathbank checkthis block has fights where a death is unsafe. If Hespori or another retrieval service holds your items, collect them before you start: an unsafe death here deletes them.

        Herblore and Farming before the elf quests

        Check both before you start Regicide. Hover over Herblore in the skills tab: Song of the Elves needs 737,627 XP (70), and nothing boosts it. Your lamps and quests give about 64, so the rest, about 311,000 XP, should already have come from the herb runs. If you are short, count the herbs you hold (the sack, the leprechaun's noted stacks and your inventory) and make them into potions now, best XP first (cadantine, snapdragon, kwuarm, avantoe, irit, ranarr); if that is still not enough, keep harvesting and making potions before you begin Regicide, and do not start Mourning's End Part II below 70. Master Farmers give seeds too, but only about 10,000 XP of potions an hour, so put the effort into the herb runs first.

        Farming is the other one that has to be right. If you are below 70, go back to the Tithe Farm for the few hours it takes; if you want the 94 the end table lists, keep going. You will be about 90 from the runs by now (an estimate), and the last four levels are about 2.6 million XP, about 25 hours of logavano at 22 minutes a sack. Nothing in Chapter 3 needs more than 85, except the Falador elite diary's 91, so treat the top-up as optional; the end table below assumes you did it.

        Done whenHerblore is 70 and Farming is 70, or 94 if you took the top-up.

        Coins: about 3,000 to 6,000 gp for eyes of newt and vials · Slots: about 18/28 during the potion rounds, 20/28 at the farm · Requirements: the potion levels above; 34 Farming for the farm

        Levels, Crafting and the runite bar

        Check your levels first: Song of the Elves needs 70 in Agility, Construction, Farming, Herblore, Hunter, Mining, Smithing and Woodcutting, and none of them can be boosted. Crafting is not on that list. Chapter 3 needs 70, and its Glass block takes you from 68 to 70, so you can stop at 68 here. If you'd rather reach 70 now, use Superglass Make, the wiki's UIM Crafting method from 77 Magic (with Lunar Diplomacy done): buy 10 buckets of sand and 10 soda ash from the charter-ship Trader Crewmembers each cycle, cast it and blow the glass straight away, dropping the products. Plain glassblowing at Port Phasmatys, as in 2.1, still works if you'd rather not use runes. Then make sure you have a runite bar, which the quest needs to repair Lady Trahaearn's exoskeleton. There are three options, and the third is recommended: hunt magpie implings in Puro-Puro (65 Hunter, a 1/21 drop), kill Vyrewatch Sentinels in Darkmeyer (38 Slayer, 2/93, which is 1/46.5), or trade rune platelegs (64,000 gp at Scavvo's Rune Store in the Champions Guild) for one at the Aldarin windmill nest, a trade that works.

        Done whenyour 70s are in place and you hold a runite bar.

        The elf quest line

        Then do the whole elf quest line: Regicide, Roving Elves (pick the crystal bow, as the wiki advises, but drop it once the elf line is done: Chapter 3 buys a fresh one from Islwyn when the Western Provinces hard diary needs it, which saves carrying a slot through the rest of the guide), Mourning's End Part I (60 Ranged and 50 Thieving, neither boostable), Mourning's End Part II and Song of the Elves. The line is linear; follow the wiki. Keep the full Mourner outfit from Mourning's End Part I, gas mask included, for Song of the Elves. The wiki's item list for Song of the Elves adds a steel full helm, platebody and platelegs, red and purple dye, silk, the runite bar, 8 limestone bricks, a tinderbox, a rope, a nature rune, seed flowers or an irit leaf, an adamant chainbody (used up; buy one, since the guide has bought none), a wine of Zamorak or Zamorak brew(4), a cabbage, a black knife or dagger, a cadantine seed, a seed dibber, a vial of water, a pestle and mortar, a hammer, a saw, a pickaxe, an axe and a spade; gather them a part at a time with the Falador deathpile, as below. In Roving Elves, Islwyn and Eluned move between two spots, so change worlds if they aren't there, and talk to Eluned before you enter Glarial's Tomb or you can't get the seed. The tomb bars weapons, armour, runes and ammunition, more strictly than Entrana, and you kill its level 84 Moss Guardian unarmed and unarmoured. The bow comes with 500 charges that wear off as you fire it, and Chapter 3 buys a crystal bow from Islwyn for the diary. In Mourning's End Part I, the first Mourner you hit drains your combat stats to 20 until you restore them (the Ferox Enclave pool does), and the wiki wants seven empty slots for the Mourner outfit when you collect it.

        Done whenyou know the order and the crystal bow is chosen.

        Regicide

        For Regicide the wiki lists 10 Crafting (not boostable) and 56 Agility (boostable, not needed to start), 6 to 9 coal with at least 25 recommended (the wiki suggests noted coal, which a UIM can't have, so carry them in the coal bag), a bow and some arrows, 2 ropes (3 in case you fail) and a strip of cloth made on a loom from 4 balls of wool, not a bolt of cloth. Talk to King Lathas before you enter the Underground Pass, or the dense forest stops you. The level 110 Tyras guard can be safespotted, and he despawns if you are slow, so be ready before you approach. Add coal to the distiller gradually, since clicking quickly overheats it, and take at least three barrels from Iorwerth Camp in case you spoil one.

        UIM riskDon't drop the Big Book of Bangs, which you need again in Rimmington.
        RewardsRegicide's rewards are 3 quest points, 13,750 Agility XP and 15,000 coins.
        Done whenRegicide is complete.

        Mourning's End Part II and Thorgel's list

        For Mourning's End Part II a UIM has no stockpile to draw on: Thorgel in the Death Altar trades a death talisman for a list of 47 items, brought over more than one trip.
