The list is babydragon bones, ball of wool, bronze bar, bronze med helm, bucket of milk, cake tin, cheese, chisel, cooked meat, egg, facemask, fishing rod, flax, gold ring, hammer, iron axe, iron nails, iron pickaxe, jug of wine, kebab, knife, leather boots, leather gloves, lobster pot, lockpick, necklace mould, needle, oak logs, pie dish, plank, pot of flour, potato cactus, 10 potatoes, pure essence, redberries, rope, rotten tomato, shears, skull, spade, swamp paste, thread, tinderbox, unicorn horn dust, vial of water, white apron and white berries, plus one random ticket (archery, agility arena or Castle Wars), one random book (crumbling tome, slashed or battered book, or Prifddinas' history, which a house bookcase can hold) and one random key (jail key, dusty key or door key). The potato cactus, skull and redberries come up earlier in the chapter for this quest, so check you still have them; buy the rest (the kebab at Karim's in Al Kharid, the cake tin at the Culinaromancer's Chest, and so on) and take Thorgel the list in two trips of up to 28 slots. To reach the Death Altar at all you need a death or catalytic talisman or tiara, or 50 junk items; a spare hammer and chisel help. The way there runs through the Temple of Light, and if you teleport out you must first turn the mirror toward the cyan door or you lock yourself out; the way back is then the Underground Pass, through a tunnel in the dwarf camp directly south of the main building that isn't on the minimap. Turn the light back toward the entrance door before you leave on each trip. The tunnel to the temple has level 182 dark beasts, and the level 73 shadows inside the temple are aggressive, so bring Protect from Melee, which needs 43 Prayer. On the last trip bring the newly made crystal and a teleport crystal.

        Done whenThorgel has his 47 items and the death talisman is yours.

        Carrying the quest items

        For a UIM this line is a carrying problem, not a storage one, because the costume room can't hold any of it. Take the list a part at a time and keep what you aren't using in a Falador Castle deathpile (cooked food and potions vanish from a pile, so buy those fresh). The pile's 60 minutes only run while you are logged in, so plan each part to fit inside them.

        UIM riskHespori's storage (Arno, 25,000 gp) is lost on any unsafe death, and this line has several fights, so don't use it here.
        Done wheneverything not in use waits in a Falador deathpile.

        The Fragment of Seren

        For the Fragment of Seren in Song of the Elves:

        • farm belladonna in the Draynor patch for at least 8 nightshades, though the wiki's phoenix method suggests 20 or more (or use explosive liquid from the Digsite); you put a necklace on when she teleports you and eat a nightshade to trigger it before the big hit lands;
        • buy blood and death runes for Ancient Magicks and make 6 to 10 phoenix necklaces (the wiki suggests 15 to 20 for its phoenix method, so make more if you struggle);
        • the rock cake is reported to work in the fight, though the wiki says otherwise, so test it before you rely on it; it is negated when a ranged projectile is incoming, so it can keep your Hitpoints steady but can't trigger the necklaces during special attacks;
        • if you struggle, make several Guthix rests, which don't delay your next attack and make it easy to manage your Hitpoints for the necklaces.

        The Seren fight is a long fight with a full inventory. Treat it as a no-death attempt, with only the items it needs on you. She is immune to recoil, and the last special of each phase can't be dodged, so have a heal or the necklace ready for it. Attack the lighter-coloured clone, since the others vanish when you hit the true one, and step away from her after each teleport because her melee only lands beside her.

        UIM riskThe wiki says a death in the original quest fight is unsafe, unlike a later replay, and that a Rebel Scout in the Underground Pass returns your gear for 80,000 gp if you haven't died anywhere else since; it doesn't say how that works for a UIM deathpile, so don't plan on it.
        Done whenSong of the Elves is complete.

        Coins: about 34.80 million gp · Slots: the fight's setup; Thorgel's list takes two trips · Requirements: 70 Agility, Construction, Farming, Herblore, Hunter, Mining, Smithing and Woodcutting (none boostable) and Mourning's End Part II for Song of the Elves; 60 Ranged and 50 Thieving (not boostable) for Mourning's End Part I; 10 Crafting and 56 Agility (boostable) for Regicide

        End of Chapter 2

        You finish Chapter 2 with Song of the Elves done, about 34.8 million gp (34,800,000) if you took the Sepulchre to 98, a costume room holding your costumes and armour and mage sets, and the skiff and sloop as working storage. End-of-chapter stats:

        SkillLevelNotes
        Attack / Strength / Defence62 / 77 / 70
        Hitpoints / Ranged / Prayer64 / 62 / 70
        Magic93Mostly from Sepulchre alchs
        Runecraft / Construction40 / 73
        Agility9884 if you stopped the Sepulchre after floor 4
        Herblore / Thieving70 / 86Herblore 70 comes mostly from potions made on the herb runs (lamps and quests give about 64); Thieving a few levels higher if you blackjacked more
        Crafting / Fletching68 / 69Chapter 3 needs 70 Crafting; Song of the Elves doesn't
        Slayer / Hunter59 / 73
        Mining / Smithing73 / 74
        Fishing / Cooking / Firemaking99 / 81 / 89
        Woodcutting / Farming / Sailing73 / 94 / 67Farming 94 needs the Tithe Farm to 85, the daily runs and the closing top-up (about 25 hours); the runs alone end near 90
        Coins at the end ofAmount
        The Sepulchre to 98 AgilityAbout 37.3 million gp: 300,000 gp + 38.2 million gp of loot - 1.2 million gp of teak plank fees
        The 2.3 purchasesAbout 34.8 million gp (34,800,000), with the gardener fees (about 0.4 million) and potion supplies (about 15,000) of the new Farming and Herblore steps inside the 1 to 2 million margin: less the shield right half (750,000), the costume room (about 1.5 million), two bought god staves (160,000) and 20,000 air runes (100,000), plus 10,000 from Bill Teach

        Quests still to do for the quest cape: Rag and Bone Man III (Chapter 3's cave horror block), While Guthix Sleeps, The Fremennik Exiles, A Night at the Theatre, The Red Reef, Dragon Slayer II, Monkey Madness II, Devious Minds, Secrets of the North, Beneath Cursed Sands, Desert Treasure II, Blood Moon Rises and Fallen From Grace. Miniquests still to do: Hopespear's Will, The Enchanted Key, Family Pest, The Frozen Door, In Search of Knowledge and Into the Tombs (Bear Your Soul and His Faithful Servants are done in this chapter).

        What Chapter 3 can assume from here: Song of the Elves done, with the eight non-boostable 70s it needs (Agility, Construction, Farming, Herblore, Hunter, Mining, Smithing and Woodcutting); Pandemonium access and 67 Sailing; the herb sack, the small essence pouch and the shield right half; the Morytania medium diary; and the costume room and both ships as storage. Crafting is at 68 or more and needs to reach 70 in Chapter 3. Coins: about 34.8 million gp (34,800,000), which already counts the Sepulchre income, about 1.2 million gp of teak plank fees, the shield right half, the costume room, the two bought god staves and the air runes.

        Where things live at the end of Chapter 2

        Verification list

        Every claim this chapter once marked as unchecked has now been checked on the wiki (13 November 2026):

        • (checked: Mahogany Homes pays 3 carpenter points a Novice contract and 4 an Adept one, and the plank sack costs 350; the wiki's averages are about 1,894 XP a Novice contract and 3,260 an Adept one, which is why the block now reaches 62 at the sack and keeps the extra contracts only as a fallback);
        • (checked: the celestial ring is Dusuri's, in the Mining Guild, for 2,000 stardust; the guild needs 60 Mining, boostable with the Rising Sun Inn's dwarven stout, which costs 3 gp);
        • (checked: tea in a porcelain cup, from oak shelves 2, gives +2 Construction; the UIM Construction guide names the Avium Savannah teaks for the log basket);
        • (checked: Gabooty in Tai Bwo Wannai buys cut opal, jade and red topaz for 60, 90 and 120 trading sticks, and uncut ones for a fifth);
        • (checked: the sack of hay is for the Morytania easy scarecrow, with a watermelon and a bronze spear);
        • (checked: swamp tar spawns in Lumbridge Swamp, and Miltog's lamp shop in Dorgesh-Kaan sells empty bullseye lanterns and has the lamp still next to it);
        • (checked: Rimmington mine has two gold rocks);
        • (checked: the Lady of the Waves sails from Cairn Isle to Port Khazard for 20 to 50 gp, a medium Karamja diary task);
        • (checked: Sir Amik Varze's subquest needs a pot of cream);
        • (checked: the Taverley Dungeon scorpion is past the dusty key gate, behind a false wall south of the two coffins);
        • (checked: praying at the Altar of Guthix in full initiate is a medium Falador diary task, and the altar is at the stone circle in Taverley, not in the dungeon);
        • (checked: the RuneScape wiki's walkthrough of In Aid of the Myreque says Aurel's crate can be filled without holding every item at once, and the Old School page doesn't say otherwise);
        • (checked: In Search of the Myreque's bridge takes 225 steel nails, 75 per plank; In Aid of the Myreque's repairs take 44; every sawmill operator sells steel nails, 1,000 in stock at 3 gp);
        • (checked: each Ectofuntus worship pays 5 ecto-tokens);
        • (checked: the salve amulet needs one salve shard from the Abandoned Mine's crystal outcrop and nothing else uses spare shards, so keep only the one);
        • (checked: the Culinaromancer's Chest sells chocolate bars and cake tins, not cakes);
        • (checked: sawmill operators don't take noted logs);
        • (checked: the Trouble Brewing minigame teleport needs 40 Cooking and Cabin Fever);
        • (checked: Hespori stores what you carry when you die in its fight, indefinitely, 25,000 gp a collection, and any unsafe death deletes it);
        • (checked: the requirement lines for the quests in 2.2 and 2.3 against the wiki's quest skill requirement table; Mourning's End Part I's 50 Thieving is boostable, which is now corrected);
        • (checked: Rellekka mine has seven coal rocks, the Lunar Diplomacy Mining top-up);
        • (checked: rune platelegs in the Aldarin windmill nest return a runite bar);
        • (checked against Chapter 1: the Reward Cart step keeps searching until you hold 22 mahogany logs, 20 for the Varrock diary and 2 for the lectern);
        • (checked: the Fletching figure of 69 fits the Sepulchre's 600,000 Fletching XP plus the earlier shafts and maple bows).
        • (checked on 14 November 2026, for the items and quests Chapter 1's audit moved here: the Rag and Bone Man I, Below Ice Mountain, Black Knights' Fortress, Troll Romance, Family Crest, Enlightened Journey and My Arm's Big Adventure quick guides; Fortunato's vinegar at 1 gp and only during the Rag and Bone Man quests; Peksa's bronze med helm at 24 gp and Wayne's iron chainbody at 210 gp; Logava Gricoller's bread at 12 gp; the Lumbridge General Store's pots, cake tin and bucket; the sled from Dunstan; insect repellent in the Catherby house north of the bank; swamp tar spawns; Dommik's ring, necklace, holy and sickle moulds; papyrus at Ali's Discount Wares; the silk trader at 3 gp; Aggie's red and yellow dyes; Sarah's empty sacks; Heskel in Falador Park; fairy rings AJR, DJR, BIP and CKS; the Nature Grotto's druid pouch and the silver sickle (b); Drezel's wolfbane; Father Urhney's ghostspeak amulet; the crystal saw seed from Brimstail; lockpicks at Martin Thwait's Lost and Found for 20 gp; rune thrownaxes at the Ranging Guild for 572 gp; the book of the dead from A Kingdom Divided, replaced by Commander Fullore);

        Three figures are estimates by design, not unchecked facts, and are labelled as such where they appear: the sand crab block's time (about 56,000 Attack XP from crabs with 60 Hitpoints), the glassblowing materials (about 50,000 gp at 5 gp a sand and soda ash and about 53 XP a piece), and the Sepulchre's fletching and rune costs, which the end figure doesn't count.
